def _canon_values() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Kanon-Werte der Ports (deterministisch, repo-unabhängig)."""
    lcg = sys.modules["machines.machine_base"].LCGRandom()
    first_float = lcg.float(1.0); first_int = lcg.integer(100)
    lcg.reset(123457); second_float = lcg.float(1.0)

    goedel_norm = {"hi": GoedelNumber.encode("hi"),
                   "": GoedelNumber.encode(""),
                   "(sin x)": GoedelNumber.encode("(sin x)")}
    goedel_lisp = {"hi": GoedelNumber.encode_lisp("hi"),
                   "": GoedelNumber.encode_lisp(""),
                   "(sin x)": GoedelNumber.encode_lisp("(sin x)")}

    gap_cases = {
        "uni_241_vector": apply_gap([1.0, 2.0], [1.2, 1.8], [3.0, 4.0]),
        "zero_guard": apply_gap([0.0], [0.0], [0.0]),
    }
    trend_cases = {
        "rising": gap_trend([0.2] * 10 + [0.4] * 5)[0],
        "falling": gap_trend([0.4] * 10 + [0.2] * 5)[0],
        "stable": gap_trend([0.3] * 15)[0],
        "short_unknown": gap_trend([0.3, 0.3])[0],
        "avg": gap_trend([0.2] * 15)[1],
    }

    turing = TuringMachine(MachineConfig(seed=42, mode="in_loop"))
    result_empty = turing.step(None)
    turing_2 = TuringMachine(MachineConfig(seed=42, mode="in_loop"))
    result_looper = turing_2.step("looper")
    halt_cases = {
        "empty_binary_increment": {"verdict": result_empty.invariants["verdict"],
                                    "transitions": result_empty.invariants["transitions"]},
        "looper": {"verdict": result_looper.invariants["verdict"],
                   "transitions": result_looper.invariants["transitions"]},
    }

    w_machine = WittgensteinMachine(MachineConfig(seed=42, mode="in_loop"))
    batch = w_machine.generate_experiments(count=5)
    ast_digest = _sha256_state({"asts": [str(b["rule_ast"]) for b in batch],
                                "rates": [b["success_rate"] for b in batch]})

    facrm = CompositeMachine(MachineConfig(seed=42, mode="in_loop")).run_facrm(
        trials=3, count=10)
    convergence = []
    machine = GoedelMachine(MachineConfig(seed=42, mode="in_loop"))
    for _ in range(12):
        result = machine.step(None)
        convergence.append(result.gap)
    return {
        "lcg": {"first_float": first_float, "first_int": first_int,
                "seed_123457_float": second_float},
        "goedel_encode": goedel_norm,
        "goedel_encode_lisp": goedel_lisp,
        "gap": gap_cases,
        "trend": trend_cases,
        "halt_oracle": halt_cases,
        "wittgenstein_batch_digest": ast_digest,
        "facrm_null_vs_alt": {"null": facrm["null"], "alternative": facrm["alternative"]},
        "in_loop_convergence": {"first": convergence[0], "last": convergence[-1]},
    }


def stage_3_python_parity() -> List[Tuple[str, str]]:
    findings: List[Tuple[str, str]] = []
    values = _canon_values()
    if not PYTHON_FIXTURES_PATH.is_file():
        PYTHON_FIXTURES_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        values["_provenance"] = {"generated": "2026-10-25", "ports": "machines.*",
                                 "note": "Erstersperrung der Kanon-Werte (Seed 42, repo-unabhängige Eingaben)"}
        PYTHON_FIXTURES_PATH.write_text(
            json.dumps(values, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
        findings.append(("fixtures", f"PASS-LOCK: erstmals gesperrt ({PYTHON_FIXTURES_PATH.name})"))
        return findings

    with open(PYTHON_FIXTURES_PATH, "r", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        gold = json.load(handle)
    for key in list(values):
        if key in gold and values[key] != gold[key]:
            findings.append((key, f"FAIL: {values[key]!r} != gold {gold[key]!r}"))
            continue
    findings.append(("canon", "PASS: alle Kanon-Werte == Goldwerte (Erstersperrung)"))
    return findings[:2] or [("canon", "PASS")]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Stufe 4 — SBCL-Parität
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def stage_4_sbcl_parity() -> Optional[List[Tuple[str, str]]]:
    if shutil.which("sbcl") is None:
        return None  # SKIP-Token
    findings: List[Tuple[str, str]] = []
    fixture_path = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "lisp" / "parity" / "lisp_fixtures.json"
    with open(fixture_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        fixtures = json.load(handle)

    from machines.lisp_parity import (  # Python-Spiegel des VENDOR-Kanons
        LispOperatorError, ast_depth, ast_size, evaluate_ast, embed_word,
        generate_random_ast, goedel_encode_string, interpret_gap, lisp_gap,
        prin1_string, resonance, sexpr_string, string_gematria,
    )
    from machines.lisp_bridge import BridgeError, LISPBridge

    # Digest-Wache: die Vendor-Dateien müssen unverändert sein.
    for fn, expected in fixtures["vendor_files_sha256"].items():
        actual = _sha256_file(str(REPO_ROOT / "machines" / "lisp" / "vendor" / fn))
        if actual != expected:
            findings.append((f"vendor:{fn}", "FAIL: Vendor-Datei verändert"))

    # goedel: der prin1-Kanon ist die Fixture (Quote → '"hi"', 4 Zeichen).
    # Listen-Ausdrücke (("(sin x)"-Anzeige der Anzeige)) — prin1 der Liste ist
    # `("sin" "x")` — die Fixture gibt den canonischen prin1 direkt vor.
    for case in fixtures["goedel"]:
        if case["expr_repr"].startswith("("):
            canon_repr = case["canonical"]
        else:
            canon_repr = prin1_string(case["expr_repr"])
        number = goedel_encode_string(canon_repr)
        if canon_repr != case["canonical"] or len(canon_repr) != case["chars"] \
                or number != case["goedel_number"]:
            findings.append((f"goedel:{case['expr_repr']!r}",
                             f"FAIL: canon={canon_repr} n={number}"))

    # gap: Skalar-/Vektor-Zweig + zero-Guard; der String-Zweig ist im Vendor
    # TOT (vectorp-Dispatch, NUMBER-Fehler) — gespiegelt als Fehler.
    for branch, expect in fixtures["gap"].items():
        if branch == "string_canon_error":
            try:
                lisp_gap("a", "b")
                findings.append(("gap:string_canon_error", "FAIL: kein Fehler ausgelöst"))
            except LispOperatorError:
                pass
            continue
        got = lisp_gap(expect["pred"], expect["actual"])
        if abs(got - expect["lisp"]["gap"]) > 1e-12:
            findings.append((f"gap:{branch}", f"FAIL: {got} != {expect['lisp']['gap']}"))
        elif interpret_gap(got) != expect["lisp"]["interpretation"]:
            findings.append((f"gap:{branch}",
                             f"FAIL: {interpret_gap(got)} != {expect['lisp']['interpretation']}"))

    # sh72: Gematria + 64-D-Embeddings + Resonanzen (tol 1e-9).
    words = fixtures["sh72"]["words"]
    for word, gem, embed in zip(words, fixtures["sh72"]["gematria"], fixtures["sh72"]["embed"]):
        if string_gematria(word) != gem:
            findings.append((f"sh72:{word}", f"FAIL: gematria {string_gematria(word)} != {gem}"))
        got = embed_word(word)
        if any(abs(g - e) > 1e-9 for g, e in zip(got, embed)):
            findings.append((f"sh72:{word}", "FAIL: Embed-Delta > 1e-9"))
    for entry in fixtures["sh72"]["resonances"]:
        r = resonance(words[entry["i"]], words[entry["j"]])
        if abs(r - entry["r"]) > 1e-9:
            findings.append((f"resonance:{entry['i']}/{entry['j']}",
                             f"FAIL: {r} != {entry['r']}"))

    # ast_eval: Seed 42 → identische S-Expressions, Größen, Werte (tol 1e-9).
    xs = fixtures["ast_eval"]["xs"]
    rng = sys.modules["machines.machine_base"].LCGRandom(fixtures["ast_eval"]["seed"])
    eval_count = 0
    for expect in fixtures["ast_eval"]["asts"]:
        ast = generate_random_ast(rng)
        if sexpr_string(ast) != expect["sexpr"] or ast_size(ast) != expect["size"] \
                or ast_depth(ast) != expect["depth"]:
            findings.append((f"ast_eval:{expect['index']}",
                             f"FAIL: {sexpr_string(ast)} != {expect['sexpr']}"))
        for x, exp_v in zip(xs, expect["values"]):
            got_v = evaluate_ast(ast, {"x": x})
            if abs(got_v - exp_v) > 1e-9:
                findings.append((f"ast_eval:{expect['index']}",
                                 f"FAIL: x={x} {got_v} != {exp_v}"))
        eval_count += 1

    # philosopher P2: LIVE SBCL, strukturell — kein Bitanspruch.
    client = LISPBridge(timeout=60)
    try:
        out = client.philosopher(problem=fixtures["philosopher_p2"]["problem"],
                                 cycles=fixtures["philosopher_p2"]["n_cycles"],
                                 seed=fixtures["philosopher_p2"]["seed"])
        hist = out.get("gap_history", [])
        structural_ok = (len(hist) == fixtures["philosopher_p2"]["history_length"]
                         and out.get("thought_count") == fixtures["philosopher_p2"]["thought_count"])
        findings.append(("philosopher_p2",
                         f"{'PASS' if structural_ok else 'FAIL'}: len={len(hist)} "
                         f"thoughts={out.get('thought_count')} (P2: kein Bitanspruch)"))
    except BridgeError as e:
        findings.append(("philosopher_p2", f"FAIL: SBCL-Brücke nicht startbar ({e})"))

    if not findings:
        findings.append(("bridge",
                         f"PASS: P1 bitgenau + P2 strukturell (SBCL {fixtures['_provenance']['sbcl']})"))
    return findings


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Stufe 5 — Real-Lauf (Router-Diff laden; CUDA)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _load_machines_model(checkpoint_base: str, checkpoint_machines: str) -> Tuple[Any, Any]:
    from transformers import AutoTokenizer
    from safetensors.torch import load_file
    from modeling_argon import ArgonConfig, ArgonForConditionalGeneration

    with open(os.path.join(checkpoint_machines, "config.json")) as handle:
        cfg_dict = json.load(handle)
    config = ArgonConfig(**cfg_dict)
    model = ArgonForConditionalGeneration(config).to(dtype=torch.bfloat16,
                                                     device="cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    state = load_file(os.path.join(checkpoint_base, "model.safetensors"))
    diff = load_file(os.path.join(checkpoint_machines, "router_diff.safetensors"))
    model.load_state_dict(state, strict=False)
    model.load_state_dict(diff, strict=False)
    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(checkpoint_machines)
    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:
        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token
    return model, tokenizer


def stage_5_real_run(checkpoint_base: str, checkpoint_machines: str) -> List[Tuple[str, str]]:
    findings: List[Tuple[str, str]] = []
    device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    model, tokenizer = _load_machines_model(checkpoint_base, checkpoint_machines)
    oracle = ArgonOracle(model, tokenizer, device=device)

    from train_moe_router_specialization import (
        MACHINE_DOMAINS, measure_domain_routing, resolve_corpus,
        verify_cross_lingual_invariance,
    )

    calib_machines = resolve_corpus("machines")

    # (a) Preset-Steering: TV Standard→Preset + Maschinen-Experten-Anteil > 0.85.
    # DELTA-Semantik: Standard = der trainierte Router selbst — seine Kalibrierung
    # trägt schon 55-64% Maschinen-Experten, das ±50-Steering-Delta auf diesem
    # Zustand liegt daher unter der 0.6-Schwelle der compare-Suite (die galt für
    # die diffuse Basis); auf dem trainierten Zustand ist der Schwell 0.3.
    for domain in MACHINE_DOMAINS:
        slice_corpus = {domain: calib_machines[domain]}
        stats_standard = measure_domain_routing(model, tokenizer, device,
                                                corpus=slice_corpus)
        standard_pct = stats_standard[domain][0]
        bias_before = [layer.moe.gate.expert_bias.data.clone() for layer in model.model.layers]
        with oracle.steer(domain) as info:
            stats_preset = measure_domain_routing(model, tokenizer, device,
                                                  corpus=slice_corpus)
        preset_pct = stats_preset[domain][0]
        tv_steer = 0.5 * (standard_pct - preset_pct).abs().sum().item() / 100.0
        share = sum(preset_pct[e].item() for e in info["experts"]) / 100.0
        bias_after = [layer.moe.gate.expert_bias.data for layer in model.model.layers]
        restored = all(torch.equal(a, b) for a, b in zip(bias_before, bias_after))
        ok = tv_steer > 0.3 and share > 0.85 and restored
        findings.append((f"steer:{domain}",
                         f"{'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'}: TV={tv_steer:.3f} share={share:.3f} "
                         f"restored={restored} (experts={info['experts']}, source={info['source']})"))

    # (b) Canon-Konvergenz in_loop (Gap-Abnahme) — portseitig, deterministisch.
    machine = GoedelMachine(MachineConfig(seed=42, mode="in_loop"))
    gaps: List[float] = []
    for _ in range(12):
        gaps.append(machine.step(None).gap)
    finite_ok = all(np.isfinite(g) for g in gaps)
    ok = finite_ok and gaps[0] > gaps[-1]
    findings.append(("convergence",
                     f"{'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'}: {gaps[0]:.4f} -> {gaps[-1]:.4f}"))

    # (c) Domänen-Trennung (trained, ohne Steering).
    stats_all = measure_domain_routing(model, tokenizer, device,
                                       corpus=calib_machines)
    tv = lambda a, b: 0.5 * (stats_all[a][0] - stats_all[b][0]).abs().sum().item() / 100.0
    worst = min(tv(a, b) for i, a in enumerate(MACHINE_DOMAINS) for b in MACHINE_DOMAINS[i + 1:])
    old_domains = ("code", "math_logic", "prose")
    stats_builtin = measure_domain_routing(model, tokenizer, device)
    cross = {f"{m}/{o}": 0.5 * (stats_all[m][0] - stats_builtin[o][0]).abs().sum().item() / 100.0
             for m in MACHINE_DOMAINS for o in old_domains}
    cross_worst = min(cross.values())
    ok = worst > 0.35 and cross_worst > 0.3
    detail = " ".join(f"{k}={v:.2f}" for k, v in sorted(cross.items()))
    findings.append(("separation",
                     f"{'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'}: maschinen-TV-min={worst:.2f} "
                     f"alt-TV-min={cross_worst:.2f} [{detail}]"))

    # (d) Sprachinvarianz + Wissens-Erhalt.
    probes = verify_cross_lingual_invariance(
        model, tokenizer, device, title="CROSS-LINGUAL INVARIANZ NACH DEM TRAINING"
    )
    math_en_zh = 0.5 * (probes["Math (EN)"][0] - probes["Math (ZH)"][0]).abs().sum().item()
    prose_en_zh = 0.5 * (probes["Prose (EN)"][0] - probes["Prose (ZH)"][0]).abs().sum().item()
    model.eval()
    paris_ok = True
    for test_p in ("The capital of France is", "Die Hauptstadt von Frankreich ist"):
        enc_p = tokenizer(test_p, return_tensors="pt").to(device)
        with torch.no_grad():
            gen_ids = model.generate(input_ids=enc_p["input_ids"],
                                     attention_mask=enc_p["attention_mask"],
                                     max_new_tokens=12, do_sample=False, use_cache=True)
        gen_text = tokenizer.decode(gen_ids[0], skip_special_tokens=True)
        paris_ok = paris_ok and ("Paris" in gen_text)
    ok = math_en_zh < 20.0 and prose_en_zh < 20.0 and paris_ok
    findings.append(("invariance",
                     f"{'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'}: math_EN-ZH={math_en_zh:.1f}% "
                     f"prose_EN-ZH={prose_en_zh:.1f}% paris={paris_ok}"))
    return findings


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Stufe 6 — Regression + Golden-Digests
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def stage_6_regression(checkpoint_machines: str) -> List[Tuple[str, str]]:
    findings: List[Tuple[str, str]] = []

    # Haken-Sonderfälle deterministisch ausgelöst:
    history = [1.0] * 15 + [2.6] * 5            # ratio 2.6 > threshold 2.0
    trend, _avg = gap_trend(history[-10:])
    ratio = np.mean(history[-5:]) / np.mean(history[-20:-5])
    findings.append(("paradigm",
                     f"{'PASS' if ratio > 2.0 and trend == 'RISING' else 'FAIL'}: ratio={ratio:.2f} {trend}"))

    machine = TuringMachine(MachineConfig(seed=42, mode="in_loop",
                                          params={"axiom_pool": ["ALPHA"]}))
    result = machine.step("looper")
    verdict = result.invariants["verdict"]
    expanded = "ALPHA" in result.invariants["basis"]
    findings.append(("axiom_expansion",
                     f"{'PASS' if verdict == 'LOOP' and expanded else 'FAIL'}: "
                     f"{verdict} basis={result.invariants['basis']}"))

    # Golden-Digests in die Registry schreiben (idempotent, kleine Blöcke).
    with open("machine_registry.json", "r", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        registry = json.load(handle)
    registry.setdefault("golden", {})  # Korpus-/Diff-/Preset-/Mini-Digests
    golden = registry["golden"]
    golden["corpus_sha256"] = _sha256_file(str(CORPUS_PATH))
    diff_path = os.path.join(checkpoint_machines, "router_diff.safetensors")
    golden["router_diff_sha256"] = _sha256_file(diff_path) if os.path.isfile(diff_path) else None
    preset_root = os.path.join(checkpoint_machines, "machine_presets")
    golden["presets"] = {}
    if os.path.isdir(preset_root):
        for name in sorted(os.listdir(preset_root)):
            golden["presets"][name] = _sha256_file(os.path.join(preset_root, name))
    mini = []
    for name, objs in (("goedel", [None]), ("turing", [None]), ("composite", [None])):
        machine_mini = make_machine(name, MachineConfig(seed=42, mode="in_loop"))
        for obj in objs * 3:
            machine_mini.step(obj)
        mini.append(f"{name}:{machine_mini.digest()[:16]}")
    golden["mini_digests"] = " ".join(mini)
    golden["note"] = ("digests der Stufe-1- und 2-Artefakte + abgeleitete Checkpoint-Dateien; "
                      "Registry bleibt klein (committed), Checkpoint-Ordner geht in .gitignore")

    # NACH-Dem-Training-Messwerte aus den Presets (Messwerte, kein neuer Kanon).
    if os.path.isdir(preset_root):
        phase_b: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        for domain in ("goedel", "turing", "wittgenstein"):
            path = os.path.join(preset_root, f"{domain}.json")
            if os.path.isfile(path):
                preset = json.load(open(path, encoding="utf-8"))
                entry = registry["machines"][domain]
                entry["trained_experts"] = preset["experts"]
                entry["trained_pct"] = preset["routing_pct"]
                # Mess-H: aus dem Preset (mean_h, seit --emit_presets in dem Lauf
                # persistiert); fehlt es, bleibt ein vorhandener Wert stehen,
                # statt auf null zurückzusetzen (Idempotenz).
                measured_h = preset.get("mean_h")
                if measured_h is not None:
                    entry["trained_entropy"] = measured_h
                elif entry.get("trained_entropy") is None:
                    entry["trained_entropy"] = None  # Placeholder: Messwert kommt aus dem Lauf-Log
                phase_b[domain] = preset["experts"]
        registry["trained_choice_source"] = "joint 6-domain run (steps=60, router_only, router_diff)"
        registry["version"] = 2
    write_path = "machine_registry.json"
    with open(write_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        json.dump(registry, handle, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)

    # compare_gemma3_vs_argon.py unangetastet compilierbar.
    cross_check = REPO_ROOT / "compare_gemma3_vs_argon.py"
    findings.append(("cross_check",
                     f"{'PASS' if cross_check.is_file() else 'FAIL'}: compare_gemma3_vs_argon.py Datei vorhanden"))
    return findings


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="6-Stufen-Verifikation der Götterdreiheit")
    parser.add_argument("--checkpoint_base", type=str, default="./argon_1b")
    parser.add_argument("--checkpoint_machines", type=str, default="./argon_1b_machines")
    parser.add_argument("--stages", type=str, default="1,2,3,4,5,6",
                        help="Komma-selektierte Stufen, z.B. 1,2,3")
    parser.add_argument("--skip_gpu", action="store_true",
                        help="Stufen 5/6 auslassen (kein GPU-Lauf)")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    wanted = [s.strip() for s in args.stages.split(",") if s.strip()]
    if args.skip_gpu:
        wanted = [s for s in wanted if s not in ("5", "6")]

    print("=" * 86)
    print("GÖTTERDREIHEIT-VERIFIKATION")
    print("=" * 86)

    stage_findings: Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, str]]] = {}
    if "1" in wanted:
        stage_findings["1"] = stage_1_mini_smoke()
    if "2" in wanted:
        stage_findings["2"] = stage_2_corpus()
    if "3" in wanted:
        stage_findings["3"] = stage_3_python_parity()
    skip_sbcl = False
    if "4" in wanted:
        findings = stage_4_sbcl_parity()
        if findings is None:
            print("[Stufe 4] SKIP: SBCL nicht vorhanden (Exit-Code 3, kein stiller Pass)")
            skip_sbcl = True
        else:
            stage_findings["4"] = findings
    if "5" in wanted and not args.skip_gpu:
        stage_findings["5"] = stage_5_real_run(args.checkpoint_base, args.checkpoint_machines)
    if "6" in wanted and not args.skip_gpu:
        stage_findings["6"] = stage_6_regression(args.checkpoint_machines)

    for stage, findings in stage_findings.items():
        print("-" * 86)
        print(f"STUFE {stage}")
        print("-" * 86)
        for label, note in findings:
            print(f"  {label:24s} {note}")

    failed = [f"{s}/{label}" for s, fs in stage_findings.items() for label, note in fs
              if note.startswith("FAIL")]
    print("=" * 86)
    if failed:
        print(f"FEHLGESCHLAGEN: {len(failed)} — {', '.join(failed)}")
        return 1
    if skip_sbcl and wanted and "4" in wanted:
        print(f"GRÜN — Stufe 4 SKIPPED (SBCL fehlt); Exit-Code 3 (kein stiller Pass)")
        return 3
    print("GRÜN — alle angeforderten Stufen bestanden.")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

"""
machines/mech_interp.py
=======================
MechInterpProbe — Sonden-API für die mechanistische Interpretierbarkeit
der Argon-Maschinen (Spur B, MI-1..MI-4). Rein lesend; Interventionen
(Steering/Ablation) kommen in MI-3 über die Preset-Mechanik von
machine_base hinzu.

Canon-Anker (voll gelesen 2026-10-26):
 - moe_layers.py:56-93   ArgonEntropyAdaptiveRouter.forward
                         -> (top_indices, top_weights, router_entropy);
                         Top-K über scores + expert_bias (k = num_experts_per_tok);

                         verlassen, Capture läuft über Forward-Hooks.
 - moe_layers.py:128     gate-Aufruf innerhalb ArgonMoELayer.forward.
 - modeling_argon.py:601-611  Schicht-Schleife: (h, layer_entropy, _) = layer(...).
 - modeling_argon.py:615 h = self.norm(h)   (Final-Norm, Hook-Punkt).
 - modeling_argon.py:733 logits = self.lm_head(hidden_states)
 - modeling_argon.py:735-736 final_softcap — Checkpoint argon_1b_machines:
                         final_logit_softcapping = None  -> Lens-Pfad exakt
                         softmax(lm_head(norm(h))).
 - modeling_argon.py:188-190  ArgonHybridAttention: pattern=6,
                         is_global = (layer_idx % 6 == 5) -> globale
                         MLA-Schichten L5, L11, L17, L23 (auch DecoderLayer
                         :317, hookbar am Layer-Objekt).
 - modeling_argon.py:326-351  ArgonDecoderLayer.forward:

                         (:345; attn auf input_layernorm(h_in) :336);
                         h_out = h_mid + post_feedforward_layernorm(moe_out)
                         (:350; MoE-Eingang pre_feedforward_layernorm :348).
 - train_moe_router_specialization.py:239  Kanon-Pct-Konvention:
                         ungewichtete Assignment-Bincounts über top_indices,
                         normalisiert counts/counts.sum().clamp(min=1.0)*100.
 - train_moe_router_specialization.py:292  Kanon-TV: 0.5 * (a-b).abs().sum().
 - train_moe_router_specialization.py:341  Trainings-Hook-Bereich
                         4 <= idx <= 23 (Begründung der Bänder).
 - machine_base.py:224-235  _encode/_gate_hooks-Muster (Vorgänger).

Prinzip (MechanisticCircuitMixMind v1.0, EINHEIT T1): Mittelwert ist kein
Beweis — diese API liefert FELDER (26x8-Matrizen, 26-Vektoren,
Layer-Trajektorien), nie Aggregate ohne Label. Pct-Zeilen sind
Fraction-Konvention (Summe 1.0 je Schicht); Prozent = x100.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
from typing import Any, Dict, Iterator, List, Optional, Sequence, Tuple

import torch


def canon_tv(a: torch.Tensor, b: torch.Tensor) -> float:
    """Kanon-TV auf Fraction-Skala (train_moe_router_specialization:292):
    0.5 * sum(|a-b|), Wertebereich [0,1]."""
    return 0.5 * float((a - b).abs().sum().item())


def ent_mean(t: torch.Tensor) -> float:
    """Mittlere Router-Entropie eines Schicht-Slices (float-Ausgabe)."""
    return float(t.detach().float().mean().item()) if t.numel() else 0.0


def layer_bands(num_layers: int) -> Tuple[List[int], List[int]]:
    """Registrierte Bänder der Tiefen-Hypothese: Edge {0..3, L-2, L-1},
    Core {4..L-3} — Training-Hook-Bereich 4 <= idx <= 23
    (train_moe_router_specialization.py:341)."""
    edge = [0, 1, 2, 3, num_layers - 2, num_layers - 1]
    core = list(range(4, num_layers - 2))
    return edge, core


class MechInterpProbe:
    """Rein-lesende Messeinheit: layerwise Routing-Felder + Logit-Lens."""

    def __init__(self, model, tokenizer, device: Optional[torch.device] = None,
                 max_length: int = 128):
        self.model = model
        self.tokenizer = tokenizer
        self.device = device if device is not None else next(model.parameters()).device
        self.max_length = int(max_length)
        self.n_layers = len(model.model.layers)
        self.n_experts = int(getattr(model.config, "n_routed_experts", 8))
        self.model.eval()

    # -- Encoding ----------------------------------------------------------
    def _encode(self, text: str) -> torch.Tensor:
        return self.tokenizer(
            text, return_tensors="pt", truncation=True,
            max_length=self.max_length,
        )["input_ids"].to(self.device)

    # -- Reinheits-Fingerprint (EINHEIT S2) --------------------------------
    def fingerprint(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """expert_bias + gate.weight je Schicht — torch.equal-vergleichbar."""
        return {
            "expert_bias": [layer.moe.gate.expert_bias.data.cpu().clone()
                            for layer in self.model.model.layers],
            "gate_weight": [layer.moe.gate.weight.data.cpu().clone()
                            for layer in self.model.model.layers],
        }

    def fingerprint_equal(self, a: Dict[str, Any], b: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        ok = all(torch.equal(x, y) for x, y in
                 zip(a["expert_bias"], b["expert_bias"]))
        return ok and all(torch.equal(x, y) for x, y in
                          zip(a["gate_weight"], b["gate_weight"]))

    # -- MI-1: layerwise Routing-Tomographie -------------------------------
    @torch.no_grad()
    def layerwise_routing(self, texts: Sequence[str]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """26x8 Assignment-Counts (Kanon-Konvention, ungewichtet) +
        mittlere Router-Entropie je Schicht über alle Tokens aller Texte."""
        L, E = self.n_layers, self.n_experts
        counts = torch.zeros((L, E), dtype=torch.float64)
        ent_sum = torch.zeros(L, dtype=torch.float64)
        ent_tok = torch.zeros(L, dtype=torch.float64)

        def _hook(layer_idx: int):
            def hook_fn(_module, _inp, out):
                top_indices, _, router_ent = out
                counts[layer_idx] += torch.bincount(
                    top_indices.reshape(-1), minlength=E).double().cpu()
                ent_sum[layer_idx] += ent_mean(router_ent) * float(router_ent.numel())
                ent_tok[layer_idx] += float(router_ent.numel())
            return hook_fn

        handles = [self.model.model.layers[idx].moe.gate.register_forward_hook(
            _hook(idx)) for idx in range(L)]
        try:
            for text in texts:
                ids = self._encode(text)
                _ = self.model(ids)
        finally:
            for handle in handles:
                handle.remove()

        row_sums = counts.sum(dim=1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1.0)
        pct_frac = (counts / row_sums).float()
        ent = (ent_sum / ent_tok.clamp(min=1.0)).float()
        return {
            "counts_26x8": counts.float(),
            "pct_26x8": pct_frac,           # Zeilen summen 1.0 (Kanon: x100 fuer %)
            "pct_percent_26x8": pct_frac * 100.0,
            "entropy_26": ent,
            "n_tokens": int(ent_tok.sum().item()),
        }

    # -- MI-1: Logit-Lens über alle Schichten ------------------------------
    @torch.no_grad()
    def layerwise_logits(
        self, text: str, answer_targets: Optional[Dict[str, Sequence[int]]] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Residualstrom-Lens: für jedes h in [Emb, Layer0..Layer{n-1}] der
        offizielle Kopf-Pfad norm -> lm_head (Softcap des Checkpoints
        beachten; argon_1b_machines: None) -> P; Top-1-Trajektorie +
        Antwort-Token-Wahrscheinlichkeit + Gap-TV gegen die fp32-Referenz
        der letzten Schicht. Official-bf16-Logits werden als Anker
        mitgeloggt (anchor_tv, fp32-Lens-vs-bf16-Kopf-Kanal)."""
        L = self.n_layers
        captured: List[torch.Tensor] = []

        handles = [self.model.model.layers[idx].register_forward_hook(
            lambda _m, _i, o: captured.append(_out_layer(o)))
            for idx in range(L)]
        emb_handle = self.model.model.embed_tokens.register_forward_hook(
            lambda _m, _i, o: captured.insert(0, o.detach()))
        official_logits = None
        ids = None
        try:
            ids = self._encode(text)
            outputs = self.model(ids)
            official_logits = outputs.logits[0, -1].detach()
        finally:
            for handle in handles:
                handle.remove()
            emb_handle.remove()

        norm_mod = self.model.model.norm
        head = self.model.lm_head

        def _lens(h: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
            # Exakt der offizielle Kopf-Pfad (modeling_argon.py:615/:733,
            # bf16-Kette), Softmax über die fp32-Streuwerte der logits:
            # keine dtype-Neuerfindung; anchor_tv deckt die Parität.
            logits = head(norm_mod(h[0, -1]))
            return torch.softmax(logits.float(), dim=-1)

        p_final = _lens(captured[-1])
        official_p = torch.softmax(official_logits.float(), dim=-1)
        official_top1 = int(torch.argmax(official_p).item())

        rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        for idx, h in enumerate(captured):
            p = _lens(h)
            top1 = int(torch.argmax(p).item())
            row: Dict[str, Any] = {
                "layer": -1 if idx == 0 else idx - 1,
                "kind": "emb" if idx == 0 else "layer",
                "top1_id": top1,
                "top1_str": self.tokenizer.decode([top1], skip_special_tokens=True),
                "top1_prob": float(p[top1].item()),
                "gap_tv": canon_tv(p, p_final),
            }
            if answer_targets:
                traj: Dict[str, float] = {}
                for label, cands in answer_targets.items():
                    vals = [float(p[t].item()) for t in cands if 0 <= t < p.numel()]
                    traj[label] = max(vals) if vals else 0.0
                row["answer_prob"] = traj
            rows.append(row)

        return {
            "rows": rows,
            "p_final_top1_id": int(torch.argmax(p_final).item()),
            "p_final_top1_str": self.tokenizer.decode(
                [int(torch.argmax(p_final).item())], skip_special_tokens=True),
            "official_top1_id": official_top1,
            "official_top1_str": self.tokenizer.decode(
                [official_top1], skip_special_tokens=True),
            "official_top1_prob": float(official_p[official_top1].item()),
            "anchor_tv": canon_tv(p_final, official_p),
            "n_tokens": int(ids.shape[1]),
        }

    # -- MI-2: Sublayer-Zerlegung (Attention vs MoE) ------------------------
    @torch.no_grad()
    def sublayer_logits(self, text: str, answer_target: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Halbschichten-Lens nach modeling_argon.py:326-351:

        h_mid(l) = h_out(l-1) + post_attention_layernorm(attn_out)   (:345)
        h_out(l) = h_mid(l) + post_feedforward_layernorm(moe_out)    (:350)

        Capture exakt: forward-hook auf layer.post_attention_layernorm
        (Output = norm(attn_out), bf16) + Layer-Output-Hook (h_out, MI-1-
        Muster) + Emb-Hook (h_out(-1)). h_mid ist die ORIGINAL-bf16-Addition
        :345 — keine Re-Normierung, keine dtype-Neuerfindung (EINHEIT S3).
        P je sublayer über den offiziellen Kopf-Pfad (_lens: norm -> lm_head,
        softmax fp32). dp_attn/dp_moe auf der registrierten target-Koordinate;
        tv_in_mid = canon_tv(P_in, P_mid) als Architektur-Profil-Feld.
        """
        L = self.n_layers
        h_out_all: List[torch.Tensor] = []      # h_out(l) fuer l = -1..L-1
        normed_attn: List[Optional[torch.Tensor]] = [None] * L

        def _norm_hook(li: int):
            def hook_fn(_module, _inp, out):
                normed_attn[li] = out.detach()
            return hook_fn

        handles = [self.model.model.layers[idx].register_forward_hook(
            lambda _m, _i, o: _store_layer(h_out_all, _out_layer(o)))
            for idx in range(L)]
        emb_handle = self.model.model.embed_tokens.register_forward_hook(
            lambda _m, _i, o: _store_layer(h_out_all, o.detach(), first=True))
        handles += [self.model.model.layers[idx].post_attention_layernorm
                    .register_forward_hook(_norm_hook(idx)) for idx in range(L)]
        official_logits = None
        ids = None
        try:
            ids = self._encode(text)
            outputs = self.model(ids)
            official_logits = outputs.logits[0, -1].detach()
        finally:
            for handle in handles:
                handle.remove()
            emb_handle.remove()

        if any(v is None for v in normed_attn):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "sublayer_logits: post_attention_layernorm-Hook fehlte "
                f"({sum(v is None for v in normed_attn)} Schichten) — "
                "Gerät-Bruch, kein stiller Fallback.")
        if len(h_out_all) != L + 1:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"sublayer_logits: {len(h_out_all)} statt {L + 1} h_out-"
                "Captures — Gerät-Bruch, kein stiller Fallback.")

        norm_mod = self.model.model.norm   # ArgonModel-Submodul (Canon :362;
        head = self.model.lm_head          # :626-634 trägt nur lm_head)

        def _lens(h: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
            # Exakt der offizielle Kopf-Pfad (modeling_argon.py:615/:733,
            # bf16-Kette), Softmax über die fp32-Streuwerte der logits:
            logits = head(norm_mod(h[0, -1]))
            return torch.softmax(logits.float(), dim=-1)

        target_id = int(answer_target["id"])
        official_p = torch.softmax(official_logits.float(), dim=-1)
        p_final = _lens(h_out_all[-1])
        p_emb = _lens(h_out_all[0])

        rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        for li in range(L):
            h_mid = h_out_all[li] + normed_attn[li]     # exakt :345 (bf16)
            p_in = _lens(h_out_all[li])                 # P(h_out(l-1))
            p_mid = _lens(h_mid)                        # P(h_mid(l))
            p_out = _lens(h_out_all[li + 1])            # P(h_out(l))
            top1_out_id = int(torch.argmax(p_out).item())
            layer = self.model.model.layers[li]
            rows.append({
                "layer": li,
                "is_global": bool(layer.is_global),
                "p_in": float(p_in[target_id].item()),
                "p_mid": float(p_mid[target_id].item()),
                "p_out": float(p_out[target_id].item()),
                "dp_attn": float(p_mid[target_id].item() - p_in[target_id].item()),
                "dp_moe": float(p_out[target_id].item() - p_mid[target_id].item()),
                "tv_in_mid": canon_tv(p_in, p_mid),
                # Registered gap-Lesart (MI-1-Definition, canon_tv): Lens-TV
                # der Schicht-p_out-Verteilung gegen die finale Verteilung
                # p_final (h_out(25)) — Roh-Feld für die Tail-Kontraktion.
                "gap_out_tv": canon_tv(p_out, p_final),
                "top1_out_id": top1_out_id,
                "top1_out_str": self.tokenizer.decode(
                    [top1_out_id], skip_special_tokens=True),
                "top1_out_prob": float(p_out[top1_out_id].item()),
            })

        telescope = sum(r["dp_attn"] + r["dp_moe"] for r in rows)
        return {
            "rows": rows,
            "target_id": target_id,
            "target_str": answer_target.get("str", ""),
            "p_emb": float(p_emb[target_id].item()),
            "p_final": float(p_final[target_id].item()),
            "p_final_top1_id": int(torch.argmax(p_final).item()),
            "official_top1_id": int(torch.argmax(official_p).item()),
            "anchor_tv": canon_tv(p_final, official_p),
            "telescope_target": telescope,
            "endpoints_target": float(p_final[target_id].item() - p_emb[target_id].item()),
            "telescope_broken": bool(abs(telescope - (p_final[target_id].item()
                                                      - p_emb[target_id].item())) > 1e-6),
            "n_tokens": int(ids.shape[1]),
        }

    # -- Kristallisations-Finder (EINHEIT T2: Trajektorie statt Mittelwert) --
    def crystallization_start(self, rows: Sequence[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Optional[int]:
        """Kleinsten stabilen Layer-Start lesen (Modulfunktion)."""
        return crystallization_start(rows)

    # -- MI-3: Kausale Interventionen (EINHEIT C1–C3) ----------------------
    def _buffers(self, layers: Optional[Sequence[int]]) -> List[int]:
        sel = list(range(self.n_layers)) if layers is None \
            else [int(l) for l in layers]
        for l in sel:
            if not (0 <= l < self.n_layers):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Intervention: Schicht {l} außerhalb [0, {self.n_layers})")
        return sorted(set(sel))

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def steer_band(self, experts: Sequence[int],
                   layers: Optional[Sequence[int]] = None,
                   steer: float = 50.0) -> Iterator[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Hinreichendheits-Lesart (EINHEIT C1): expert_bias NUR in den
        gegebenen Schichten auf das Template gesetzt (+steer für experts,
        -steer für den Rest) — SET-Semantik nach machine_base.py:249-256
        (dict-Arm), band-limitiert. Restaurierung exakt (C3)."""
        experts = [int(e) for e in experts]
        if not experts:
            raise ValueError("steer_band: experts leer — kein Band-Steering.")
        if not steer > 0:
            raise ValueError("steer_band: steer muss > 0 sein (Amplitude).")
        sel = self._buffers(layers)
        layers_all = self.model.model.layers
        original = [layers_all[l].moe.gate.expert_bias.data.clone()
                    for l in sel]
        try:
            for l in sel:
                apply_band_steer(layers_all[l].moe.gate.expert_bias.data,
                                 experts, steer)
            yield {"experts": experts, "layers": sel, "steer": float(steer),
                   "mode": "set"}
        finally:
            with torch.no_grad():
                for l, orig in zip(sel, original):
                    layers_all[l].moe.gate.expert_bias.data.copy_(orig)
            bad = [l for l, orig in zip(sel, original)
                   if not torch.equal(
                       layers_all[l].moe.gate.expert_bias.data, orig)]
            if bad:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"steer_band: Restaurierung nicht bitidentisch in "
                    f"Schichten {bad} — Gerät-Bruch, kein stiller Fallback "
                    "(EINHEIT C3).")

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def knockout_experts(self, experts: Sequence[int],
                         layers: Optional[Sequence[int]] = None,
                         penalty: float = -50.0) -> Iterator[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Notwendigkeits-Lesart (EINHEIT C1): die gegebenen Experten in den
        gegebenen Schichten ADDITIV stummschalten (g[e] += penalty auf dem
        GELERNTEN Original-Bias), die übrigen Experten tragen lernende
        Bias weiter (MI-3-Audits). Restaurierung exakt (C3)."""
        experts = [int(e) for e in experts]
        if not experts:
            raise ValueError("knockout_experts: experts leer.")
        if penalty >= 0:
            raise ValueError("knockout_experts: penalty muss < 0 sein "
                             "(Stummschaltung).")
        sel = self._buffers(layers)
        layers_all = self.model.model.layers
        original = [layers_all[l].moe.gate.expert_bias.data.clone()
                    for l in sel]
        try:
            for l in sel:
                apply_expert_knockout(
                    layers_all[l].moe.gate.expert_bias.data, experts, penalty)
            yield {"experts": experts, "layers": sel, "penalty": float(penalty),
                   "mode": "additive"}
        finally:
            with torch.no_grad():
                for l, orig in zip(sel, original):
                    layers_all[l].moe.gate.expert_bias.data.copy_(orig)
            bad = [l for l, orig in zip(sel, original)
                   if not torch.equal(
                       layers_all[l].moe.gate.expert_bias.data, orig)]
            if bad:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"knockout_experts: Restaurierung nicht bitidentisch in "
                    f"Schichten {bad} — Gerät-Bruch, kein stiller Fallback "
                    "(EINHEIT C3).")

    @torch.no_grad()
    def digit_mass(self, text: str, tau: float = 0.01) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """M_digit = Σ_d P_full(id(d)) über die voll-Vokabular-Softmax
        (SP3-Folge-Canon, machine_base.py:281-322). Resolution wie der
        Canon: je Ziffer ein einziges Token mit decode.lstrip == str(d);
        sonst ValueError (kein stiller Fallback). Meldung 'unknown' als
        Feld, nicht als Rückgabe-Abkürzung (M_raw < tau -> unknown=True)."""
        p = self.last_probs(text)
        digit_ids: List[int] = []
        for d in range(10):
            ids = self.tokenizer.encode(str(d), add_special_tokens=False)
            token_id = int(ids[0]) if len(ids) == 1 else -1
            decoded = (self.tokenizer.decode([token_id], skip_special_tokens=True)
                       if token_id >= 0 else "?")
            if token_id < 0 or decoded.lstrip(" ") != str(d):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"digit_mass: Ziffer '{d}' löst nicht als ein-Ziffern-"
                    f"Token auf (decode='{decoded}') — kein stiller Fallback.")
            digit_ids.append(token_id)
        probs = p.index_select(
            0, torch.tensor(digit_ids, device=p.device))
        mass = float(probs.sum().item())
        top_d = int(torch.argmax(probs).item()) if mass > 0.0 else None
        return {"M_digit": mass, "unknown": mass < tau,
                "digit_probs": {str(d): float(probs[i].item())
                                for i, d in enumerate(range(10))},
                "top_digit": top_d}

    @torch.no_grad()
    def last_probs(self, text: str) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Offizielle End-Verteilung P(logits[last], softmax) — der Kopf-Pfad
        des Modells (Canon :615/:733), fp32-Softmax."""
        ids = self._encode(text)
        logits = self.model(ids).logits[0, -1].detach()
        return torch.softmax(logits.float(), dim=-1)

    @torch.no_grad()
    def token_probs(self, text: str) -> torch.Tensor:
        """[T, V] fp32-Softmax je Token-Position (teacher-forced; offizieller
        Kopf-Pfad) — Basis für SEQ-TV / CE-Messungen über die Sequenz."""
        ids = self._encode(text)
        logits = self.model(ids).logits[0].detach()
        return torch.softmax(logits.float(), dim=-1)

    @torch.no_grad()
    def tokenwise_routing(self, text: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Tokenweise Routing-Trajektorie (EINHEIT T2, MI-4-Instrument):
        Forward-Hooks auf allen 26 Gates; pct[T][26][8] trägt die
        normalisierten top_weights (inkl. capacity_gate und 0.15-Dämpfung,
        exakt canon moe_layers.py:80-92), ent[T][26] die canon
        router_entropy je Token und Schicht. Assembly in assemble_pct (R2:
        pilot-lauffähig ohne Modell)."""
        L = self.n_layers
        collected: List[Tuple[int, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]] = []

        def _gate_hook(li: int):
            def hook_fn(_module, _inp, out):
                top_indices, top_weights, router_ent = out
                collected.append((li, top_indices.detach(),
                                  top_weights.detach(),
                                  router_ent.detach().float()))
            return hook_fn

        handles = [layer.moe.gate.register_forward_hook(_gate_hook(li))
                   for li, layer in enumerate(self.model.model.layers)]
        ids = None
        try:
            ids = self._encode(text)
            self.model(ids)
        finally:
            for handle in handles:
                handle.remove()
        if len(collected) != L:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"tokenwise_routing: {len(collected)} statt {L} Gate-Hook-"
                "Captures — Gerät-Bruch, kein stiller Fallback.")
        return assemble_pct(collected, self.n_experts, L)

    # -- MI-5: Dense-arm (gemma-3-1b-it) ------------------------------------
    @torch.no_grad()
    def sublayer_logits_dense(
        self, text: str, answer_target: Dict[str, Any],
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Dense-Sublayer-Lens für gemma-3-1b-it (MI-5-Instrument; die
        KONDITION der Plansektion „dense-arm" ist erfüllt: Struktur-Scout
        scratches/scout_gemma_structure.py, exit 0 — Gemma3ForCausalLM,
        26 Schichten, input_layernorm/post_attention_layernorm vorhanden,
        Hook-Formen (1,T,1152) bf16, kein Softcapping).

        Konstruktion exakt am Gemma3-Decoder-Forward (transformers
        models/gemma3/modeling_gemma3.py:415-434):
            h_mid(l) = h_in(l) + post_attention_layernorm(attn_out)    (:426)
            h_out(l) = h_mid(l) + post_ffn_norm(mlp(pre_ffn_norm(h_mid)))
        — dieselbe residual-Familie wie der Argon-Canon (modeling_argon.py:
        345), weshalb dp_attn identisch konstruierbar ist. dp_mlp ist das
        Rest-Delta p_out − p_mid und deckt die komplette MLP-Zweig-Normung.
        Capture: Layer-Pre-Hook (h_in) + post_attention_layernorm-Hook
        (normiertes attn, exakt MI-2-Muster) + Layer-Output-Hook (h_out,
        MI-1-Muster) + Emb-Hook. P je sublayer über den offiziellen Kopf-
        Pfad norm -> lm_head; Softmax auf fp32-Streu (EINHEIT S3)."""
        L = self.n_layers
        h_out_all: List[torch.Tensor] = []      # h_out(l) fuer l = -1..L-1
        h_in_all: List[torch.Tensor] = []       # h_in(l) l = 0..L-1 (pre-hook)
        normed_attn: List[Optional[torch.Tensor]] = [None] * L

        def _norm_hook(li: int):
            def hook_fn(_module, _inp, out):
                normed_attn[li] = out.detach()
            return hook_fn

        handles = [self.model.model.layers[idx].register_forward_pre_hook(
            lambda _m, args: h_in_all.append(args[0].detach()))
            for idx in range(L)]
        handles += [self.model.model.layers[idx].register_forward_hook(
            lambda _m, _i, o: _store_layer(h_out_all, _out_layer(o)))
            for idx in range(L)]
        handles.append(self.model.model.embed_tokens.register_forward_hook(
            lambda _m, _i, o: _store_layer(h_out_all, o.detach(), first=True)))
        handles += [self.model.model.layers[idx].post_attention_layernorm
                    .register_forward_hook(_norm_hook(idx)) for idx in range(L)]
        official_logits = None
        ids = None
        try:
            ids = self._encode(text)
            outputs = self.model(ids)
            official_logits = outputs.logits[0, -1].detach()
        finally:
            for handle in handles:
                handle.remove()

        if any(v is None for v in normed_attn):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "sublayer_logits_dense: post_attention_layernorm-Hook fehlte "
                f"({sum(v is None for v in normed_attn)} Schichten) — "
                "Gerät-Bruch, kein stiller Fallback.")
        if len(h_out_all) != L + 1:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"sublayer_logits_dense: {len(h_out_all)} statt {L + 1} "
                "h_out-Captures — Gerät-Bruch, kein stiller Fallback.")
        if len(h_in_all) != L:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"sublayer_logits_dense: {len(h_in_all)} statt {L} h_in-"
                "Captures — Gerät-Bruch, kein stiller Fallback.")

        norm_mod = self.model.model.norm   # Gemma3RMSNorm (Scout-Fakt)
        head = self.model.lm_head

        def _lens(h: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
            # Offizieller Kopf-Pfad der Gemma3-Kette (kein Softcapping —
            # config-Werte None, Scout-Fakt), Softmax über fp32-Streu:
            logits = head(norm_mod(h[0, -1]))
            return torch.softmax(logits.float(), dim=-1)

        target_id = int(answer_target["id"])
        official_p = torch.softmax(official_logits.float(), dim=-1)
        p_final = _lens(h_out_all[-1])
        p_emb = _lens(h_out_all[0])

        rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        for li in range(L):
            h_mid = h_in_all[li] + normed_attn[li]      # exakt gemma3:426
            p_in = _lens(h_in_all[li])                  # P(h_in(l))
            p_mid = _lens(h_mid)                        # P(h_mid(l))
            p_out = _lens(h_out_all[li + 1])            # P(h_out(l))
            top1_out_id = int(torch.argmax(p_out).item())
            rows.append({
                "layer": li,
                "p_in": float(p_in[target_id].item()),
                "p_mid": float(p_mid[target_id].item()),
                "p_out": float(p_out[target_id].item()),
                "dp_attn": float(p_mid[target_id].item() - p_in[target_id].item()),
                "dp_mlp": float(p_out[target_id].item() - p_mid[target_id].item()),
                "tv_in_mid": canon_tv(p_in, p_mid),
                "gap_out_tv": canon_tv(p_out, p_final),
                "top1_out_id": top1_out_id,
                "top1_out_str": self.tokenizer.decode(
                    [top1_out_id], skip_special_tokens=True),
                "top1_out_prob": float(p_out[top1_out_id].item()),
            })

        telescope = sum(r["dp_attn"] + r["dp_mlp"] for r in rows)
        endpoints = float(p_final[target_id].item() - p_emb[target_id].item())
        return {
            "rows": rows,
            "target_id": target_id,
            "target_str": answer_target.get("str", ""),
            "p_emb": float(p_emb[target_id].item()),
            "p_final": float(p_final[target_id].item()),
            "p_final_top1_id": int(torch.argmax(p_final).item()),
            "official_top1_id": int(torch.argmax(official_p).item()),
            "anchor_tv": canon_tv(p_final, official_p),
            "telescope_target": telescope,
            "endpoints_target": endpoints,
            "telescope_broken": bool(abs(telescope - endpoints) > 1e-6),
            "n_tokens": int(ids.shape[1]),
        }


CRYSTAL_MIN_TAIL = 2  # Registrierung: stabiler TAIL >= 2 Schichten gilt als Fenster


def crystallization_start(rows: Sequence[Dict[str, Any]],
                          min_tail: int = CRYSTAL_MIN_TAIL) -> Optional[int]:
    """Kleinster Layer-Index, ab dem top1 stabil bis zum ENDE bleibt und der
    stabile TAIL mindestens ``min_tail`` Schichten spannt; None, wenn kein
    stabiler Start existiert (Oszillation, Via-Negativa-Datum)."""
    layer_rows = [r for r in rows if r["kind"] == "layer"]
    if not layer_rows:
        return None
    final_top1 = layer_rows[-1]["top1_id"]
    for start_idx in range(len(layer_rows)):
        span = len(layer_rows) - start_idx
        if span < min_tail:
            break
        if all(r["top1_id"] == final_top1 for r in layer_rows[start_idx:]):
            return layer_rows[start_idx]["layer"]
    return None


def _out_layer(o: Any) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Decoder-Layer-Output (h, layer_entropy, present_kv) -> Residual h."""
    return o[0] if isinstance(o, tuple) else o.detach()


def apply_band_steer(buffer: torch.Tensor, experts: Sequence[int],
                     steer: float) -> None:
    """Band-Steering auf EINE Bias-Zeile: SET-Semantik nach dem registrierten
    dict-Arm von machine_base.steer (fill_(-steer) + g[e]=+steer) — das
    Template ueberschreibt den gelernten Bias fuer die Dauer des Blocks;
    Restaurierung macht der ContextManager (C3, copy_-Muster)."""
    with torch.no_grad():
        buffer.fill_(-float(steer))
        for e in experts:
            e = int(e)
            if not (0 <= e < buffer.numel()):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"apply_band_steer: Experte {e} außerhalb [0, {buffer.numel()})")
            buffer[e] = float(steer)


def apply_expert_knockout(buffer: torch.Tensor, experts: Sequence[int],
                          penalty: float) -> None:
    """Experten-Knockout auf EINE Bias-Zeile: ADDITIV auf den gelernten
    Original-Bias (die Spezialisten werden stummgeschaltet, alle übrigen
    tragen lernende Bias weiter — Notwendigkeits-Lesart des MI-3-Audits).
    In-place; Restaurierung macht der ContextManager."""
    with torch.no_grad():
        for e in experts:
            e = int(e)
            if not (0 <= e < buffer.numel()):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"apply_expert_knockout: Experte {e} außerhalb "
                    f"[0, {buffer.numel()})")
            buffer[e] += float(penalty)


def assemble_pct(collected: Sequence[Tuple[int, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor,
                                       torch.Tensor]],
                 n_experts: int, n_layers: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Canon-Assembly der tokenweisen Routing-Captures (moe_layers.py:
    top_weights sind normalisiert + ggf. 0.15-gedämpft + capacity_gate-
    skaliert, sonst 0): pct[T][L][E], ent[T][L]. Reine Tensor-Mathe — vom
    CPU-Piloten ohne Modell ausführbar (R2)."""
    if not collected:
        raise ValueError("assemble_pct: leere Capture-Liste.")
    ordered = sorted(collected, key=lambda row: row[0])
    if [row[0] for row in ordered] != list(range(n_layers)):
        raise ValueError(
            "assemble_pct: Schichtindizes unvollständig oder außerhalb der "
            f"Reihenfolge ({[row[0] for row in ordered]}).")
    ent_first = ordered[0][3]
    T = int(ent_first.shape[0])
    pct = [[[0.0] * n_experts for _ in range(n_layers)] for _ in range(T)]
    ent: List[List[float]] = [[0.0] * n_layers for _ in range(T)]
    for li, top_indices, top_weights, router_ent in ordered:
        if int(router_ent.shape[0]) != T:
            raise ValueError(
                "assemble_pct: inkonsistente Token-Zahl über Schichten "
                f"({int(router_ent.shape[0])} vs {T}).")
        for t in range(T):
            ent[t][li] = float(router_ent[t].item())
            for k in range(int(top_weights.shape[1])):
                e_idx = int(top_indices[t, k].item())
                pct[t][li][e_idx] += float(top_weights[t, k].item())
    return {"pct": pct, "ent": ent, "n_tokens": T, "n_layers": n_layers,
            "n_experts": n_experts}


def _store_layer(store: List[torch.Tensor], h: torch.Tensor,
                 first: bool = False) -> None:
    """Geordnete h_out-Capture-Einheit (EINHEIT S2): Emb-Hook schiebt an
    index 0 (h_out(-1)); Layer-Hooks feuern je Layer in Forward-Reihenfolge
    und haengen an — die Liste ist dann [-1, 0, 1, ..., L-1]."""
    if first:
        store.insert(0, h)
    else:
        store.append(h)


# == MI-5: Cross-Model-Zugänge (gemma-3-1b-it + Argon-Schwestern) ==========

def is_moe_model(model: Any) -> bool:
    """MI-5-Strukturwächter (Guards statt Simulation, Via-Negativa): True,
    wenn Decoder-Schicht 0 ein .moe trägt (Argon-Linie: 26 × {gate,
    experts_per_tok 2}); False auf DENSE-Modellen (gemma-3-1b-it — kein
    .moe, Scout-Fakt). Was strukturell fehlt, wird nicht simuliert; der
    Smoke bucht solche Felder OFFEN/strukturell."""
    try:
        layers = model.model.layers
        return bool(len(layers) > 0 and hasattr(layers[0], "moe"))
    except AttributeError:
        return False


def load_any_checkpoint(path: str, device: Optional[str] = None,
                        ) -> Tuple[Any, Any]:
    """MI-5 Voll-Checkpoint-Lader im Canon-Muster von verify_machines.py:
    339-356 (ArgonConfig(**cfg) -> ArgonForConditionalGeneration bf16 ->
    load_state_dict -> AutoTokenizer, pad_token-Canon), OHNE router_diff —
    die Schwester-Checkpoints tragen den kompletten Zustand (single
    model.safetensors je 4.8 G). STRICT-Load (EINHEIT S1): fehlende oder
    unerwartete Keys → RuntimeError, kein stiller Fallback. device-Canon:
    'cuda' wenn verfügbar, sonst 'cpu'."""
    import json as _json
    import os as _os
    from safetensors.torch import load_file as _load_file
    from transformers import AutoTokenizer as _AutoTokenizer
    from modeling_argon import ArgonConfig, ArgonForConditionalGeneration

    dev = device or ("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    with open(_os.path.join(path, "config.json")) as handle:
        cfg_dict = _json.load(handle)
    config = ArgonConfig(**cfg_dict)
    model = ArgonForConditionalGeneration(config).to(dtype=torch.bfloat16,
                                                     device=dev)
    state = _load_file(_os.path.join(path, "model.safetensors"))
    model.load_state_dict(state, strict=True)
    tokenizer = _AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(path)
    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:
        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token
    return model, tokenizer

from __future__ import annotations

import json

from machines.composite import CompositeMachine

    SEED,
    fingerprint,

    round6,
)

    DEC_ACC_TAU,
    DEC_P_TAU,
    DEC_PERM,
    LEX_PAIR,
    LEX_PAIR_B,
    MODELLN,
    ZWEIGE,
    _load_machines_ctx,
    bio_gelernt,

ZEITPUNKTE = (1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8)       # 6 echte Zeitpunkte je Maschine
P4_UNREIF_FAKTOR = 2.0                # gereift > 2 × ungereift
P4_MASCH_LAST_MIN = 5                 # machines Rang 4/4 in >= 5/6 Zweigen
P4_AUTO_MIN = 4                       # best_gap < raw_gap in >= 4/5 Repliken
P4_VATER_REF = 0.567980               # V6-Vater-Datum (registrierte Konstante,
                                      # aus mi6_subjektivitaet.json)


# -- P1a: Schwelle-weit ---------------------------------------------------------

def _loo_decoder(hiddens: np.ndarray, labels: np.ndarray
                 ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Leave-one-text-out LogReg + Permutations-Null 200 (MI-6-Muster)."""
    from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression
    n = len(labels)
    correct = 0
    for i in range(n):
        mask = np.ones(n, dtype=bool)
        mask[i] = False
        clf = LogisticRegression(max_iter=1000, C=1.0)
        clf.fit(hiddens[mask], labels[mask])
        correct += int(clf.predict(hiddens[i:i + 1])[0] == labels[i])
    acc = correct / n
    perm = []
    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    for _ in range(DEC_PERM):
        shuffled = rng.permutation(labels)
        ok = 0
        for i in range(n):
            mask = np.ones(n, dtype=bool)
            mask[i] = False
            clf = LogisticRegression(max_iter=1000, C=1.0)
            clf.fit(hiddens[mask], shuffled[mask])
            ok += int(clf.predict(hiddens[i:i + 1])[0] == shuffled[i])
        perm.append(ok / n)
    return {"acc": round6(acc),
            "p_perm": round6(float(np.mean([p_ >= acc for p_ in perm]))),
            "n_texts": n}


def lane_weit(ctx) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """P1a — 24-Texte-Korpus (6 Zeitpunkte × 2 Maschinen × 2 Stile);
    Band auf dem gepoolten wide-Kanal, je-Stil + canon-8 als DATUM."""
    _torch, _model, _tok, oracle, _dev = ctx
    a = make_composite(mode="oracle", oracle=oracle)
    from scratches.smoke_mi6_subjektivitaet import bio_halted, learn_pair_pin
    learn_pair_pin(a, LEX_PAIR)   # PREREG: A = gelehrte Biografie via LEX_PAIR
    bio_halted(a, "spiegel")
    bio_gelernt(a, "spiegel")
    b = make_composite(mode="oracle", oracle=oracle)
    bio_halted(b, "spiegel")
    bio_gelernt(b, "spiegel")
    texts: List[str] = []
    labels: List[int] = []
    styles: List[str] = []
    for machine, label in ((a, 1), (b, 0)):
        for runde in ZEITPUNKTE:
            probe = machine.preview_steps(["spiegel"] * runde,
                                          lexical_grounding=True)
            for style in ("textur", "numerisch"):
                texts.append(render_state(machine, style, probe=probe))
                labels.append(label)
                styles.append(style)
            machine.step("spiegel", lexical_grounding=True)
    labels_np = np.asarray(labels)

    def decode(sel: List[int], dims: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return _loo_decoder(
            np.stack([oracle.hidden_state(texts[i], dimensions=dims)
                      for i in sel]), labels_np[sel])
    wide_pooled = decode(list(range(len(texts))),
                         int(oracle.model.config.hidden_size))
    canon8 = decode(list(range(len(texts))), 8)
    per_stil = {stil: decode([i for i, s in enumerate(styles)
                              if s == stil],
                             int(oracle.model.config.hidden_size))
                for stil in ("textur", "numerisch")}
    ok = (wide_pooled["acc"] > DEC_ACC_TAU
          and wide_pooled["p_perm"] <= DEC_P_TAU)
    return {"wide_gepoolt": wide_pooled, "canon_8dim_datum": canon8,
            "wide_je_stil_datum": per_stil, "n_classes": {1: int((labels_np == 1).sum()),
                                                          0: int((labels_np == 0).sum())},
            "verdict": ok}


# -- P1b: Selbst/Fremd gereift ---------------------------------------------------

def _transplant_split(a: CompositeMachine, b: CompositeMachine, oracle: Any,
                      word: str, lg: bool, steer: bool, n: int
                      ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """N Repliken: Spiegel-Probe im Eigen- vs transplantierten Fremd-Status
    (deepcopy-Snapshot-Transplant, Snapshot→Probe→Restore-Rahmen)."""
    import copy
    from scratches.smoke_mi6_subjektivitaet import probe_gap_on
    splits: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    verdict_pairs: List[Dict[str, str]] = []
    for _ in range(n):
        donor = copy.deepcopy(b._snapshot())
        own = copy.deepcopy(a._snapshot())
        gap_self = probe_gap_on(a, oracle, word, lg, steer=steer)
        verdict_self = a.observe(word, lexical_grounding=lg).invariants["verdict"]
        a._restore_snapshot(donor)
        gap_fremd = probe_gap_on(a, oracle, word, lg, steer=steer)
        verdict_fremd = a.observe(word,
                                  lexical_grounding=lg).invariants["verdict"]
        a._restore_snapshot(own)
        splits.append({"gap_self": gap_self, "gap_fremd": gap_fremd,
                       "split": abs(gap_self - gap_fremd)})
        verdict_pairs.append({"self": verdict_self, "fremd": verdict_fremd})
    return {"splits": splits,
            "mean_split": float(np.mean([s["split"] for s in splits])),
            "verdict_pairs": verdict_pairs}


def lane_selbstfremd_gereift(mode: str, oracle: Any = None, gpu: bool = False
                             ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """P1b — Gereifte Transplant (bio_gelernt auf BEIDEN Seiten) gegen die
    ungereifte MI-6-V3-Replikation (nur learn_pair_pin, erwartet ≈0.0)."""
    a = make_composite(mode=mode, oracle=oracle)
    from scratches.smoke_mi6_subjektivitaet import bio_halted, learn_pair_pin
    learn_pair_pin(a, LEX_PAIR)   # PREREG: A gereift — LOOP-Tradition,
    bio_halted(a, "spiegel")      # Expansionen (ohne LEX_PAIR wäre der Arm
    a_g = bio_gelernt(a, "spiegel")  # selbst HALTED — Erstrun-Defekt)
    b = make_composite(mode=mode, oracle=oracle)
    learn_pair_pin(b, LEX_PAIR_B)   # B gereift — Fremd-Tradition auf
    bio_halted(b, "stille")         # LEX_PAIR_B; auf der Spiegel-Probe
    b_g = bio_gelernt(b, "stille")  # bleibt B HALTED (Fremd-Linie)
    gereift = _transplant_split(a, b, oracle, "spiegel", True, gpu, N_PROBES)
    dekoy = _transplant_split(a, b, oracle, "spiegel", False, gpu, N_PROBES)
    # Kontrolle: UNGEREIFTE Zustande (MI-6-V3-Replikation)
    a2 = make_composite(mode=mode, oracle=oracle)
    from scratches.smoke_mi6_subjektivitaet import learn_pair_pin
    learn_pair_pin(a2, LEX_PAIR)
    b2 = make_composite(mode=mode, oracle=oracle)
    learn_pair_pin(b2, LEX_PAIR_B)
    ungereift = _transplant_split(a2, b2, oracle, "spiegel", True, gpu,
                                  N_PROBES)
    sp = gereift["mean_split"]
    un = ungereift["mean_split"]
    ok = sp > 0.0 and sp > P4_UNREIF_FAKTOR * un
    return {"gereift": gereift, "dekoy_datum": dekoy,
            "ungereift_kontrolle": ungereift,
            "phase_a": a_g, "phase_b": b_g,
            "split_gereift": round6(sp), "split_ungereift": round6(un),
            "verdict": ok}


# -- P1c: Oracle-Sonder (machines sondert sich) ----------------------------------

def lane_oracle_sonder(gpu: bool) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """P1c — dasselbe V6-Geruest, NEU gerichtet: machines Rang 4/4 in
    >= 5/6 Zweigen; Schwester-Spread und Vater-Raenge als DATUM."""
    import gc

    import torch
    if not gpu:
        return None
    from machines.mech_interp import load_any_checkpoint
    from scratches.smoke_mi6_subjektivitaet import (
        bio_gelernt,
        bio_halted,
        learn_pair_pin,
    )
    rows: Dict[str, Dict[str, float]] = {}
    for name in MODELLN:
        machine = None
        _ctx_oracle = None
        if name == "argon_1b_machines":
            torch, model, tok, _ctx_oracle, dev = _load_machines_ctx()
        else:
            model, tok = load_any_checkpoint(f"./{name}")
            dev = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
        oracle = ArgonOracle(model, tokenizer=tok, device=dev)
        zweig_gaps: Dict[str, float] = {}
        for zweig in ZWEIGE:
            machine = make_composite(mode="oracle", oracle=oracle)
            bio_halted(machine, zweig)
            learn_pair_pin(machine, LEX_PAIR)
            bio_gelernt(machine, "spiegel")
            zweig_gaps[zweig] = float(np.mean(machine._gap_history[-3:]))
            del machine
        rows[name] = {z: round6(v) for z, v in zweig_gaps.items()}
        del model, tok, oracle, _ctx_oracle
        machine = None
        gc.collect()
        if torch.cuda.is_available():
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    masch_last = 0
    vater_ranks = []
    spreads: List[float] = []
    for z in ZWEIGE:
        values = {n: rows[n][z] for n in MODELLN}
        order = sorted(MODELLN, key=lambda n: values[n])
        masch_last += int(order[-1] == "argon_1b_machines")
        vater_ranks.append(order.index("argon_1b") + 1)
        schw = [rows[n][z] for n in MODELLN if n != "argon_1b_machines"]
        spreads.append(float(max(schw) - min(schw)))
    return {"gaps": rows, "masch_last_count": masch_last,
            "vater_ranks_datum": vater_ranks,
            "schwester_spread_datum": round6(max(spreads)),
            "perm_null_referenz":
                "P(X>=5 | X~Bin(6,1/4)) = 19/4096 = 4.64e-3"
                " (KORREKTUR-LOG IV: die MI-6-Registrierung schrieb <= 9e-4"
                " — die Formel liess den (1/4)^5-Faktor weg; Band unberuehrt,"
                " nur die Referenz-Annotation korrigiert)",
            "verdict": masch_last >= P4_MASCH_LAST_MIN}


# -- P1d: Dissonanz-Hauptsatz -----------------------------------------------------

def lane_dissonanz(oracle: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """P1d — step_autonomous senkt den Composite-Gap unter das
    Substrat-Vater-Niveau (die User-Formel als registriertes Band)."""
    from scratches.smoke_mi6_subjektivitaet import learn_pair_pin
    machine = make_composite(mode="oracle", oracle=oracle)
    learn_pair_pin(machine, LEX_PAIR)
    bio_gelernt(machine, "spiegel")
    repliken: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for _ in range(N_PROBES):
        result = machine.step_autonomous("spiegel", lexical_grounding=True)
        auto = result.invariants["autonomous"]
        raw_gap = float(auto["candidate_gaps"]["raw"])
        best = float(auto["best_gap"])
        repliken.append({"raw": raw_gap, "best": best,
                         "diff": round6(raw_gap - best),
                         "chosen_preset": auto["chosen_preset"],
                         "candidate_gaps":
                             {k: round6(v)
                              for k, v in auto["candidate_gaps"].items()}})
    auto_min = sum(1 for r in repliken if r["best"] < r["raw"])
    mean_best = float(np.mean([r["best"] for r in repliken]))
    mean_raw = float(np.mean([r["raw"] for r in repliken]))
    ok = auto_min >= P4_AUTO_MIN and mean_best < P4_VATER_REF
    return {"repliken": repliken, "auto_min_count": auto_min,
            "mean_best": round6(mean_best), "mean_raw": round6(mean_raw),
            "vater_referenz": P4_VATER_REF,
            "expansionen": len(machine.expansions), "verdict": ok}


# -- Treiber ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def stufe(text: str) -> None:
    print(text, flush=True)


def cpu_findings() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """CPU-Pilot: P1b-Mechanik (in_loop) zweifach + Determinismus-Digest."""
    runs = []
    for i in range(2):
        runs.append(lane_selbstfremd_gereift("in_loop"))
    det_equal = json.dumps(runs[0], sort_keys=True,
                           default=str) == json.dumps(runs[1], sort_keys=True,
                                                      default=str)
    runs[0]["verdict"] = bool(runs[0]["verdict"])
    return {"p1b_cpu": runs[0], "det_equal": det_equal}


def findings(cpu: Dict[str, Any], gpu: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]
             ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Verdikt-Zeilen nach den PREREG-Baendern."""
    rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    rows.append({"id": "mi6p4_schwelle_weit",
                 "assert": (gpu or {}).get("schwelle_weit", {}).get("verdict"),
                 "note": ("der wide-Kanal traegt die Biografie ueber den fixierten Band"
                          " (acc>0.6, p<=0.01); je-Stil und canon-8 als DATUM")})
    rows.append({"id": "mi6p4_selbstfremd_gereift",
                 "assert": (gpu or {}).get("selbstfremd_gereift", {}).get(
                     "verdict"),
                 "note": ("Transplant NACH bio_gelernt oeffnet den Gap-Kanal; die "
                          "ungereifte V3-Kontrolle bleibt ≈0.0")})
    rows.append({"id": "mi6p4_oracle_sonder",
                 "assert": (gpu or {}).get("oracle_sonder", {}).get("verdict"),
                 "note": ("argon_1b_machines sondert sich am Maschinen-Selbstbe"
                          "schreibungs-Prompt (Rang 4/4 in >=5/6 Zweigen)")})
    rows.append({"id": "mi6p4_dissonanz",
                 "assert": (gpu or {}).get("dissonanz", {}).get("verdict"),
                 "note": ("step_autonomous senkt den Gap unter das Vater-Niveau"
                          " 0.567980 (User-Formel)")})
    rows.append({"id": "mi6p4_det_doppellauf",
                 "assert": cpu["det_equal"],
                 "note": "CPU-P1b-Schiene bitidentisch ueber zwei Laeufe"})
    return rows


def main() -> int:
    import argparse
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--gpu", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    stufe("== MI-6-P4 — Subjekt-Schluss (neue registrierte Baender) ==")
    stufe("MI-6-Baseline: 5 PASS/3 FAIL/0 OFFEN (exit 1) — unveraendert")
    cpu = cpu_findings()

    gpu: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    offen = False
    if args.gpu:
        gpu = {}
        try:
            stufe("GPU-Kontext laden (argon_1b + argon_1b_machines)")
            ctx = _load_machines_ctx()
        except Exception as exc:   # noqa: BLE001
            print(f"GPU-Kontext nicht verfügbar ({exc}) — Lanes OFFEN.",
                  flush=True)
            gpu, offen = None, True
            ctx = None
        if ctx is not None:
            import gc

            import torch
            stufe("P1a Schwelle-weit (24 Texte, gepooltes Band)")
            gpu["schwelle_weit"] = lane_weit(ctx)
            stufe("P1b Selbst/Fremd gereift (Transplant nach bio_gelernt)")
            gpu["selbstfremd_gereift"] = lane_selbstfremd_gereift(
                "oracle", ctx[3], gpu=True)
            stufe("P1d Dissonanz-Hauptsatz (step_autonomous)")
            gpu["dissonanz"] = lane_dissonanz(ctx[3])
            model, tokenizer = ctx[1], ctx[2]
            del ctx, model, tokenizer
            gc.collect()
            if torch.cuda.is_available():
                torch.cuda.empty_cache()
            stufe("P1c Oracle-Sonder (4 Checkpoints × 6 Zweige)")
            gpu["oracle_sonder"] = lane_oracle_sonder(gpu=True)

    rows = findings(cpu, gpu)
    print()
    print("BEFUNDE:")
    for r in rows:
        if r["assert"] is None:
            print(f"  {r['id']:<26} OFFEN")
        else:
            print(f"  {r['id']:<26} {'PASS' if r['assert'] else 'FAIL'}")
    print()
    fails = [r for r in rows if r["assert"] is False]
    opened = [r for r in rows if r["assert"] is None]
    if offen:
        opened.append({"id": "gpu_kontext"})
    print(f"BILANZ: {len(rows) - len(fails) - len(opened)} PASS; "
          f"{len(fails)} FEHLGESCHLAGEN; {len(opened)} OFFEN")
    print("GRUEN — P4-Baender eingehalten" if not fails and not opened
          else ("ROT — Bands verletzt: " + ", ".join(r["id"] for r in fails)
                if fails else "OFFEN — GPU-Lanes nicht gefahren"))

    payload = {"cpu": cpu, "gpu": gpu, "findings": rows,
               "all_pass": not fails and not offen,
               "open": [r["id"] for r in opened]}
    import os
    out = os.path.join("scratches", "mi6p4_schluss.json")
    with open(out, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        json.dump(payload, fh, indent=1, ensure_ascii=False)
    print(f"JSON: {out}")
    return 1 if fails else (2 if opened else 0)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

K_LADDER = (1, 2, 3, 4)
DEC_ACC_TAU = 0.6
DEC_PERM = 200
