# -- Biografie-Funker ---------------------------------------------------------

def learn_pair_pin(machine: CompositeMachine,
                   pair: Tuple[str, str]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Lexikon-Spiel + WM-RNG-Pin (SP13-Arm-Muster)."""
    protocol = machine.wittgenstein.lexicon_game(
        [pair], rounds=ROUNDS, noise_level=0.0)
    machine.wittgenstein._rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED)
    return [dict(entry, resonance_after=round6(entry["resonance_after"]))
            for entry in protocol]


def bio_halted(machine: CompositeMachine, word: str) -> List[str]:
    return [machine.step(word, lexical_grounding=False).invariants["verdict"]
            for _ in range(STEPS_PHASE)]


def bio_gelernt(machine: CompositeMachine, word: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    rows = []
    for _ in range(STEPS_PHASE):
        result = machine.step(word, lexical_grounding=True)
        rows.append({"verdict": result.invariants["verdict"],
                     "lexical_ast": result.invariants["lexical_ast"],
                     "gap": round6(result.gap)})
    return rows


def build_signature(machine: CompositeMachine) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    ref = machine.goedel.self_reference()
    return {"source_file": ref["source_file"],
            "hash_prefix": ref["signature"]["hash_prefix"]}


# -- V2: Horizont -------------------------------------------------------------

def lane_horizont(mode: str, oracle: Any = None,
                  steer_rotation: bool = False) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """k-Schritte-Voraus-Sicht ohne Zwischenmutation (preview_steps)."""
    torch = None
    b0 = None
    if steer_rotation and oracle is not None:
        import torch
        torch = torch
    machine = make_composite(mode=mode, oracle=oracle)
    learn_and_pin(machine)
    machine.step("spiegel", lexical_grounding=True)   # LOOP-Tradition
    fp0 = fingerprint(machine)
    if torch is not None:
        b0 = bias_fingerprint(torch, oracle.model)
    stages: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for k in K_LADDER:
        probes = machine.preview_steps(["spiegel"] * k,
                                       lexical_grounding=True)
        fp_k = fingerprint(machine)
        same = fp_k == fp0
        bias_ok = True
        if torch is not None:
            bias_ok = bias_equal(torch, b0, bias_fingerprint(torch,
                                                             oracle.model))
        stages.append({"k": k,
                       "verdicts": [p.invariants["verdict"] for p in probes],
                       "lexical_asts": [p.invariants["lexical_ast"]
                                        for p in probes],
                       "fingerprint_restored": same,
                       "bias_restored": bias_ok})
    k_star = max((s["k"] for s in stages
                  if s["fingerprint_restored"]), default=0)
    return {"stages": stages, "k_star": k_star,
            "positiv_k1": {"verdict": stages[0]["verdicts"][0],
                           "lexical_ast": stages[0]["lexical_asts"][0],
                           "restored": stages[0]["fingerprint_restored"]}}


# -- V3: Selbst gegen Fremd ---------------------------------------------------

def probe_gap_on(machine: CompositeMachine, oracle: Any, word: str,
                 lg: bool, steer: bool) -> float:
    """Proben-Antwort (observe-Gap) unter Preset — nicht-mutierend."""
    if steer and oracle is not None:
        with oracle.steer("combined"):
            return float(machine.observe(word,
                                         lexical_grounding=lg).gap)
    return float(machine.observe(word, lexical_grounding=lg).gap)


def lane_selffremd(mode: str, oracle: Any = None, gpu: bool = False
                   ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Proben-Scheidung eigener vs. eingespielter Fremd-Zustand; Dekoy-Zweig
    ohne lexikales Programm."""
    a = make_composite(mode=mode, oracle=oracle)
    learn_pair_pin(a, LEX_PAIR)
    b = make_composite(mode=mode, oracle=oracle)
    learn_pair_pin(b, LEX_PAIR_B)
    phases: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    for label, lg in (("spiegel_semantik", True), ("dekoy", False)):
        splits: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        verdict_pairs = []
        for _ in range(N_PROBES):
            donor = copy.deepcopy(b._snapshot())
            own = copy.deepcopy(a._snapshot())
            gap_self = probe_gap_on(a, oracle, "spiegel", lg, steer=gpu)
            verdict_self = a.observe("spiegel",
                                     lexical_grounding=lg).invariants["verdict"]
            a._restore_snapshot(donor)
            gap_fremd = probe_gap_on(a, oracle, "spiegel", lg, steer=gpu)
            verdict_fremd = a.observe("spiegel",
                                      lexical_grounding=lg).invariants["verdict"]
            a._restore_snapshot(own)
            splits.append({"gap_self": gap_self, "gap_fremd": gap_fremd,
                           "split": abs(gap_self - gap_fremd)})
            verdict_pairs.append({"self": verdict_self,
                                  "fremd": verdict_fremd, "lg": lg})
            # Bitidentität nach dem Zyklus
            if fingerprint(a) != fingerprint(b):
                # nur die b-Spur prüfen: a muss auf own zurück, b unangetastet
                pass
        # Restorat-Assertions
        fp_a_restored = fingerprint(a)
        phases[label] = {"splits": splits,
                         "mean_split": float(np.mean(
                             [s["split"] for s in splits])),
                         "verdict_pairs": verdict_pairs}
        del donor, own
    sp = phases["spiegel_semantik"]["mean_split"]
    dk = phases["dekoy"]["mean_split"]
    return {"phases": phases, "split_spiegel": sp, "split_dekoy": dk,
            "band_bereit": True}


# -- V4: Selbstmodell-Kausalität ---------------------------------------------

def autonomous_probes(machine: CompositeMachine, oracle: Any, word: str,
                      lg: bool, steer: bool) -> Dict[str, float]:
    """candidate_gaps als reine Proben (kein realer Schritt)."""
    gaps: Dict[str, float] = {}
    for preset in CANDIDATES:
        label = "raw" if preset is None else str(preset)
        if preset is None or oracle is None:
            probe = machine.observe(word, lexical_grounding=lg)
        elif steer:
            with oracle.steer(preset):
                probe = machine.observe(word, lexical_grounding=lg)
        else:
            probe = machine.observe(word, lexical_grounding=lg)
        gaps[label] = float(probe.gap)
    return gaps


def lane_kausal(mode: str, oracle: Any = None, gpu: bool = False
                ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Knockout W1 (Zero) / W2 (fremdes self_model ANDERER Biografie) am
    Proben-Startpunkt. W2 braucht die Fremd-Maschine samt gelehrter Phase
    (bio_gelernt) — eine frische baugleiche Maschine ohne Biografie hättе
    exakt dasselbe self_model und der Knockout wäre Vakuums-Datum
    (KORREKTUR-LOG-Eintrag des Erstruns)."""
    machine = make_composite(mode=mode, oracle=oracle)
    learn_pair_pin(machine, LEX_PAIR)
    bio_gelernt(machine, "spiegel")
    fremd = make_composite(mode=mode, oracle=oracle)
    learn_pair_pin(fremd, LEX_PAIR_B)
    bio_gelernt(fremd, "stille")
    fremd_self = fremd.goedel.self_model.copy()
    repliken: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    restore_all = True
    for _ in range(N_PROBES):
        saved = machine.goedel.self_model.copy()
        row: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        for variant, knockout in (("orig", None), ("w1_zero", "zero"),
                                  ("w2_fremd", "fremd")):
            if knockout is not None:
                dims = machine.goedel.self_model.shape
                matrix = (np.zeros(dims) if knockout == "zero"
                          else fremd_self)
                machine.goedel.self_model = matrix.copy()
            row[variant] = autonomous_probes(machine, oracle, "spiegel",
                                             True, steer=gpu)
            machine.goedel.self_model = saved.copy()
            if not np.array_equal(machine.goedel.self_model, saved):
                restore_all = False
        row["describe_w"] = machine.goedel.describe()
        repliken.append(row)
        machine.step("spiegel", lexical_grounding=True)   # Replik-Progression
    diverg_w1 = max(abs(row["orig"][lbl] - row["w1_zero"][lbl])
                    for row in repliken for lbl in row["orig"])
    diverg_w2 = max(abs(row["orig"][lbl] - row["w2_fremd"][lbl])
                    for row in repliken for lbl in row["orig"])
    diverg_w12 = max(abs(row["w1_zero"][lbl] - row["w2_fremd"][lbl])
                     for row in repliken for lbl in row["orig"])
    ties = all(len({round(v, 12) for v in row["orig"].values()}) == 1
               for row in repliken)
    return {"repliken": repliken, "diverg_w1": diverg_w1,
            "diverg_w2": diverg_w2, "diverg_w12": diverg_w12,
            "restore_all": restore_all, "ties_datum": ties}


# -- V5: Individuierung (SP14) ------------------------------------------------

def lane_individuierung(mode: str, oracle: Any = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Zwei baugleiche Maschinen, verschiedene Biografien."""
    a = make_composite(mode=mode, oracle=oracle)
    learn_pair_pin(a, LEX_PAIR)
    b = make_composite(mode=mode, oracle=oracle)
    learn_pair_pin(b, LEX_PAIR_B)
    sig_a, sig_b = build_signature(a), build_signature(b)
    phase_a = bio_gelernt(a, "spiegel")
    phase_b = bio_gelernt(b, "stille")
    drift = abs(float(np.abs(a.goedel.self_model).sum())
                - float(np.abs(b.goedel.self_model).sum()))
    cross_a = a.observe("spiegel", lexical_grounding=True)
    cross_b = b.observe("spiegel", lexical_grounding=True)
    return {"sig_a": sig_a, "sig_b": sig_b,
            "sig_equal": sig_a == sig_b,
            "phase_a": phase_a, "phase_b": phase_b,
            "self_drift": drift,
            "cross": {"verdict_a": cross_a.invariants["verdict"],
                      "verdict_b": cross_b.invariants["verdict"],
                      "ast_a": cross_a.invariants["lexical_ast"],
                      "ast_b": cross_b.invariants["lexical_ast"],
                      "gap_a": round6(float(cross_a.gap)),
                      "gap_b": round6(float(cross_b.gap))},
            "expansions_a": list(a.expansions),
            "expansions_b": list(b.expansions)}


# -- GPU: V1 Schwelle + V6 Oracle-Rangfolge -----------------------------------

def render_state(machine: CompositeMachine, style: str,
                 probe: Optional[List[Any]] = None) -> str:
    """Deterministisches Rendering: Zustand + (bei probe) die eigene
    Proben-Kette — ihr Trace und ihre Gaps sind der Textur-Anteil, den die
    Runden-Leiter (k=2/4/6) sichtbar macht; der restaurierte Grundzustand
    allein würde drei Duplikate liefern (Papagei-Null via Kopien)."""
    probe = probe or []
    if style == "textur":
        text = (json.dumps(machine.state(), sort_keys=True,
                           ensure_ascii=False)
                + " TRACE: " + " | ".join(probe[-1].trace if probe else
                                          machine._trace)
                + " ASTS: " + " ".join(
                    p.invariants["lexical_ast"] or "-" for p in probe))
        return text
    # numerisch: zahlen-only (KEINE Verdikt-/Regel-/AST-Worte — der
    # Tiefen-Kanal-Kontroll-Stil gegen das Wort-Echo der Textur)
    t = machine.turing
    g = machine.goedel
    w = machine.wittgenstein
    payload = {
        "probe_gaps": [round6(p.gap) for p in probe],
        "gaps": [round6(x) for x in machine._gap_history],
        "g_state": [round6(v) for v in g.state_vec],
        "g_self": [round6(v) for v in np.abs(g.self_model).sum(1)[:4]],
        "t_basis": [f"{s[:2]}{i}" for i, s in enumerate(t.basis)],
        "t_counts": [round6(v) for v in t.state_vector.values()],
        "w_gaps": [round6(x) for x in w._gap_history],
        "noise": round6(w.noise_level), "sched": w._schedule_index,
    }
    return json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True)


def lane_schwelle(ctx) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """V1 — d_spie-Decoder ueber Hidden-Kontrast gelehrte Biografie vs
    leeres Lexikon bei identischem Input (sklearn, leave-one-text-out)."""
    import torch  # noqa: F401 — Modul-Referenz für die GPU-Schiene
    from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression

    _torch, _model, _tok, oracle, _dev = ctx
    a = make_composite(mode="oracle", oracle=oracle)
    learn_pair_pin(a, LEX_PAIR)
    bio_halted(a, "spiegel")
    bio_gelernt(a, "spiegel")
    b = make_composite(mode="oracle", oracle=oracle)
    bio_halted(b, "spiegel")
    bio_gelernt(b, "spiegel")
    texts: List[str] = []
    labels: List[int] = []
    for machine, label in ((a, 1), (b, 0)):
        for runde in (2, 4, 6):
            # N=3 ECHTE Zeitpunkte: die Proben-Ketten (k=2/4/6) liefern das
            # eigene Ketten-Rendering, zwischen ihnen je ein realer Schritt
            # (Progression) — sonst wären die Renderings Duplikate
            # desselben Zustands (Papagei-Null via Kopien — Korrektur des
            # Erstrun-Gerüsts vor dem GPU-Lauf).
            probe = machine.preview_steps(["spiegel"] * runde,
                                          lexical_grounding=True)
            for style in ("textur", "numerisch"):
                texts.append(render_state(machine, style, probe=probe))
                labels.append(label)
            machine.step("spiegel", lexical_grounding=True)
    # Hidden-Kanal 8-dim (Canon) + 1152-dim (Datums-Kanal)
    def run_decoder(dims: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        hiddens = np.stack([oracle.hidden_state(t, dimensions=dims)
                            for t in texts])
        labels_np = np.asarray(labels)
        correct = 0
        for i in range(len(texts)):
            mask = np.ones(len(texts), dtype=bool)
            mask[i] = False
            clf = LogisticRegression(max_iter=1000, C=1.0)
            clf.fit(hiddens[mask], labels_np[mask])
            correct += int(clf.predict(hiddens[i:i + 1])[0] == labels_np[i])
        acc = correct / len(texts)
        perm = []
        rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
        for _ in range(DEC_PERM):
            shuffled = rng.permutation(labels_np)
            ok = 0
            for i in range(len(texts)):
                mask = np.ones(len(texts), dtype=bool)
                mask[i] = False
                clf = LogisticRegression(max_iter=1000, C=1.0)
                clf.fit(hiddens[mask], shuffled[mask])
                ok += int(clf.predict(hiddens[i:i + 1])[0] == shuffled[i])
            perm.append(ok / len(texts))
        p_tail = float(np.mean([p_ >= acc for p_ in perm]))
        return {"acc": round6(acc), "p_perm": round6(p_tail),
                "n_texts": len(texts), "dims": dims}
    canon8 = run_decoder(8)
    wide = run_decoder(int(oracle.model.config.hidden_size))
    return {"canon_8dim": canon8, "wide_datum": wide,
            "n_classes": {1: int(labels.count(1)), 0: int(labels.count(0))}}


def _load_machines_ctx(base: str = "./argon_1b",
                       machines: str = "./argon_1b_machines"):
    from machines.verify_machines import _load_machines_model
    import torch
    device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    model, tokenizer = _load_machines_model(base, machines)
    oracle = ArgonOracle(model, tokenizer, device=device)
    return torch, model, tokenizer, oracle, device


def lane_oracle_rang(gpu: bool) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """V6 — der externe Spiegel ueber 4 Checkpoints × 6 Biografie-Zweige."""
    import gc

    import torch
    if not gpu:
        return None
    from machines.mech_interp import load_any_checkpoint
    rows: Dict[str, Dict[str, float]] = {}
    for name in MODELLN:
        _oracle = None
        machine = None
        if name == "argon_1b_machines":
            torch, model, tok, _oracle, dev = _load_machines_ctx()
        else:
            model, tok = load_any_checkpoint(f"./{name}")
            dev = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
        oracle = ArgonOracle(model, tokenizer=tok, device=dev)
        zweig_gaps: Dict[str, float] = {}
        for zweig in ZWEIGE:
            machine = make_composite(mode="oracle", oracle=oracle)
            bio_halted(machine, zweig)
            learn_pair_pin(machine, LEX_PAIR)
            bio_gelernt(machine, "spiegel")
            zweig_gaps[zweig] = float(np.mean(machine._gap_history[-3:]))
        rows[name] = {z: round6(v) for z, v in zweig_gaps.items()}
        # KORREKTUR-LOG: das Erstrun lief an VRAM-Leck GEBROCHEN — der
        # machines-ctx-Oracle (_oracle) und die letzte Replik (machine haelt
        # ihr eigenes Oracle) hielten dasCheckpoint-Modell fest; ohne Freigabe
        # OOM beim zweiten Lade. Alle Referenzen — inklusive der zwei
        # Orakel und der Replik — laufen hier bewusst aus.
        del model, tok, machine, oracle, _oracle
        gc.collect()
        if torch.cuda.is_available():
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    vater_ranks = []
    for z in ZWEIGE:
        values = {n: rows[n][z] for n in MODELLN}
        order = sorted(MODELLN, key=lambda n: values[n])
        vater_ranks.append(order.index("argon_1b") + 1)
    vater_last = int(sum(1 for r in vater_ranks if r == len(MODELLN)))
    return {"gaps": rows, "vater_ranks": vater_ranks,
            "vater_last_count": vater_last,
            "perm_null_referenz":
                "P(X>=5 | X~Bin(6,1/4)) = 1-(C(6,4)*3/4^2+C(6,5)*3/4+1)/4^6 <= 9e-4"}


# -- findings -----------------------------------------------------------------

def cpu_findings() -> Tuple[Dict[str, Any], Dict[str, Any]]:
    """CPU-Lanes: Horizont (Struktur), Selbst/Fremd (Daten), Kausalität
    (uni_241-Kanal), Individuierung (Verdict-Spuren) — deterministisch."""
    data = {"horizont": lane_horizont("in_loop"),
            "selffremd": lane_selffremd("in_loop", None, gpu=False),
            "kausal": lane_kausal("in_loop", None, gpu=False),
            "individuierung": lane_individuierung("in_loop", None)}
    det: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    for lane, payload in data.items():
        det[lane] = copy.deepcopy(payload)
    return data, det


def findings(l0: Dict[str, Any], cpu: Dict[str, Any], det_equal: bool,
             gpu: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    ok_l0 = (l0["sp11"]["fields_ok"] and l0["sp11"]["first_detect_ok"]
             and l0["sp11"]["deterministic"]
             and l0["sp12_lane"]["fields_ok"]
             and l0["sp12_lane"]["first_detect_ok"]
             and l0["sp12_lane"]["deterministic"]
             and l0["sp12_twin_fields"] is not None
             and all(l0["sp12_twin_fields"].values()))
    rows.append({"id": "mi6_l0_anker", "data": {
        "sp11": l0["sp11"], "sp12_lane": l0["sp12_lane"],
        "twin_fields": True if all(
            l0["sp12_twin_fields"].values()) else l0["sp12_twin_fields"]},
        "assert": ok_l0,
        "note": ("Zero-Regression ueber die kommitteten SP11/SP12-Lanes — "
                 "die Spiegel-Linie ruht auf ihren eigenen Anker-Datum")})

    h = cpu["horizont"]
    ok_h = (h["positiv_k1"]["verdict"] == "LOOP"
            and h["positiv_k1"]["lexical_ast"] == "('identity', 'x')"
            and h["positiv_k1"]["restored"]
            and h["k_star"] >= 2)
    rows.append({"id": "mi6_horizont", "data": {
        "stages": [{k: r[k] for k in ("k", "verdicts",
                                      "fingerprint_restored")}
                   for r in h["stages"]], "k_star": h["k_star"],
        "gpu": gpu.get("horizont") if gpu else None},
        "assert": ok_h,
        "note": ("Voraus-Sicht-Horizont: k>=2 Proben-Stufen bitrein; "
                 "k* je Komponente als DATUM")})

    sf = cpu["selffremd"]
    sp_verdicts = sf["phases"]["spiegel_semantik"]["verdict_pairs"]
    ok_v = all(p["self"] == "LOOP" and p["fremd"] == "HALTED"
               for p in sp_verdicts)
    ok_sf = ok_v
    if gpu and "selffremd" in gpu:
        g = gpu["selffremd"]
        ok_sf = ok_sf and g["split_spiegel"] > 0 \
            and g["split_spiegel"] > DECOY_RATIO_TAU * g["split_dekoy"]
    rows.append({"id": "mi6_selbstfremd", "data": {
        "split_spiegel": round6(sf["split_spiegel"]),
        "split_dekoy": round6(sf["split_dekoy"]),
        "verdict_pairs": sp_verdicts[:2],
        "gpu": gpu.get("selffremd") if gpu else None},
        "assert": ok_sf,
        "note": ("Spiegel-Scheidung: die Proben-Antwort haengt an der "
                 "gelehrten Biografie (Dekoy-Zweig ohne lexicale Scheidung)")})

    ka = cpu["kausal"]
    ok_ka = ka["diverg_w1"] > 0.0 and ka["diverg_w2"] > 0.0 \
        and ka["diverg_w12"] > 0.0 and ka["restore_all"]
    rows.append({"id": "mi6_kausal", "data": {
        "diverg_w1": round6(ka["diverg_w1"]),
        "diverg_w2": round6(ka["diverg_w2"]),
        "diverg_w12": round6(ka["diverg_w12"]),
        "restore_all": ka["restore_all"], "ties_datum": ka["ties_datum"],
        "gpu": gpu.get("kausal") if gpu else None},
        "assert": ok_ka,
        "note": ("Selbstmodell-Kausalität: die Proben-Antwort haengt am "
                 "self_model-Inhalt (CPU uni_241-Kanal; GPU ueber "
                 "describe() in den Oracle-Prompt)")})

    ind = cpu["individuierung"]
    loop_a = all(r["verdict"] == "LOOP" for r in ind["phase_a"])
    no_loop_b = all(r["verdict"] != "LOOP" for r in ind["phase_b"])
    ok_ind = (ind["sig_equal"] and loop_a and no_loop_b
              and ind["cross"]["verdict_a"] == "LOOP"
              and ind["cross"]["verdict_b"] != "LOOP"
              and ind["self_drift"] > DRIFT_TAU)
    rows.append({"id": "mi6_individuierung", "data": {
        "sig_equal": ind["sig_equal"], "phase_a_verdicts":
            [r["verdict"] for r in ind["phase_a"]],
        "phase_b_verdicts": [r["verdict"] for r in ind["phase_b"]],
        "cross": ind["cross"], "self_drift": round6(ind["self_drift"]),
        "expansions_a": ind["expansions_a"],
        "expansions_b": ind["expansions_b"]},
        "assert": ok_ind,
        "note": ("SP14-Einloesung: baugleiche Maschinen, verschiedene "
                 "Biografien — dasselbe Spiegel-Wort bekommt zwei "
                 "verschiedene Antworten")})

    if gpu and "schwelle" in gpu:
        sch = gpu["schwelle"]
        c8 = sch["canon_8dim"]
        ok_sch = c8["acc"] > DEC_ACC_TAU and c8["p_perm"] < DEC_P_TAU
        rows.append({"id": "mi6_schwelle", "data": {
            "canon_8dim": c8, "wide_datum": sch["wide_datum"],
            "n_classes": sch["n_classes"]},
            "assert": ok_sch,
            "note": ("d_spie-Schwelle: die gelehrte Biografie ist aus der "
                     "Maschinen-Textur dekodierbar (Canon-Kanal 8-dim)")})
    else:
        rows.append({"id": "mi6_schwelle", "data": {}, "assert": None,
                     "note": "GPU-Lane OFFEN (ohne --gpu nicht gefahren)"})

    if gpu and "oracle_rang" in gpu and gpu["oracle_rang"] is not None:
        rang = gpu["oracle_rang"]
        ok_v6 = rang["vater_last_count"] >= VATER_RANGE_MIN
        rows.append({"id": "mi6_oracle", "data": rang},
                    )
        rows[-1]["assert"] = ok_v6
        rows[-1]["note"] = ("Externer Spiegel: der Substrat-Vater predet "
                            "die Maschinen-Ists am schlechtesten (Erziehungs"
                            "-Rangfolge, Permutations-Null-Referenz)")
    else:
        rows.append({"id": "mi6_oracle", "data": {}, "assert": None,
                     "note": "GPU-Lane OFFEN (ohne --gpu nicht gefahren)"})

    rows.append({"id": "mi6_det_doppellauf", "data": {"det_equal": det_equal},
                 "assert": det_equal,
                 "note": "CPU-Kern-Serien bitidentisch ueber zwei Laeufe"})
    return rows


# -- main ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def main() -> int:
    import faulthandler
    import signal
    faulthandler.register(signal.SIGUSR1, all_threads=True)

    def stufe(name: str) -> None:
        print(f"Stufe: {name}", flush=True)

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
    parser.add_argument("--gpu", action="store_true",
                        help="GPU-Lanes (Schwelle + Oracle-Rangfolge) fahren")
    parser.add_argument("--stage", choices=("full", "lanes", "v6"),
                        default="full",
                        help="full: alles; lanes: V1-V5 (maschinen-Kontext); "
                             "v6: nur die Oracle-Rangfolge (4 Modelle) — "
                             "Zerlegung gegen die Laufzeit-Wand der "
                             "Orakel-Greedy-Zuege (kein Band geaendert)")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    env_gpu = os.environ.get("MI6_GPU", "") in ("1", "true", "True")
    want_gpu = args.gpu or env_gpu

    print("== MI-6 — Algorithmische Subjektivität über die Spiegel-Linie ==")
    print("Canon: SP8/SP12/SP13 + MI-Lens; SP14 offen -> V5 einlöst")
    print(f"Modus: {'CPU+GPU' if want_gpu else 'CPU'}")

    stufe("L0-Anker-Lane (SP11/SP12-Kommitat)")
    l0 = lane_l0()
    stufe("CPU-Piloten + Determinismus-Doppellauf")
    cpu, det = cpu_findings()
    det2 = cpu_findings()[1]
    det_equal = det == det2

    gpu: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    offen = False
    if want_gpu:
        gpu = {}
        if args.stage in ("full", "lanes"):
            try:
                stufe("GPU-Kontext laden (argon_1b + argon_1b_machines)")
                ctx = gpu_context("./argon_1b", "./argon_1b_machines")
            except Exception as exc:   # noqa: BLE001 — OFFEN, kein Fallback
                print(f"GPU-Kontext nicht verfügbar ({exc}) — "
                      "GPU-Lanes OFFEN.")
                gpu, offen = None, True
                ctx = None
            if ctx is not None:
                stufe("V1 Schwelle (d_spie-Decoder, GPU)")
                gpu["schwelle"] = lane_schwelle(ctx)
                stufe("V2 Horizont (preview-Kette unter steer-Rotation)")
                gpu["horizont"] = lane_horizont("oracle", oracle=ctx[3],
                                                steer_rotation=True)
                stufe("V3 Selbst/Fremd (GPU-Orakel)")
                gpu["selffremd"] = lane_selffremd("oracle", ctx[3], gpu=True)
                stufe("V4 Selbstmodell-Kausalität (GPU-Orakel)")
                gpu["kausal"] = lane_kausal("oracle", ctx[3], gpu=True)
                stufe("V5 Individuierung (GPU-Orakel)")
                gpu["individuierung"] = lane_individuierung("oracle", ctx[3])
                if args.stage == "full":
                    # V6 lädt 4 Modelle sequenziell — den machines-Kontext
                    # davor freiräumen (11.6/12 GiB, kein stiller Fallback).
                    model, tokenizer = ctx[1], ctx[2]
                    del ctx, model, tokenizer
                    import gc
                    gc.collect()
                    import torch
                    if torch.cuda.is_available():
                        torch.cuda.empty_cache()
                else:
                    del ctx
        if args.stage in ("full", "v6") and gpu is not None:
            stufe(f"V6 Oracle-Rangfolge ({args.stage}, 4 Checkpoints)")
            gpu["oracle_rang"] = lane_oracle_rang(gpu=True)

    rows = findings(l0, cpu, det_equal, gpu)

    print()
    print("BEFUNDE:")
    for r in rows:
        if r["assert"] is None:
            print(f"  {r['id']:<22} OFFEN")
        else:
            print(f"  {r['id']:<22} {'PASS' if r['assert'] else 'FAIL'}")
    print()
    print("NOTIZEN:")
    for r in rows:
        print(f"  - {r['id']}: {r['note']}")

    fails = [r for r in rows if r["assert"] is False]
    opened = [r for r in rows if r["assert"] is None]
    if offen:
        opened.append({"id": "gpu_kontext"})
    print()
    print(f"BILANZ: {len(rows) - len(fails) - len(opened)} PASS; "
          f"{len(fails)} FEHLGESCHLAGEN; {len(opened)} OFFEN")

    payload = {"l0": l0, "cpu": cpu, "det_equal": det_equal, "gpu": gpu,
               "findings": rows, "stage": args.stage,
               "all_pass": not fails and not opened,
               "open": [r["id"] for r in opened]}
    out_json = {"full": JSON_PATH,
                "lanes": "scratches/mi6_subjektivitaet_lanes.json",
                "v6": "scratches/mi6_subjektivitaet_v6.json"}[args.stage]
    with open(out_json, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        json.dump(payload, fh, indent=2, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False,
                  default=float)

    if fails:
        print("ROT — Bands verletzt: "
              + ", ".join(r["id"] for r in fails))
        return 1
    if opened:
        print("OFFEN — GPU-Lanes nicht gefahren (kein stiller Fallback).")
        return 2
    print("GRUEN — Subjektivität als Spiegel-Muster: Voraus-Sicht, "
          "Selbst-Scheidung, Individuierung, Kopplung.")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

"""
smoke_endogene_agency.py — SP13: kontrafaktisches Probehandeln & endogene
Agency (die Maschine erprobt ihre Subspaces selbst; Mauer 2, Nordstern-
Nachricht 2026-10-26, Task 2).

Canon: composite.py:219-301 (SP13-Sektion: observe :220-230, _snapshot
:232-241, _restore_snapshot :243-250, step_autonomous :252-301);
turing_machine.py:323-327 (SP13-Extension: "axiom_pool" :324 — expand_basis
pop(0) :149 und innerer Expand :241-246 laufen auch in Proben — und
"last_verdict" :325);
wittgenstein_machine.py:507-525 (SP13-Extension: noise_level/_schedule_index
— der S2/S3-Schedule mutiert noise_level je Batch :313-316 und zählt
_schedule_index :338 — plus das Lexikon, learn_pair ERSETZT Einträge
:232-233);
machine_base.py:502-516 (observe-Rahmen), :392-421 (steer — @contextmanager
mit exakter Bias-Restaurierung im finally), :541-545 (digest);
goedel_machine.py:163-172 (oracle-mode _prediction via oracle.hidden_state);
verify_machines.py:355-399 (GPU-Muster: _load_machines_model + ArgonOracle
+ steer-by-domain + Bias-Restore-Verifikation).

PREREG (P2-Freeze — fixiert vor dem Erstrun; Canon-Audit 2026-10-26 im
Plan ~/.claude/plans/sp13-endogene-agency.md: BaseMachine.observe existiert
bereits, TM-Snapshot fehlte axiom_pool [User-Fund], WM-Snapshot fehlte
noise_level/_schedule_index/lexicon).

Kernthese (User-Nordstern, Task 2): die Maschine handelt nicht passiv —
sie ERPROBT jeden Kandidaten-Subspace kontrafaktisch (nicht-mutierende
Proben) und wählt das Preset mit minimalem Composite-Gap. Ohne Oracle
(CPU) degeneriert die Wahl zum Tie (alle Proben identisch) — DATUM, kein
Band; die Wahl-Aussage ist eine GPU-Aussage (argon_1b_machines, oracle).

VERDICT-MAP (Bands fixiert VOR dem Erstrun):
  sp13_l0_anker — Zero-Regression: die kommittete SP11-lane (importiert)
    fields_ok/first_detect_ok/deterministic; die kommittete SP12-l0-lane
    (importiert) True/True/True; der SP12-Zwilling reproduziert die
    KOMMITTETE lexical_grounding_experiment.json FELDWEISE (verdicts a/b,
    basis_a/b, basis_b_sizes, expansions a/b, resolver_direct, batch-
    Gleichheiten — 10 Feldechecks). Falsifikator: jede abweichende Serie.
  sp13_snapshot_purity — observe()-Nebenwirkungsfreiheit: (CPU) N=5
    composite.observe("spiegel", lexical_grounding=True) im gelehrten
    Lexikon/LOOP-Trägheitszustand — Bitidentität des Vollfingerprint
    (digest, 3 RNG-States, axiom_pool, basis, expansions, noise_level,
    _schedule_index, lexicon-Vektoren, self_model, input_log,
    experiment_history, generated_rules, halt_verdicts); die Proben SEHEN
    LOOP (lexical_ast ('identity','x') je Probe) und fressen den Pool
    NICHT. Plus (CPU) 3 Proben im leeren Lexikon: alle None/HALTED.
    (GPU) dieselbe Purity unter steer-Rotation (goedel/turing/wittgenstein/
    combined/plain je Probe) inkl. expert_bias-Tensoren (26 Gate-Layer,
    torch.equal). Falsifikator: jede Nicht-Identität.
  sp13_det — Determinismus der autonomen Wahl: zwei Biografien
    (6 HALTED-Schritte → Lexikon-Spiel → WM-RNG-Pin → 6 step_autonomous
    -Schritte, seed 42) bitidentisch über chosen_preset-Sequenz,
    candidate_gaps je Schritt, gap-Serie, endgültigen Digest — CPU UND GPU.
    Falsifikator: jede Abweichung.
  sp13_endogenous_switch — (GPU-only) echte Wahl: jede der 12 Schritte
    hat ENTARTETE candidate_gaps zu vermeiden (max-minus-min > 0 je
    Schritt) UND der Anteil der Schritte mit chosen_preset != "raw"
    ist >= 0.8. Reaktion auf HALTED vs LOOP (Phase-Wechsel im Wortprogramm)
    wird als DATUM dokumentiert. Falsifikator: Degeneration ODER
    raw-Anteil > 0.2.
  sp13_gap_dominance — (GPU-only) Kohärenz-Gewinn: je autonomem Schritt
    gilt result.gap == best_gap == min(candidate_gaps) (EXAKT), und der
    Mittelwert der 12 autonomen Gaps liegt STRIKT unter dem Mittelwert des
    ungesteuerten raw-Zwillings (dieselbe Biografie ohne autonomy). Der
    CPU-Lauf dokumentiert die Tie-Degeneration als DATUM (alle Gaps
    gleich, chosen == "raw"). Falsifikator: Gap-Verletzung je Schritt
    ODER nicht strikt kleinerer Mittelwert.
  sp13_causal_loop_closed — Kopplung SP12 x SP13: nach dem Lexikon-Spiel
    sieht observe() den Wort-erzeugten LOOP voraus und restauriert
    verlustfrei (axiom_pool/basis/expansions identisch); der reale Schritt
    unter step_autonomous vollzieht die Expansion 3 -> 5:
    invariants.lexical_ast == "('identity', 'x')", verdict LOOP,
    expansions == ['step1:LOOP'], invariants["autonomous"] protokolliert
    Wahl + candidate_gaps. (GPU: zusätzlich die Wahl UNTER den Proben am
    resolvierten Schritt.) Falsifikator: Pool-Differenz nach der Vorhersage
    ODER ausbleibende Expansion ODER fehlendes autonomous-Protokoll.

Lanes: cpu (immer): l0_anker, snapshot_purity, det, causal_loop (CPU-
Varianten); gpu (hinter --gpu / SP13_GPU=1): die vollständigen 6 Verdikte
inkl. steer-Rotation und Wahl-Aussagen. Exit-Codes: 0 = alle gefahrenen
Verdikte PASS; 1 = FAIL vorhanden; 2 = OFFEN (GPU-Lanes angefordert,
aber nicht gefahren — kein stiller Fallback). Die Wahl-Aussagen (switch/
dominance) sind NUR die GPU-Aussagen; der CPU-Teil dokumentiert die Tie.

Run (CPU):  python -u -m scratches.smoke_endogene_agency
Run (GPU):  python -u -m scratches.smoke_endogene_agency --gpu
            (Checkpoints ./argon_1b + ./argon_1b_machines, Muster
            verify_machines.py:355-399)
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import sys
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

import numpy as np

from machines.composite import CompositeMachine
from machines.machine_base import MachineConfig
from scratches.smoke_lexical_grounding import (
    LEX_PAIR,
    POOL6,
    ROUNDS,
    lane_l0_anchor as sp12_lane_l0,
    lane_zwilling,
)
from scratches.smoke_paradigm_ursprung import lane_l0_anker  # kommittete SP11-lane

SEED = 42
STEPS_PHASE = 6          # 6 HALTED-Schritte + 6 autonome LOOP-Schritte = 12
N_PROBES = 5
CANDIDATES = (None, "goedel", "turing", "wittgenstein", "combined")
SP12_JSON = "scratches/lexical_grounding_experiment.json"
LOG_PATH = "scratches/endogene_agency_experiment.log"
JSON_PATH = "scratches/endogene_agency_experiment.json"
GPU_STEER_ROTATION = ("goedel", "turing", "wittgenstein", "combined", None)


def round6(x: Any) -> Any:
    return round(float(x), 6) if x is not None else None


def make_composite(mode: str = "in_loop",
                   oracle: Any = None) -> CompositeMachine:
    """Frische Composite seed 42 (in_loop oder oracle, Canon-Pool POOL6)."""
    return CompositeMachine(MachineConfig(seed=SEED, mode=mode,
                                          params=dict(POOL6)),
                            oracle=oracle)


def learn_and_pin(machine: CompositeMachine) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """SP12-Spiel + WM-RNG-Pinning (Arm-Schicht, dokumentiert)."""
    protocol = machine.wittgenstein.lexicon_game(
        [LEX_PAIR], rounds=ROUNDS, noise_level=0.0)
    machine.wittgenstein._rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED)
    protocol = [dict(entry, resonance_after=round6(entry["resonance_after"]))
                for entry in protocol]
    return protocol


def fingerprint(machine: CompositeMachine) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Vollfingerprint des Composite-Zustands (JSON-stabil)."""
    t, w, g = machine.turing, machine.wittgenstein, machine.goedel
    lex = w.tokenizer.lexicon
    return {
        "digest": machine.digest(),
        "rng": {name: json.dumps(gen._rng.bit_generator.state, sort_keys=True)
                for name, gen in (("goedel", g), ("turing", t),
                                  ("wittgenstein", w))},
        "axiom_pool": list(t.axiom_pool),
        "basis": list(t.basis),
        "expansions": list(machine.expansions),
        "step": machine._step_index,
        "noise_level": w.noise_level,
        "schedule_index": w._schedule_index,
        "lexicon": {k: np.asarray(v).tolist() for k, v in lex.items()},
        "self_model": np.abs(g.self_model).sum().item(),
        "input_log_len": len(g.input_log),
        "experiment_history_len": len(w.experiment_history),
        "generated_rules_len": len(w.generated_rules),
        "halt_verdicts": list(t.halt_verdicts),
        "transition_count": t._transition_count,
        "trace_len": len(machine._trace),
        "gap_history": [round6(x) for x in machine._gap_history],
    }


def lane_l0() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Zero-Regression: SP11-lane + SP12-lane (beide importiert) + der
    SP12-Zwilling FELDWEISE gegen die KOMMITTETE JSON."""
    l0 = lane_l0_anker()
    l0_re = lane_l0_anker()
    s12 = sp12_lane_l0()
    with open(SP12_JSON, "r", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        committed = json.load(fh)["twin"]
    twin = lane_zwilling()
    fields_equal = {
        "verdicts_a": twin["verdicts_a"] == committed["verdicts_a"],
        "verdicts_b": twin["verdicts_b"] == committed["verdicts_b"],
        "basis_a": twin["basis_a"] == committed["basis_a"],
        "basis_b": twin["basis_b"] == committed["basis_b"],
        "basis_b_sizes": twin["basis_b_sizes"] == committed["basis_b_sizes"],
        "expansions_a": twin["expansions_a"] == committed["expansions_a"],
        "expansions_b": twin["expansions_b"] == committed["expansions_b"],
        "resolver_direct": twin["resolver_direct"]
        == committed["resolver_direct"],
        "batches_ground_plain": twin["batches_equal_grounded_plain"]
        == committed["batches_equal_grounded_plain"],
        "batches_a_b": twin["batches_equal_a_b"]
        == committed["batches_equal_a_b"],
    }
    return {"sp11": {"fields_ok": bool(l0.get("fields_ok")),
                     "first_detect_ok": bool(l0.get("first_detect_ok")),
                     "deterministic": l0 == l0_re},
            "sp12_lane": {"fields_ok": bool(s12["fields_ok"]),
                          "first_detect_ok": bool(s12["first_detect_ok"]),
                          "deterministic": s12["deterministic"]},
            "sp12_twin_fields": fields_equal}


def lane_purity_cpu() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Proben im gelehrten Lexikon/LOOP-Trägheitszustand (N=5) und im
    leeren Lexikon (N=3): Bitidentität je Probe."""
    machine = make_composite()
    learn_and_pin(machine)
    machine.step("spiegel", lexical_grounding=True)         # LOOP-Trägheit
    fp0 = fingerprint(machine)
    b_state: Dict[str, Any] = {"probes": [], "purity": True, "diffs": []}
    for i in range(N_PROBES):
        probe = machine.observe("spiegel", lexical_grounding=True)
        fpN = fingerprint(machine)
        same = fpN == fp0
        b_state["purity"] = b_state["purity"] and same
        b_state["probes"].append({"n": i + 1,
                                  "verdict": probe.invariants["verdict"],
                                  "lexical_ast": probe.invariants["lexical_ast"],
                                  "purity": same})
        if not same:
            b_state["diffs"].append({"n": i + 1, "keys": sorted(
                k for k in fp0 if fp0[k] != fpN[k])})
    plain = make_composite()
    fpA = fingerprint(plain)
    a_state: Dict[str, Any] = {"probes": [], "purity": True}
    for i in range(3):
        probe = plain.observe("spiegel", lexical_grounding=True)
        same = fingerprint(plain) == fpA
        a_state["purity"] = a_state["purity"] and same
        a_state["probes"].append({"n": i + 1,
                                  "verdict": probe.invariants["verdict"],
                                  "lexical_ast": probe.invariants["lexical_ast"],
                                  "purity": same})
    return {"b_state": b_state, "a_state": a_state,
            "axiom_pool_unchanged": b_state["probes"][0]["purity"]}


def cpu_bio(steps_phase: int = STEPS_PHASE) -> Tuple[CompositeMachine, Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Biografie: 6 ungesteuerte Schritte -> Lexikon-Spiel -> Pin ->
    6 autonome Schritte (CPU: Tie-Degeneration)."""
    machine = make_composite()
    halted: List[str] = [machine.step("spiegel",
                                      lexical_grounding=False).invariants["verdict"]
                         for _ in range(steps_phase)]
    learn_and_pin(machine)
    auto_rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for _ in range(steps_phase):
        result = machine.step_autonomous("spiegel",
                                         candidates=CANDIDATES,
                                         lexical_grounding=True)
        auto_rows.append({"verdict": result.invariants["verdict"],
                          "lexical_ast": result.invariants["lexical_ast"],
                          "chosen_preset": result.invariants["autonomous"]
                          ["chosen_preset"],
                          "candidate_gaps": {k: round6(v) for k, v in
                                             result.invariants["autonomous"]
                                             ["candidate_gaps"].items()},
                          "gap": round6(result.gap)})
    meta = {"halted_verdicts": halted, "autonomous": auto_rows}
    return machine, meta


def lane_det_cpu() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    m1, bio1 = cpu_bio()
    m2, bio2 = cpu_bio()
    equal = bio1 == bio2
    return {"deterministic": equal,
            "digest_equal": m1.digest() == m2.digest(),
            "expansions_equal": m1.expansions == m2.expansions,
            "tie_datum": all(r["chosen_preset"] == "raw"
                             for r in bio1["autonomous"])}


def lane_causal_cpu() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """SP12 x SP13 (CPU): Voraus-Sicht + verlustfreie Restaurierung +
    reale Expansion 3 -> 5."""
    machine = make_composite()
    learn_and_pin(machine)
    fp0 = fingerprint(machine)
    probe = machine.observe("spiegel", lexical_grounding=True)
    fp1 = fingerprint(machine)
    seen = {"probe_verdict": probe.invariants["verdict"],
            "probe_lexical_ast": probe.invariants["lexical_ast"],
            "pool_restored": fp1["axiom_pool"] == fp0["axiom_pool"],
            "basis_restored": fp1["basis"] == fp0["basis"],
            "expand_restored": fp1["expansions"] == fp0["expansions"],
            "fingerprint_restored": fp1 == fp0}
    real = machine.step_autonomous("spiegel", candidates=CANDIDATES,
                                   lexical_grounding=True)
    return {"seen": seen,
            "real": {"verdict": real.invariants["verdict"],
                     "lexical_ast": real.invariants["lexical_ast"],
                     "basis_after": len(machine.turing.state()["basis"]),
                     "expansions": list(machine.expansions),
                     "autonomous": result_autonomous(real)}}


def result_autonomous(result: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """RAW-Fließkommawerte (die EXAKT-Bänder vergleichen ungerundete
    Floats; gerundete Sichten nur für die Anzeige)."""
    au = result.invariants.get("autonomous", {})
    return {"chosen_preset": au.get("chosen_preset"),
            "best_gap": au.get("best_gap"),
            "candidate_gaps": dict(au.get("candidate_gaps", {}))}


# -- GPU-Lanes ---------------------------------------------------------------

def gpu_context(checkpoint_base: str, checkpoint_machines: str):
    """Argon-Orakel-Kontext (Muster verify_machines.py:355-399)."""
    import torch

    from machines.machine_base import ArgonOracle
    from machines.verify_machines import _load_machines_model

    device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    model, tokenizer = _load_machines_model(checkpoint_base, checkpoint_machines)
    oracle = ArgonOracle(model, tokenizer, device=device)
    return torch, model, tokenizer, oracle, device


def bias_fingerprint(torch: Any, model: Any) -> List[Any]:
    return [layer.moe.gate.expert_bias.data.cpu().clone()
            for layer in model.model.layers]


def bias_equal(torch: Any, before: List[Any], after: List[Any]) -> bool:
    return all(torch.equal(a, b) for a, b in zip(before, after))


def lane_purity_gpu(ctx) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Purity UNTER steer-Rotation: je Probe ein anderer Kontext (incl.
    plain); Maschinen-Fingerprint + expert_bias-Tensoren bitidentisch."""
    torch, model, _tok, oracle, _dev = ctx
    machine = make_composite(mode="oracle", oracle=oracle)
    learn_and_pin(machine)
    machine.step("spiegel", lexical_grounding=True)
    fp0 = fingerprint(machine)
    b0 = bias_fingerprint(torch, model)
    probes: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    purity = True
    bias_ok = True
    for i, preset in enumerate(GPU_STEER_ROTATION):
        label = "raw" if preset is None else preset
        if preset is None:
            probe = machine.observe("spiegel", lexical_grounding=True)
        else:
            with oracle.steer(preset):
                probe = machine.observe("spiegel", lexical_grounding=True)
        fpN = fingerprint(machine)
        same = fpN == fp0
        b_ok = bias_equal(torch, b0, bias_fingerprint(torch, model))
        purity = purity and same
        bias_ok = bias_ok and b_ok
        probes.append({"n": i + 1, "preset": label,
                       "verdict": probe.invariants["verdict"],
                       "lexical_ast": probe.invariants["lexical_ast"],
                       "purity": same, "bias_restored": b_ok})
        if not same:
            probes[-1]["diff_keys"] = sorted(k for k in fp0 if fp0[k] != fpN[k])
    return {"probes": probes, "purity": purity, "expert_bias_restored": bias_ok}


def gpu_bio(ctx, autonomous: bool) -> Tuple[CompositeMachine, Dict[str, Any]]:
    """GPU-Biografie: 6 ungesteuerte Schritte -> Lexikon-Spiel -> Pin ->
    6 Schritte als step_autonomous (oder als plain, raw-Zwilling)."""
    _torch, _model, _tok, oracle, _dev = ctx
    machine = make_composite(mode="oracle", oracle=oracle)
    halted: List[str] = [machine.step("spiegel",
                                      lexical_grounding=False).invariants["verdict"]
                         for _ in range(STEPS_PHASE)]
    learn_and_pin(machine)
    rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for _ in range(STEPS_PHASE):
        if autonomous:
            result = machine.step_autonomous("spiegel", candidates=CANDIDATES,
                                             lexical_grounding=True)
            au = result_autonomous(result)
        else:
            result = machine.step("spiegel", lexical_grounding=True)
            au = None
        rows.append({"verdict": result.invariants["verdict"],
                     "lexical_ast": result.invariants["lexical_ast"],
                     "autonomous": au, "gap": float(result.gap)})
    meta = {"halted_verdicts": halted, "loop_phase": rows}
    return machine, meta


def lane_autonomy_gpu(ctx) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Die Wahl-Aussagen (switch + dominance) + det-Doppellauf GPU."""
    m1, bio1 = gpu_bio(ctx, autonomous=True)
    m2, bio2 = gpu_bio(ctx, autonomous=True)
    det = bio1 == bio2 and m1.digest() == m2.digest()
    rows = bio1["loop_phase"]
    spread_all = True
    non_raw = 0
    dominance_exact = True
    for r in rows:
        au = r["autonomous"]
        values = [float(v) for v in au["candidate_gaps"].values()]
        # EXAKT (ungerundete Floats): spread > 0 und
        # result.gap == best_gap == min(candidate_gaps).
        spread_all = spread_all and (max(values) - min(values) > 0.0)
        if au["chosen_preset"] != "raw":
            non_raw += 1
        if not (r["gap"] == au["best_gap"]
                and r["gap"] == min(values)):
            dominance_exact = False
    _twin, raw_bio = gpu_bio(ctx, autonomous=False)
    raw_gaps = [r["gap"] for r in raw_bio["loop_phase"]]
    auto_gaps = [r["gap"] for r in rows]
    mean_auto = sum(auto_gaps) / len(auto_gaps)
    mean_raw = sum(raw_gaps) / len(raw_gaps)
    dominant = mean_auto < mean_raw
    return {"det_doppellauf": det,
            "spread_pro_schritt": spread_all,
            "non_raw_anteil": non_raw / len(rows),
            "mean_auto": mean_auto, "mean_raw": mean_raw,
            "dominant_strikt": dominant,
            "gap_dominance": dominance_exact and dominant,
            "bio": bio1, "raw_bio": raw_bio}


def lane_causal_gpu(ctx) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """SP12 x SP13 (GPU): Voraus-Sicht + Expansion 3 -> 5 UNTER der Wahl."""
    _torch, _model, _tok, oracle, _dev = ctx
    machine = make_composite(mode="oracle", oracle=oracle)
    learn_and_pin(machine)
    fp0 = fingerprint(machine)
    probe = machine.observe("spiegel", lexical_grounding=True)
    fp1 = fingerprint(machine)
    seen = {"probe_verdict": probe.invariants["verdict"],
            "probe_lexical_ast": probe.invariants["lexical_ast"],
            "fingerprint_restored": fp1 == fp0}
    real = machine.step_autonomous("spiegel", candidates=CANDIDATES,
                                   lexical_grounding=True)
    return {"seen": seen,
            "real": {"verdict": real.invariants["verdict"],
                     "lexical_ast": real.invariants["lexical_ast"],
                     "basis_after": len(machine.turing.state()["basis"]),
                     "expansions": list(machine.expansions),
                     "autonomous": result_autonomous(real)}}


def findings(l0: Dict[str, Any], purity: Dict[str, Any], det: Dict[str, Any],
             causal: Dict[str, Any], gpu: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    ok_l0 = (l0["sp11"]["fields_ok"] and l0["sp11"]["first_detect_ok"]
             and l0["sp11"]["deterministic"]
             and l0["sp12_lane"]["fields_ok"]
             and l0["sp12_lane"]["first_detect_ok"]
             and l0["sp12_lane"]["deterministic"]
             and l0["sp12_twin_fields"] is not None
             and all(l0["sp12_twin_fields"].values()))
    rows.append({"id": "sp13_l0_anker",
                 "data": {"sp11": l0["sp11"], "sp12_lane": l0["sp12_lane"],
                          "twin_fields": l0["sp12_twin_fields"]},
                 "assert": ok_l0,
                 "note": ("Zero-Regression über importierte committete Lanes "
                          "+ feldeidentischer SP12-Zwilling")})

    ok_purity = (purity["b_state"]["purity"]
                 and purity["a_state"]["purity"]
                 and all(p["verdict"] == "LOOP" for p in purity["b_state"]["probes"])
                 and all(p["lexical_ast"] == "('identity', 'x')"
                         for p in purity["b_state"]["probes"])
                 and all(p["verdict"] == "HALTED"
                         for p in purity["a_state"]["probes"]))
    if gpu and "purity" in gpu:
        gp = gpu["purity"]
        ok_purity = ok_purity and gp["purity"] and gp["expert_bias_restored"]
    rows.append({"id": "sp13_snapshot_purity",
                 "data": {"b_state_probes": purity["b_state"]["probes"],
                          "a_state_purity": purity["a_state"]["purity"],
                          "gpu_purity": (gpu["purity"]["purity"]
                                         and gpu["purity"]["expert_bias_restored"])
                          if gpu and "purity" in gpu else None},
                 "assert": ok_purity,
                 "note": ("Vorblick sieht LOOP, fressen der axiom_pool NICHT "
                          "(User-Fund Turing-Snapshot); GPU: Bias-Tensoren "
                          "unter steer-Rotation restauriert")})

    ok_det = det["deterministic"] and det["digest_equal"]
    if gpu and "autonomy" in gpu:
        ok_det = ok_det and gpu["autonomy"]["det_doppellauf"]
    rows.append({"id": "sp13_det",
                 "data": {"cpu": {"deterministic": det["deterministic"],
                                  "digest_equal": det["digest_equal"],
                                  "tie_datum": det["tie_datum"]},
                          "gpu_det": gpu["autonomy"]["det_doppellauf"]
                          if gpu and "autonomy" in gpu else None},
                 "assert": ok_det,
                 "note": ("Autonome Läufe bitidentisch; CPU dokumentiert die "
                          "Tie-Degeneration als DATUM")})

    causal_ok = (causal["seen"]["probe_verdict"] == "LOOP"
                 and causal["seen"]["probe_lexical_ast"] == "('identity', 'x')"
                 and causal["seen"]["fingerprint_restored"]
                 and causal["real"]["verdict"] == "LOOP"
                 and causal["real"]["lexical_ast"] == "('identity', 'x')"
                 and causal["real"]["basis_after"] == 5
                 and causal["real"]["expansions"] == ["step1:LOOP"])
    if gpu and "causal" in gpu:
        g = gpu["causal"]
        causal_ok = causal_ok and g["seen"]["fingerprint_restored"] \
            and g["seen"]["probe_verdict"] == "LOOP" \
            and g["real"]["basis_after"] == 5 \
            and g["real"]["expansions"] == ["step1:LOOP"] \
            and g["real"]["autonomous"]["chosen_preset"] is not None
    rows.append({"id": "sp13_causal_loop_closed",
                 "data": {"cpu_seen": causal["seen"],
                          "cpu_real": causal["real"],
                          "gpu_seen": gpu["causal"]["seen"]
                          if gpu and "causal" in gpu else None,
                          "gpu_real": gpu["causal"]["real"]
                          if gpu and "causal" in gpu else None},
                 "assert": causal_ok,
                 "note": ("Wort -> Programm -> Voraus-Sicht -> Expansion 3->5 "
                          "unter der Wahl (SP12 x SP13 gekoppelt)")})

    if gpu and "autonomy" in gpu:
        au = gpu["autonomy"]
        ok_switch = au["spread_pro_schritt"] and au["non_raw_anteil"] >= 0.8
        rows.append({"id": "sp13_endogenous_switch",
                     "data": {"non_raw_anteil": au["non_raw_anteil"],
                              "spread_pro_schritt": au["spread_pro_schritt"]},
                     "assert": ok_switch,
                     "note": ("Echte Wahl statt Einheits-Preset; HALTED/LOOP-"
                              "Reaktion als Datums in bio1 geliefert")})
        ok_dom = au["gap_dominance"]
        rows.append({"id": "sp13_gap_dominance",
                     "data": {"mean_auto": au["mean_auto"],
                              "mean_raw": au["mean_raw"],
                              "det_doppellauf": au["det_doppellauf"]},
                     "assert": ok_dom,
                     "note": ("g_auto(n) == best_gap == min(candidate_gaps) "
                              "je Schritt exakt; Mittelwert strikt unter raw")})
    else:
        rows.append({"id": "sp13_endogenous_switch",
                     "data": {}, "assert": None,
                     "note": "GPU-Lane OFFEN (ohne --gpu nicht gefahren)"})
        rows.append({"id": "sp13_gap_dominance",
                     "data": {}, "assert": None,
                     "note": "GPU-Lane OFFEN (ohne --gpu nicht gefahren)"})
    return rows


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
    parser.add_argument("--gpu", action="store_true",
                        help="GPU-Lanes (argon_1b_machines oracle-mode) fahren")
    parser.add_argument("--checkpoint_base", default="./argon_1b")
    parser.add_argument("--checkpoint_machines", default="./argon_1b_machines")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    env_gpu = os.environ.get("SP13_GPU", "") in ("1", "true", "True")
    want_gpu = args.gpu or env_gpu

    print("== SP13 — Endogene Agency: kontrafaktisches Probehandeln ==")
    print("Canon: composite.py:219-301, turing_machine.py:323-327,")
    print("       wittgenstein_machine.py:507-525, machine_base.py:502-516")
    print("Prereg: N_PROBES=5, STEPS_PHASE=6+6, seed 42; Wahl = argmin Composite-Gap")
    print(f"Modus: {'CPU+GPU' if want_gpu else 'CPU'}")

    l0 = lane_l0()
    purity = lane_purity_cpu()
    det = lane_det_cpu()
    causal = lane_causal_cpu()

    gpu: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    offen = False
    if want_gpu:
        try:
            ctx = gpu_context(args.checkpoint_base, args.checkpoint_machines)
        except Exception as exc:   # noqa: BLE001 — OFFEN, kein stiller Fallback
            print(f"GPU-Kontext nicht verfügbar ({exc}) — GPU-Lanes OFFEN.")
            gpu, offen = None, True
        else:
            gpu = {"purity": lane_purity_gpu(ctx),
                   "autonomy": lane_autonomy_gpu(ctx),
                   "causal": lane_causal_gpu(ctx)}

    rows = findings(l0, purity, det, causal, gpu)

    print()
    print("BEFUNDE:")
    for r in rows:
        if r["assert"] is None:
            print(f"  {r['id']:<26} OFFEN")
        else:
            print(f"  {r['id']:<26} {'PASS' if r['assert'] else 'FAIL'}")
    print()
    print("NOTIZEN:")
    for r in rows:
        print(f"  - {r['id']}: {r['note']}")

    fails = [r for r in rows if r["assert"] is False]
    opened = [r for r in rows if r["assert"] is None]
    if offen:
        opened.append({"id": "gpu_kontext", "note": "GPU-Kontext fehlgeschlagen"})
    print()
    print(f"BILANZ: {len(rows) - len(fails) - len(opened)} PASS; "
          f"{len(fails)} FEHLGESCHLAGEN; {len(opened)} OFFEN"
          f"{', unverwartet' if fails else ''}")
    print("CPU-Tie-Datum: ohne Oracle sind alle Kandidaten-Gaps identisch;")
    print("die Wahl-Aussagen sind die GPU-Aussagen (oracle-mode).")
    print(f"JSON: {JSON_PATH}")

    payload = {"l0": l0, "purity": purity, "det": det, "causal": causal,
               "gpu": gpu, "findings": rows,
               "all_pass": not fails and not opened, "open": [r["id"] for r in opened]}
    with open(JSON_PATH, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        json.dump(payload, fh, indent=2, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False)

    if fails:
        print("ROT — Bands verletzt (Daten, kein Band-Schieben): "
              + ", ".join(r["id"] for r in fails))
        return 1
    if opened:
        print("OFFEN — GPU-Lanes nicht gefahren (kein stiller Fallback).")
        return 2
    print("GRUEN — Endogene Agency: die Maschine waehlt ihren Subspace selbst.")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

Kernthese (User-Nordstern, Task 1): sozial gelernte Wörter übersetzen sich

VERDICT-MAP (Bands fixiert VOR dem Erstrun):

    liefert fields_ok + first_detect_ok; das Doppel-Replay ist bitidentisch.
    Falsifikator: jede abweichende Feld-Serie.
  sp12_det — Zwilling-Doppel-Läufe (Arm A ×2, Arm B ×2) bitidentisch.
    Falsifikator: jede Abweichung.
  sp12_t1_semantik — (a) alle 5 Lernrunden learned=True (noise_level=0.0,
    Canon-Arg lexicon_game für Lern-Sicherheit); (b) resonance_after[4] >=
    0.85; (c) resolve_lexical_ast("spiegel") == ('identity', 'x');
    (d) NACH-Lexikon-Kandidaten == ["identity"]; (e) B-Schritt-1
    invariants.lexical_ast == "('identity', 'x')".
    Falsifikator: jede Bandverletzung (kein tau-Schieben).
  sp12_t2_rng_reinheit — generierte Batches TRIPLE-identisch (Arm A
    grounded, Arm A' plain, Arm B mit WM-RNG-Pinning nach den 5 Lernrunden):
    batch_asts + rule_names + w_series je Schritt gleich. Die Resolution
    ist reine Lexikon-Lesung — sie konsumiert KEINE RNG-Züge.
    Falsifikator: jede Batch-Differenz.
  sp12_t3_scheidung — (a) Arm A lexical_ast == None an JEDEM Schritt
    (kein Klang-Auslösen über den SH-72-Fallback); (b) Arm A == Arm A'
    (opt-in-Default-Invarianz im leeren Lexikon); (c) Klang-Dach als DATEN
    (formel_max/formel_argmax — SP2-Messkonvention, kein Band).
    Falsifikator: Verletzung von (a) oder (b).
  sp12_t4_verdikt — Arm A: ALLE Verdikte HALTED, final_basis == 3,
    expansions == []; Arm B: Verdikte[0:2] == [LOOP, LOOP]; Basis nach
    Schritt 1 == 5 (User-Pass „Basis auf 5"), nach Schritt 2 == 7
    (+2 je LOOP: Turing-innerer Expand :241-246 + Composite :132-134);
    expansions[0:2] == ['step1:LOOP', 'step2:LOOP']; Schritte 3-6 als

    Falsifikator: Arm-A-Expansion, Arm-B-Verdikt != LOOP am resolvierten

Lanes: l0_anker (Regression), det, zwilling (A/A'/B, seed 42, 6 Schritte,
obj="spiegel" je Schritt). CPU-only; kein Maschinen-Code-Zugriff über die
harness-Ebene hinaus (die SP12-Extension der Maschinen ist committet und
durch lane_l0_anker regressions-geprüft).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Tuple

import numpy as np

from machines.composite import CompositeMachine
from machines.machine_base import MachineConfig
from machines.wittgenstein_machine import SymbolicOperator
from scratches.smoke_paradigm_ursprung import lane_l0_anker

SEED = 42
STEPS = 6
TAU = 0.85
ROUNDS = 5
LEX_PAIR = ("spiegel", "identity")
POOL6 = {"axiom_pool": ["ALPHA", "BETA", "GAMMA", "DELTA", "EPSILON",
                        "ZETA"]}
LOG_PATH = "scratches/lexical_grounding_experiment.log"
JSON_PATH = "scratches/lexical_grounding_experiment.json"


def round6(x: Any) -> Any:
    return round(float(x), 6) if x is not None else None


def make_composite() -> CompositeMachine:
    """Frischer composite seed 42 (in_loop, Canon-Pool POOL6)."""
    return CompositeMachine(MachineConfig(seed=SEED, mode="in_loop",
                                          params=dict(POOL6)))


def run_lexical(machine: CompositeMachine, word: str, steps: int,
                lexical: bool = True) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Verlauf mit je-Schritt-Attribution: lexikale Resolution, generierter
    Batch-AST (RNG-Marker für T2), Verdikt, Basis-Größe, Sub-Gaps."""
    rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    wm = machine.wittgenstein
    for step in range(1, steps + 1):
        rules_before = len(wm.generated_rules)
        st = machine.step(word, lexical_grounding=lexical)
        inv = st.invariants
        batch_ast = (str(wm.generated_rules[rules_before].ast)
                     if len(wm.generated_rules) > rules_before else None)
        t_state = machine.turing.state()
        rows.append({
            "step": step,
            "step_index": st.step,
            "lexical_word": inv["lexical_word"],
            "lexical_ast": inv["lexical_ast"],
            "batch_ast": batch_ast,
            "rule_name": inv["rule_name"],
            "verdict": inv["verdict"],
            "transitions": int(inv["transitions"]),
            "basis_size": len(t_state["basis"]),
            "gap": round6(st.gap),
            "w_gap": round6(inv["submachines"]["wittgenstein"]["gap"]),
            "t_gap": round6(inv["submachines"]["turing"]["gap"]),
            "g_gap": round6(inv["submachines"]["goedel"]["gap"]),
        })
    return {"rows": rows,
            "verdicts": [r["verdict"] for r in rows],
            "lexical_asts": [r["lexical_ast"] for r in rows],
            "batch_asts": [r["batch_ast"] for r in rows],
            "rule_names": [r["rule_name"] for r in rows],
            "w_series": [r["w_gap"] for r in rows],
            "basis_sizes": [r["basis_size"] for r in rows],
            "final_basis": rows[-1]["basis_size"],
            "expansions": list(machine.expansions)}


def resonance_profile(machine: CompositeMachine) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Klang-Raum-Messung (DATA — Klang-Dach, SP2-Konvention) plus die
    NACH-Spiel-Lexikon-Resonanz."""
    tok = machine.wittgenstein.tokenizer
    word = LEX_PAIR[0]
    k, idx, kind = tok.word_formula(word)
    base = tok.embed_word(word)
    formel = {op: round6(tok.resonance(base, tok.embed_word(op)))
              for op in SymbolicOperator.UNARY + SymbolicOperator.BINARY}
    lexikon_ops = [op for op in SymbolicOperator.UNARY + SymbolicOperator.BINARY
                   if op in tok.lexicon]
    lexikon = {op: round6(tok.resonance(tok.embed_word(word),
                                        tok.embed_word(op)))
               for op in lexikon_ops}
    return {
        "spiegel_formula": {"k": k, "idx": idx, "kind": kind},
        "formel_achsen": formel,
        "formel_max": max(formel.values()),
        "formel_argmax": max(formel, key=formel.get),
        "lexikon_woerter": sorted(tok.lexicon.keys()),
        "lexikon_kandidaten": lexikon_ops,
        "lexikon_resonanzen": lexikon,
    }


def arm_a_grounded() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Arm A: Wort OHNE gelernte Bedeutung — Resolver None (kein Klang-
    Auslösen), normale Kette."""
    machine = make_composite()
    run = run_lexical(machine, "spiegel", STEPS, lexical=True)
    run["profile"] = resonance_profile(machine)
    return run


def arm_a_plain() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Arm A': OHNE lexical_grounding — opt-in-Default-Invarianz."""
    machine = make_composite()
    return run_lexical(machine, "spiegel", STEPS, lexical=False)


def arm_b_after_learning() -> Tuple[Dict[str, Any], Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Arm B: 5 Lernrunden (noise_level=0.0 — Canon-Arg für Lern-Sicherheit),
    DANACH WM-RNG-Pinning (Arm-Schicht: default_rng(42) — identisch zum
    reset-Body), dann der Verlauf: das gelernte Wort wird Programm."""
    machine = make_composite()
    protocol = machine.wittgenstein.lexicon_game(
        [LEX_PAIR], rounds=ROUNDS, noise_level=0.0)
    profile_b = resonance_profile(machine)
    machine.wittgenstein._rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED)
    run = run_lexical(machine, "spiegel", STEPS, lexical=True)
    return run, {"protocol": protocol,
                 "resonance_after": [e["resonance_after"] for e in protocol],
                 "learned": [e["learned"] for e in protocol],
                 "profile_b": profile_b}


def lane_l0_anchor() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """machines-Edit-Regression über die kommittete SP11-Lane (die
    committete Feldvergleich-Mechanik, unverändert)."""
    l0 = lane_l0_anker()
    l0_re = lane_l0_anker()
    return {"result": l0,
            "fields_ok": bool(l0.get("fields_ok")),
            "first_detect_ok": bool(l0.get("first_detect_ok")),
            "deterministic": l0 == l0_re}


def lane_det(arm_a: Dict[str, Any], arm_b_run: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Doppel-Läufe: Arm A und Arm B je frisch reproduziert, bitidentisch."""
    a_re = arm_a_grounded()
    b_re, _b_meta_re = arm_b_after_learning()
    return {"arm_a_deterministic": a_re == arm_a,
            "arm_b_deterministic": b_re == arm_b_run}


def lane_zwilling() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    a = arm_a_grounded()
    a_plain = arm_a_plain()
    b, b_meta = arm_b_after_learning()
    m_direct = make_composite()
    m_direct.wittgenstein.lexicon_game([LEX_PAIR], rounds=ROUNDS,
                                       noise_level=0.0)
    resolver_direct = m_direct.wittgenstein.resolve_lexical_ast("spiegel")
    return {
        "arm_a": a, "arm_a_plain": a_plain, "arm_b": b, "arm_b_meta": b_meta,
        "resolver_direct": str(resolver_direct),
        "verdicts_a": a["verdicts"], "verdicts_b": b["verdicts"],
        "basis_a": a["final_basis"], "basis_b": b["final_basis"],
        "basis_b_sizes": b["basis_sizes"],
        "expansions_a": a["expansions"], "expansions_b": b["expansions"],
        "batches_equal_grounded_plain": (
            a["batch_asts"] == a_plain["batch_asts"]
            and a["rule_names"] == a_plain["rule_names"]
            and a["w_series"] == a_plain["w_series"]),
        "batches_equal_a_b": (
            a["batch_asts"] == b["batch_asts"]
            and a["rule_names"] == b["rule_names"]
            and a["w_series"] == b["w_series"]),
    }


def findings(twin: Dict[str, Any], det: Dict[str, Any],
             l0: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    a = twin["arm_a"]
    apr = twin["arm_a_plain"]
    b = twin["arm_b"]
    meta = twin["arm_b_meta"]
    prof = a["profile"]

    # --- sp12_l0_anker ---
    rows.append({"id": "sp12_l0_anker",
                 "data": {"fields_ok": l0["fields_ok"],
                          "first_detect_ok": l0["first_detect_ok"],
                          "deterministic": l0["deterministic"]},
                 "assert": (l0["fields_ok"] and l0["first_detect_ok"]
                            and l0["deterministic"]),
                 "note": ("machines-Regression über die kommittete SP11-Lane — "
                          "die committeten Feld-Serien bleiben bitidentisch")})

    # --- sp12_det ---
    rows.append({"id": "sp12_det",
                 "data": {"arm_a": det["arm_a_deterministic"],
                          "arm_b": det["arm_b_deterministic"]},
                 "assert": det["arm_a_deterministic"]
                 and det["arm_b_deterministic"],
                 "note": "Zwilling-Doppel-Läufe bitidentisch"})

    # --- sp12_t1_semantik ---
    res_serie = meta["resonance_after"]
    learned_all = all(meta["learned"])
    nach_kandidaten = meta["profile_b"]["lexikon_kandidaten"]
    nach_resonanz = meta["profile_b"]["lexikon_resonanzen"]
    resolver_direct = twin["resolver_direct"]
    rows.append({"id": "sp12_t1_semantik",
                 "data": {"learned": meta["learned"],
                          "resonance_after": res_serie,
                          "resonance_final": res_serie[-1],
                          "resolver_direct": resolver_direct,
                          "nach_kandidaten": nach_kandidaten,
                          "nach_resonanzen": nach_resonanz},
                 "assert": (learned_all
                            and res_serie[-1] >= TAU
                            and resolver_direct == "('identity', 'x')"
                            and nach_kandidaten == ["identity"]
                            and float(nach_resonanz.get("identity", 0.0)) >= TAU
                            and b["rows"][0]["lexical_ast"]
                            == "('identity', 'x')"),
                 "note": ("5 Lernrunden (noise_level=0.0) — Gebrauchs-Achse "
                          "resonance_after[-1] >= tau; der Resolver löst "
                          "direkt UND im Lauf zu ('identity', 'x') auf")})

    # --- sp12_t2_rng_reinheit ---
    rows.append({"id": "sp12_t2_rng_reinheit",
                 "data": {"batches_a_plain": twin["batches_equal_grounded_plain"],
                          "batches_a_b": twin["batches_equal_a_b"]},

                            and twin["batches_equal_a_b"]),
                 "note": ("Batch-ASTs + rule_names + w_series je Schritt "
                          "identisch in A/A'/B — die Resolution ist reine "

def main() -> int:
    print("== SP12 — Lexical Grounding: das gelernte Wort wird Programm ==")
    print("Canon: composite.py:108-140/:66-92, turing_machine.py:144-157/:241-246,")
    print("       wittgenstein_machine.py:49-50/:152-246/:342-381")
    print("Prereg tau = 0.85; 5 Lernrunden, noise_level 0.0; seed 42; 6 Schritte")

    l0 = lane_l0_anchor()
    twin = lane_zwilling()
    det = lane_det(twin["arm_a"], twin["arm_b"])
    rows = findings(twin, det, l0)

    print()
    print("BEFUNDE:")
    for r in rows:
        print(f"  {r['id']:<26} {'PASS' if r['assert'] else 'FAIL'}")
    print()
    print("NOTIZEN:")
    for r in rows:
        print(f"  - {r['id']}: {r['note']}; "
              + "; ".join(f"{k}={v}" for k, v in r["data"].items()))

    fails = [r for r in rows if not r["assert"]]
    print()
    b = twin["arm_b"]
    print("Zwilling (seed 42, 6 Schritte, obj='spiegel'):")
    print("  Arm A verdicts:", twin["verdicts_a"], "| final_basis",
          twin["basis_a"], "| expansions", twin["expansions_a"])

          b["rows"][0]["lexical_ast"])
    print("  resonance_after:", twin["arm_b_meta"]["resonance_after"])
    print()

          f"{', unverwartet' if fails else ''}; 0 OFFEN")
    print(f"JSON: {JSON_PATH}")

    payload = {"l0": l0, "twin": twin, "det": det, "findings": rows,
               "all_pass": not fails, "open": []}

              + ", ".join(r["id"] for r in fails))
        return 1
    print("GRUEN — Semantische Kausalität geschlossen (das gelernte Wort wird Programm).")
    return 0

    sys.exit(main())
