def mark(name: str, state: str, note: str = "") -> None:
    """Verdikt-Register: state in {PASS, FAIL, OFFEN}."""
    FINDINGS.append((name, state))
    if note:
        NOTES.append(f"{name}: {note}")


def round6(x: Any) -> float:
    return round(float(x), 6)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Canon-Replikation des Signals (composite.py:167-182 / goedel:229-239)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def signal_from_history(history: Sequence[float]
                        ) -> Tuple[Optional[float], bool]:
    """Exakte Replikation: am Beobachtungsschritt sieht das COMPOSITE-Signal
    die Serie der Schritte 1..n-1 (Append nach der Prüfung, :143); das
    GOEDEL-Signal die Serie 1..n (Goedel.step läuft vor :135)."""
    if len(history) < 10:
        return None, False
    recent = float(np.mean(history[-5:]))
    baseline = (float(np.mean(history[-20:-5])) if len(history) >= 20
                else float(np.mean(history[:-5])))
    ratio = recent / (baseline + GAP_EPS)
    return ratio, bool(ratio > THRESHOLD)


def replicate_flags(series: Sequence[float], offset: int) -> List[Tuple[Optional[float], bool]]:
    """(ratio, detected) je Beobachtungsschritt n=1..len(series);
    offset der Anzahl Gaps, die das Signal im Schritt N schon sieht:
    Composite-Signal: offset 0 (sieht Serie bis n-1), Goedel-Signal: offset 1."""
    out: List[Tuple[Optional[float], bool]] = []
    for n in range(1, len(series) + 1):
        history = series[:n - 1 + offset]
        out.append(signal_from_history(history))
    return out


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Harness (SP10-Muster, seedy generalisiert)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def make_composite(seed: int, force_ast: Tuple[Any, ...]
                   ) -> Tuple[CompositeMachine, Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Composite mit Zwangs-Haken am composite-step-Zähler (pending-Flag):
    die ERSTE Regel des Batch wird am Zwangsschritt durch force_ast ersetzt —
    ohne eine Zeile Maschinen-Code (wm.create_rule-Abdeckung, SP10-Muster)."""
    machine = CompositeMachine(MachineConfig(seed=seed, mode="in_loop",
                                             params=dict(POOL6)))
    harness: Dict[str, Any] = {"pending": False}
    wm = machine.wittgenstein
    original = wm.create_rule

    def patched() -> GeneratedSymbolicRule:
        if harness["pending"]:
            harness["pending"] = False
            rule = GeneratedSymbolicRule(force_ast)
            wm.generated_rules.append(rule)
            return rule
        return original()

    wm.create_rule = patched
    return machine, harness


def run_composite(machine: CompositeMachine, harness: Dict[str, Any],
                  steps: int, force_steps: "set[int]") -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Verlauf mit je-Schritt-Attribution: Sub-Gaps, beide Signale (goedel /
    composite), neue expansions-Einträge, Basis-Größe."""
    rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    trace: List[str] = []
    for step in range(1, steps + 1):
        if step in force_steps:
            harness["pending"] = True
        pre_expansions = list(machine.expansions)
        st = machine.step(None)
        inv = st.invariants
        new_exp = [e for e in machine.expansions if e not in pre_expansions]
        trace.append(st.trace[-1])
        rows.append({
            "step": step,
            "rule_name": inv["rule_name"],
            "verdict": inv["verdict"],
            "transitions": int(inv["transitions"]),
            "t_count": int(machine.turing.state()["transition_count"]),
            "gap": float(st.gap),
            "w_gap": float(inv["submachines"]["wittgenstein"]["gap"]),
            "t_gap": float(inv["submachines"]["turing"]["gap"]),
            "g_gap": float(inv["submachines"]["goedel"]["gap"]),
            "g_ratio": (float(inv["paradigm_signal"]["ratio"])
                        if inv["paradigm_signal"]["ratio"] is not None else None),
            "g_detected": bool(inv["paradigm_signal"]["detected"]),
            "c_ratio": (float(inv["paradigm_signal_composite"]["ratio"])
                        if inv["paradigm_signal_composite"]["ratio"] is not None else None),
            "c_detected": bool(inv["paradigm_signal_composite"]["detected"]),
            "new_expansions": new_exp,
            "basis_size": len(machine.turing.basis),
        })
    expansions = list(machine.expansions)
    verdict_exp_steps = sorted(
        int(m.group(1)) for m in
        (re.match(r"step(\d+):(LOOP|COLLAPSE|EXPLOSION)", e) for e in expansions)
        if m)
    para_steps = sorted(
        int(m.group(1)) for m in
        (re.match(r"step(\d+):paradigm", e) for e in expansions) if m)
    gap_series = [r["gap"] for r in rows]
    w_series = [r["w_gap"] for r in rows]
    t_series = [r["t_gap"] for r in rows]
    g_series = [r["g_gap"] for r in rows]
    return {
        "rows": rows, "trace": trace, "expansions": expansions,
        "verdict_exp_steps": verdict_exp_steps, "para_steps": para_steps,
        "gap_series": gap_series, "w_series": w_series, "t_series": t_series,
        "g_series": g_series, "basis_size": rows[-1]["basis_size"],
        "final_basis": len(machine.turing.basis),
    }


def rep_composite_flags(run: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Tuple[Optional[float], bool]]:
    """T2-Replikation: Composite-Detect-Muster aus der finalen Gap-Serie
    (das Signal am Schritt n sieht die Serie 1..n-1)."""
    return replicate_flags(run["gap_series"], offset=0)


def counterfactuals(run: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """T3b/T3c — Gefrieren auf dem Vor-Expansions-Niveau, Datenebene.
    None, wenn der Lauf keine Expansion hatte."""
    first = run["verdict_exp_steps"][0] if run["verdict_exp_steps"] else None
    if not first:
        return None
    w, t, g = run["w_series"], run["t_series"], run["g_series"]
    cut = first - 1
    t_pre = float(np.mean(t[:cut]))
    g_pre = float(np.mean(g[:cut]))
    cf_t = [t[i] if i < cut else t_pre for i in range(len(t))]
    cf_g = [g[i] if i < cut else g_pre for i in range(len(g))]
    cf_b = [(w[i] + cf_t[i] + g[i]) / 3.0 for i in range(len(w))]
    cf_c = [(w[i] + t[i] + cf_g[i]) / 3.0 for i in range(len(w))]
    return {
        "first_expansion": first,
        "t_pre": t_pre, "g_pre": g_pre,
        "orig_detections": list(run["para_steps"]),
        "cf_b_detections": [n for n, (_, det) in
                            enumerate(replicate_flags(cf_b, offset=0), 1) if det],
        "cf_c_detections": [n for n, (_, det) in
                            enumerate(replicate_flags(cf_c, offset=0), 1) if det],
        "orig_flags": [r["c_detected"] for r in run["rows"]],
        "cf_b_flags": [det for _, det in replicate_flags(cf_b, offset=0)],
        "cf_c_flags": [det for _, det in replicate_flags(cf_c, offset=0)],
    }


def t2_check(run: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    """Mechanik-Falsifikator: rekomputierte (ratio, detected) == aufgezeichnet."""
    rep = rep_composite_flags(run)
    for row, (ratio, det) in zip(run["rows"], rep):
        if det != row["c_detected"]:
            return False, f"Flag-Differenz am Schritt {row['step']}"
        if (ratio is None) != (row["c_ratio"] is None):
            return False, f"Ratio-None-Differenz am Schritt {row['step']}"
        if ratio is not None and abs(ratio - row["c_ratio"]) > RATIO_TOL:
            return False, (f"Ratio-Differenz am Schritt {row['step']}: "
                           f"{ratio!r} != {row['c_ratio']!r}")
    # Detect-Menge-Ableitung aus den expansions-Einträgen (Konsistenz):
    return True, "ok"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# P4-Runde — Akkumulationsgesetz + Dedrift (Vorschlag A); Bands fixiert
# vor dem Rerun. Canon: turing_machine.py:234/:197/:250/:293,
# machine_base.py:54/:531, wittgenstein_machine.py:413-414.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def accumulation_check(run: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """P4-Vorschlag A: t̂_n = |C_{n-1} - s_n|/(1 + s_n + 1e-6) gegen die
    aufgezeichnete Turing-Gap-Serie; C_{n-1} = kumulierte transitions-Summe;
    zweiter Strang: je-Zeiler-Kontostand (state()["transition_count"], :293)
    == kumulierte Summe der transitions je Schritt."""
    s = [r["transitions"] for r in run["rows"]]
    residues: List[float] = []
    c_prior = 0
    for n, s_n in enumerate(s, 1):
        t_hat = abs(c_prior - s_n) / (1.0 + abs(s_n) + GAP_EPS)
        residues.append(abs(t_hat - run["t_series"][n - 1]))
        c_prior += s_n
    counts = [r["t_count"] for r in run["rows"]]
    count_ok = all(c == sum(s[:i]) for i, c in enumerate(counts, 1))
    return {"max_resid": max(residues) if residues else 0.0,
            "residues": residues, "count_ok": count_ok,
            "c_series": [sum(s[:i]) for i in range(len(s))]}


def dedrift(run: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """P4-Vorschlag A: gleitende Prediction statt kumulierter Summe —
    ῡ_{n-1} = mean(s_{max(1, n-10) .. n-1}) (Präzedenz
    wittgenstein_machine.py:413-414, np.mean(history[-10:]) im
    Nicht-Orakel-Zweig; leeres Verlauf → 0.0); dedrifted Composite-Serie
    cf_d = (w + t_dedrift + g)/3, Detect-Menge aus cf_d (Offset 0)."""
    s = [r["transitions"] for r in run["rows"]]
    t_dedrift: List[float] = []
    for n, s_n in enumerate(s, 1):
        history = s[max(0, n - 10):n - 1]
        u = float(np.mean(history)) if history else 0.0
        t_dedrift.append(abs(u - float(s_n)) / (1.0 + abs(s_n) + GAP_EPS))
    cf_d = [(w + t + g) / 3.0 for w, t, g in
            zip(run["w_series"], t_dedrift, run["g_series"])]
    flags = replicate_flags(cf_d, offset=0)
    return {"t_dedrift_series": t_dedrift, "cf_d_series": cf_d,
            "dedrift_flags": [det for _, det in flags],
            "dedrift_detections": [n for n, (_, det) in enumerate(flags, 1)
                                   if det]}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Lanes
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def lane_l0_anker() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """L0-Replay gegen hermeneutik_experiment.json — inklusive der
    committeten Signal-Kolumnen (paradigm_detected == Composite-Flag,
    paradigm_ratio == Goedel-Ratio)."""
    committed = json.load(open("scratches/hermeneutik_experiment.json"))
    out: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    fields_ok = True

    for arm, force_ast, key in (("kripke", TAUTAST, "kripke"),
                                ("quus", QUUSAST, "contrast_quus")):
        machine, harness = make_composite(SEED_L0, force_ast)
        run = run_composite(machine, harness, STEPS_L0, FORCE_L0)
        cm = committed[key]
        verdicts_ok = [r["verdict"] for r in run["rows"]] == cm["verdicts"]
        final_ok = run["final_basis"] == cm["final_basis"]
        gaps_ok = ([round6(g) for g in run["gap_series"]]
                   == [round6(g) for g in cm["gaps"]])
        exp_ok = run["expansions"] == cm["expansions"]
        det_ok = ([r["c_detected"] for r in run["rows"]] == cm["paradigm_detected"])
        ratio_ok = True
        ratios_live = [r["g_ratio"] for r in run["rows"]]
        ratios_commit = cm["paradigm_ratio"]
        for idx, (a, b) in enumerate(zip(ratios_live, ratios_commit)):
            if a is None and b is None:
                continue
            if a is None or b is None:
                ratio_ok = False
                break
            if abs(a - b) > RATIO_TOL:
                ratio_ok = False
                break
        arm_ok = verdicts_ok and final_ok and gaps_ok and exp_ok and det_ok and ratio_ok
        fields_ok = fields_ok and arm_ok
        out[arm] = {
            "run": run,
            "checks": {"verdicts": verdicts_ok, "final_basis": final_ok,
                       "gaps": gaps_ok, "expansions": exp_ok,
                       "paradigm_detected": det_ok, "paradigm_ratio": ratio_ok},
        }
    first_detect_ok = all(
        out[arm]["run"]["para_steps"][0] == 11
        for arm in ("kripke", "quus") if out[arm]["run"]["para_steps"])
    fields_ok = fields_ok and first_detect_ok

    # Herkunfts-Attribution der kripke-Paradigma-Einträge (T1-Daten):
    attr = []
    for n in out["kripke"]["run"]["para_steps"]:
        row = out["kripke"]["run"]["rows"][n - 1]
        attr.append({"step": n, "c_ratio": row["c_ratio"],
                     "c_detected": row["c_detected"],
                     "g_ratio": row["g_ratio"], "g_detected": row["g_detected"]})
    out["kripke_attribution"] = attr
    out["fields_ok"] = fields_ok
    out["first_detect_ok"] = first_detect_ok
    mark("sp11_l0_anchor", "PASS" if fields_ok and first_detect_ok else "FAIL",
         "Feldvergleich kripke+quus inkl. committeter Signal-Kolumnen "
         "(paradigm_ratio = Goedel, paradigm_detected = Composite)")
    return out


def lane_sweep() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """6 unverzwungene Läufe (Seeds 11-16, 24 Schritte) — T1/T2/T3-Daten."""
    runs: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for seed in SP11_SEEDS:
        machine, harness = make_composite(seed, TAUTAST)
        runs[f"seed{seed}"] = run_composite(machine, harness, STEPS_SWEEP, set())
    return runs


def lane_zwang() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Seed-21-Paar: unverzwungener Zwilling + Zwang {13} — T4-Daten
    (+ Doppel-Läufe für die Determinismus-Prüfung)."""
    twin, harness_t = make_composite(SEED_ZW, TAUTAST)
    forced, harness_f = make_composite(SEED_ZW, TAUTAST)
    zw_twin = run_composite(twin, harness_t, STEPS_ZW, set())
    zw_forced = run_composite(forced, harness_f, STEPS_ZW, FORCE_ZW)
    forced2, harness_f2 = make_composite(SEED_ZW, TAUTAST)
    zw_forced_re = run_composite(forced2, harness_f2, STEPS_ZW, FORCE_ZW)
    return {"zw_twin": zw_twin, "zw_forced": zw_forced, "zw_forced_re": zw_forced_re}


def determinism_check(l0: Dict[str, Any], sweep: Dict[str, Any],
                      zwang: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    """Doppel-Läufe bitidentisch: trace + gap-Serie + Sub-Serien + Flags."""
    machine, harness = make_composite(SEED_L0, TAUTAST)
    l0_re = run_composite(machine, harness, STEPS_L0, FORCE_L0)
    l0_first = l0["kripke"]["run"]
    same_trace = l0_re["trace"] == l0_first["trace"]
    same_gaps = l0_re["gap_series"] == l0_first["gap_series"]
    same_subs = (l0_re["w_series"] == l0_first["w_series"]
                 and l0_re["t_series"] == l0_first["t_series"]
                 and l0_re["g_series"] == l0_first["g_series"])
    same_flags = ([r["c_detected"] for r in l0_re["rows"]]
                  == [r["c_detected"] for r in l0_first["rows"]])
    if not (same_trace and same_gaps and same_subs and same_flags):
        return False, "L0-Wiederholung weicht ab"

    machine11, harness11 = make_composite(11, TAUTAST)
    s11_re = run_composite(machine11, harness11, STEPS_SWEEP, set())
    s11_first = sweep["seed11"]
    if not (s11_re["trace"] == s11_first["trace"]
            and s11_re["gap_series"] == s11_first["gap_series"]
            and [r["c_detected"] for r in s11_re["rows"]]
            == [r["c_detected"] for r in s11_first["rows"]]):
        return False, "Sweep-seed11-Wiederholung weicht ab"

    if not (zwang["zw_forced_re"]["trace"] == zwang["zw_forced"]["trace"]
            and zwang["zw_forced_re"]["gap_series"] == zwang["zw_forced"]["gap_series"]
            and [r["c_detected"] for r in zwang["zw_forced_re"]["rows"]]
            == [r["c_detected"] for r in zwang["zw_forced"]["rows"]]):
        return False, "Zwang-Wiederholung weicht ab"
    return True, "ok"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# main
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def main() -> int:
    print("== SP11 — Der Ursprung der Paradigmen ==")
    print("Canon: composite.py:132-140/:167-182, goedel_machine.py:229-239,")
    print("       turing_machine.py:144-157 (loop_detector-Reset), GAP_EPS 1e-6")
    print("P4: turing_machine.py:197/:234/:250/:293 (Akkumulationsgesetz),")
    print("       machine_base.py:54/:531, wittgenstein_machine.py:413-414")
    print()

    l0 = lane_l0_anker()
    sweep = lane_sweep()
    zwang = lane_zwang()

    # Determinismus
    det_ok, det_note = determinism_check(l0, sweep, zwang)
    mark("sp11_det", "PASS" if det_ok else "FAIL", det_note)

    # T1: Urheber — niemals goedel.detected
    all_runs: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {
        "l0_kripke": l0["kripke"]["run"], "l0_quus": l0["quus"]["run"],
        **{k: v for k, v in sweep.items()},
        "zw_twin": zwang["zw_twin"], "zw_forced": zwang["zw_forced"],
    }
    breach = [(name, r["step"]) for name, run in all_runs.items()
              for r in run["rows"] if r["g_detected"]]
    t1_ok = not breach
    mark("sp11_t1_urheber", "PASS" if t1_ok else "FAIL",
         f"Goedel-Verletzungen: {breach}" if breach else
         "kein Schritt mit goedel.detected in allen 9 Läufen")

    # T2: Mechanik — Replikation aus der Serie
    t2_fail: List[str] = []
    for name, run in all_runs.items():
        ok, note = t2_check(run)
        if not ok:
            t2_fail.append(f"{name}: {note}")
    mark("sp11_t2_mechanik", "PASS" if not t2_fail else "FAIL",
         f"Fehler: {t2_fail}" if t2_fail
         else "Detect-Menge und Ratio je Lauf exakt aus der Gap-Serie rekomputiert")

    # T3a: Expansion-freie Läufe paradigmfrei
    free_runs = {n: r for n, r in all_runs.items() if not r["verdict_exp_steps"]}
    if free_runs:
        viol = [(n, r["para_steps"]) for n, r in free_runs.items() if r["para_steps"]]
        t3a_state = "PASS" if not viol else "FAIL"
        t3a_note = (f"Expansion-frei: {sorted(free_runs)}; Verletzungen: {viol}"
                    if viol else
                    f"Expansion-freie Läufe {sorted(free_runs)} alle paradigmfrei")
    else:
        t3a_state = "OFFEN"
        t3a_note = ("kein Expansion-freier Lauf in der Grundmenge "
                    "(6 Sweep-Seeds + Zwang-Zwilling) — Test nicht anwendbar")
    mark("sp11_t3a_expansionfrei", t3a_state, t3a_note)

    # T3b/T3c: Counterfaktur je Lauf mit Expansion
    cfs: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    t3b_fail: List[str] = []
    t3c_fail: List[str] = []
    for name, run in all_runs.items():
        cf = counterfactuals(run)
        if cf is None:
            continue
        cfs[name] = cf
        if cf["cf_b_detections"]:
            t3b_fail.append(f"{name}: Überlebende {cf['cf_b_detections']}")
        if cf["cf_c_detections"] != cf["orig_detections"]:
            t3c_fail.append(f"{name}: {cf['cf_c_detections']} != "
                            f"{cf['orig_detections']}")
    t3b_state = "PASS" if (not t3b_fail and cfs) else ("FAIL" if t3b_fail else "OFFEN")
    t3c_state = "PASS" if (not t3c_fail and cfs) else ("FAIL" if t3c_fail else "OFFEN")
    mark("sp11_t3b_turing_echo", t3b_state, f"Fehler: {t3b_fail}" if t3b_fail
         else f"alle {len(cfs)} Gegenproben ohne überlebende Detection")
    mark("sp11_t3c_goedel_vergleich", t3c_state, f"Fehler: {t3c_fail}" if t3c_fail
         else f"Detection-Mengen in {len(cfs)} Gegenproben unverändert")

    # T4: Zwang
    induced = sorted(set(zwang["zw_forced"]["para_steps"])
                     - set(zwang["zw_twin"]["para_steps"]))
    in_band = [n for n in induced if ZW_BAND_LO <= n <= ZW_BAND_HI]
    mark("sp11_t4_forced", "PASS" if in_band else "FAIL",
         f"induzierte Paradigmas {induced}; in Band [14, 22]: {in_band}")

    # P4-Runde (Vorschlag A): Akkumulationsgesetz + Dedrift — Bands fixiert
    # vor dem Rerun; die Ursprungs-Verdikte t3a/t3b bleiben FAIL (ERRATA).
    free_names = [n for n, run in all_runs.items()
                  if not run["verdict_exp_steps"]]
    acc: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    acc_fails: List[str] = []
    for name, run in all_runs.items():
        a = accumulation_check(run)
        acc[name] = {"max_resid": a["max_resid"], "count_ok": a["count_ok"],
                     "c_series": a["c_series"]}
        if not a["count_ok"] or a["max_resid"] > RATIO_TOL:
            acc_fails.append(f"{name}: count_ok={a['count_ok']}, "
                             f"max_resid={a['max_resid']:.3e}")
    acc_max = max((v["max_resid"] for v in acc.values()), default=0.0)
    mark("sp11_t3_accumulation_law_p4", "PASS" if not acc_fails else "FAIL",
         f"Strang-Verletzung: {acc_fails}" if acc_fails else
         (f"t_n == |C(n-1) - s_n|/(1+s_n+1e-6) in allen {len(all_runs)} "
          f"Läufen; max Residuum {acc_max!r}; Kontostand == kumulierte "
          "Summe in jedem Schritt (turing_machine.py:234/:197/:293)"))

    dd: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    drift_fails: List[str] = []
    for name, run in all_runs.items():
        d = dedrift(run)
        dd[name] = d
        if name in free_names and d["dedrift_detections"]:
            drift_fails.append(f"{name}: überlebende {d['dedrift_detections']}")
    mark("sp11_t3_dedrifted_echo_p4", "PASS" if not drift_fails else "FAIL",
         f"überlebende Dedrift-Detections: {drift_fails}" if drift_fails else
         ("dedrifted Detect-Menge == leer in den expansion-freien Läufen "
          f"{free_names} (Gleitfenster-Präzedenz "
          "wittgenstein_machine.py:413-414); Rest-Läufe als Daten"))

    # Befunde
    print("BEFUNDE:")
    for name, state in FINDINGS:
        print(f"  {name:<26s} {state}")
    print()
    if NOTES:
        print("NOTIZEN:")
        for n in NOTES:
            print(f"  - {n}")
        print()

    if ERRATA:
        print("ERRATA (P4-Klassifikation):")
        for name, text in ERRATA.items():
            print(f"  - {name}: {text}")
        print()

    failed = [n for n, s in FINDINGS if s == "FAIL" and n not in ERRATA]
    offen = [n for n, s in FINDINGS if s == "OFFEN"]
    passes = [n for n, s in FINDINGS if s == "PASS"]
    print(f"BILANZ: {len(passes)} PASS; {len(failed)} FEHLGESCHLAGEN (unverwartet); "
          f"{len([n for n, s in FINDINGS if s == 'FAIL']) - len(failed)} dokumentierte Errata; "
          f"{len(offen)} OFFEN")

    payload = {
        "l0": {"kripke": {"checks": l0["kripke"]["checks"],
                          "expansions": l0["kripke"]["run"]["expansions"],
                          "para_steps": l0["kripke"]["run"]["para_steps"],
                          "transitions": [r["transitions"] for r in
                                          l0["kripke"]["run"]["rows"]],
                          "t_count": [r["t_count"] for r in
                                      l0["kripke"]["run"]["rows"]],
                          "final_basis": l0["kripke"]["run"]["final_basis"]},
               "quus": {"checks": l0["quus"]["checks"],
                        "expansions": l0["quus"]["run"]["expansions"],
                        "para_steps": l0["quus"]["run"]["para_steps"],
                        "transitions": [r["transitions"] for r in
                                        l0["quus"]["run"]["rows"]],
                        "t_count": [r["t_count"] for r in
                                    l0["quus"]["run"]["rows"]],
                        "final_basis": l0["quus"]["run"]["final_basis"]},
               "kripke_attribution": l0["kripke_attribution"],
               "fields_ok": l0["fields_ok"],
               "first_detect_ok": l0["first_detect_ok"]},
        "sweep": {name: {
            "verdicts": [r["verdict"] for r in run["rows"]],
            "expansions": run["expansions"],
            "para_steps": run["para_steps"],
            "verdict_exp_steps": run["verdict_exp_steps"],
            "gap_series": run["gap_series"],
            "w_series": run["w_series"], "t_series": run["t_series"],
            "g_series": run["g_series"],
            "transitions": [r["transitions"] for r in run["rows"]],
            "t_count": [r["t_count"] for r in run["rows"]],
            "c_ratio": [r["c_ratio"] for r in run["rows"]],
            "g_ratio": [r["g_ratio"] for r in run["rows"]],
            "c_detected": [r["c_detected"] for r in run["rows"]],
            "g_detected": [r["g_detected"] for r in run["rows"]],
            "basis_size": run["basis_size"],
        } for name, run in sweep.items()},
        "zwang": {name: {
            "verdicts": [r["verdict"] for r in run["rows"]],
            "expansions": run["expansions"],
            "para_steps": run["para_steps"],
            "gap_series": run["gap_series"],
            "w_series": run["w_series"], "g_series": run["g_series"],
            "t_series": run["t_series"],
            "transitions": [r["transitions"] for r in run["rows"]],
            "t_count": [r["t_count"] for r in run["rows"]],
            "c_detected": [r["c_detected"] for r in run["rows"]],
        } for name, run in
            (("zw_twin", zwang["zw_twin"]), ("zw_forced", zwang["zw_forced"]))},
        "counterfactuals": cfs,
        "p4": {
            "errata": ERRATA,
            "accumulation": acc,
            "dedrift": {name: {"t_dedrift_series": d["t_dedrift_series"],
                               "cf_d_series": d["cf_d_series"],
                               "dedrift_flags": d["dedrift_flags"],
                               "dedrift_detections": d["dedrift_detections"]}
                        for name, d in dd.items()},
            "expansion_freie_laeufe": free_names,
        },
        "findings": [{"name": n, "state": s} for n, s in FINDINGS],
        "notes": NOTES,
        "errata": ERRATA,
        "all_pass": not failed,
        "open": offen,
    }
    with open("scratches/paradigm_ursprung_experiment.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        json.dump(payload, fh, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=False)
    print("JSON: scratches/paradigm_ursprung_experiment.json")

    if failed:
        print(f"FEHLGESCHLAGEN: {failed}")
        return 1
    print(f"GRUEN — Loesungskette geschlossen (OFFEN: {offen if offen else 'keine'}).")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

### bytes: 36503   lines: 781

"""
modeling_argon.py
=================
Hauptarchitektur für Argon (1B Hybrid MLA-MoE Multimodal Transformer).

Kernkomponenten:
1. ArgonMultimodalEmbedding: Early-Fusion für Text, Vision-Patches und Audio-Frames;
   bidirektionale Attention-Maske und 2D-RoPE für zusammenhängende Bild-Regionen.
2. ArgonHybridAttention:
   - Lokale Sliding-Window-Schichten (5:1): MQA mit rope_theta = 10.000 und KV-Ringpuffer.
   - Globale Schichten (layer % 6 == 5): Multi-Head Latent Attention (MLA) mit
     rope_theta = 1.000.000 und komprimiertem Latent-KV-Cache ([c_kv, k_rope] = 320 Dim).
3. ArgonMoELayer: Shared + Routed GeGLU-Experten mit Entropie-adaptiver Kapazität.
4. Gauß-gewichtete Router-Entropie-Regularisierung über die Netzwerktiefe.
"""

import math
from typing import Optional, Tuple, List, Dict, Any

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from transformers.configuration_utils import PretrainedConfig
from transformers.modeling_utils import PreTrainedModel
from transformers.modeling_outputs import CausalLMOutputWithPast
from transformers.cache_utils import Cache, DynamicCache
from transformers.generation import GenerationMixin

from moe_layers import ArgonMoELayer


class ArgonConfig(PretrainedConfig):
    model_type = "argon"

    def __init__(
        self,
        vocab_size: int = 262144,
        hidden_size: int = 1152,
        intermediate_size: int = 6912,
        num_hidden_layers: int = 26,
        num_attention_heads: int = 4,
        num_key_value_heads: int = 1,
        head_dim: int = 256,
        rms_norm_eps: float = 1e-6,
        rope_theta_local: float = 10000.0,
        rope_theta_global: float = 1000000.0,
        sliding_window: int = 512,
        sliding_window_pattern: int = 6,
        attn_logit_softcapping: Optional[float] = None,
        final_logit_softcapping: Optional[float] = None,
        use_mla: bool = True,
        kv_lora_rank: int = 256,
        q_lora_rank: int = 512,
        qk_nope_head_dim: int = 192,
        qk_rope_head_dim: int = 64,
        v_head_dim: int = 256,
        use_moe: bool = True,
        n_shared_experts: int = 1,
        n_routed_experts: int = 8,
        num_experts_per_tok: int = 2,
        moe_inter_dim: int = 1728,
        router_entropy_threshold: float = 1.8,
        router_entropy_target: float = 1.0,
        router_depth_peak: float = 0.55,
        router_depth_sigma: float = 0.30,
        vision_bidirectional: bool = True,
        vision_2d_rope: bool = True,
        shared_intermediate_size: Optional[int] = 6912,
        vision_config: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
        audio_config: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
        tie_word_embeddings: bool = True,
        **kwargs,
    ):
        super().__init__(tie_word_embeddings=tie_word_embeddings, **kwargs)
        self.vocab_size = vocab_size
        self.hidden_size = hidden_size
        self.intermediate_size = intermediate_size
        self.num_hidden_layers = num_hidden_layers
        self.num_attention_heads = num_attention_heads
        self.num_key_value_heads = num_key_value_heads
        self.head_dim = head_dim
        self.rms_norm_eps = rms_norm_eps
        self.rope_theta_local = rope_theta_local
        self.rope_theta_global = rope_theta_global
        self.sliding_window = sliding_window
        self.sliding_window_pattern = sliding_window_pattern
        self.attn_logit_softcapping = attn_logit_softcapping
        self.final_logit_softcapping = final_logit_softcapping
        self.use_mla = use_mla
        self.kv_lora_rank = kv_lora_rank
        self.q_lora_rank = q_lora_rank
        self.qk_nope_head_dim = qk_nope_head_dim
        self.qk_rope_head_dim = qk_rope_head_dim
        self.v_head_dim = v_head_dim
        self.use_moe = use_moe
        self.n_shared_experts = n_shared_experts
        self.n_routed_experts = n_routed_experts
        self.num_experts_per_tok = num_experts_per_tok
        self.moe_inter_dim = moe_inter_dim
        self.router_entropy_threshold = router_entropy_threshold
        self.router_entropy_target = router_entropy_target
        self.router_depth_peak = router_depth_peak
        self.router_depth_sigma = router_depth_sigma
        self.vision_bidirectional = vision_bidirectional
        self.vision_2d_rope = vision_2d_rope
        self.shared_intermediate_size = shared_intermediate_size or intermediate_size
        self.vision_config = vision_config or {"vision_hidden_size": 1152, "image_size": 384}
        self.audio_config = audio_config or {"audio_hidden_size": 896}


class ArgonRMSNorm(nn.Module):
    """
    Zero-centered RMSNorm mit (1.0 + weight) Skalierung.
    """
    def __init__(self, dim: int, eps: float = 1e-6):
        super().__init__()
        self.eps = eps
        self.weight = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(dim))

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        orig_dtype = x.dtype
        x_float = x.float()
        variance = x_float.pow(2).mean(-1, keepdim=True)
        norm_x = x_float * torch.rsqrt(variance + self.eps)
        out = norm_x * (1.0 + self.weight.float())
        return out.to(orig_dtype)


def apply_rotary_pos_emb(x: torch.Tensor, cos: torch.Tensor, sin: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    d = x.shape[-1] // 2
    x1 = x[..., :d]
    x2 = x[..., d:]
    rotated = torch.cat((-x2, x1), dim=-1)
    return (x * cos) + (rotated * sin)


class ArgonMultimodalEmbedding(nn.Module):
    """
    Early-Fusion Eingangsprojektion für Text, Vision-Patches und Audio-Frames.
    """
    def __init__(self, config: ArgonConfig):
        super().__init__()
        self.hidden_size = config.hidden_size
        self.text_embed = nn.Embedding(config.vocab_size, config.hidden_size)
        vision_dim = config.vision_config.get("vision_hidden_size", config.hidden_size)
        audio_dim = config.audio_config.get("audio_hidden_size", 896)
        self.vision_proj = nn.Linear(vision_dim, config.hidden_size, bias=False)
        self.audio_proj = nn.Linear(audio_dim, config.hidden_size, bias=False)
        self.norm = ArgonRMSNorm(config.hidden_size, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)

    def forward(
        self,
        input_ids: torch.Tensor,
        modality_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
        vision_patches: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
        audio_frames: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        h = self.text_embed(input_ids) * math.sqrt(self.hidden_size)

        if modality_mask is not None:
            if vision_patches is not None:
                v_emb = self.norm(self.vision_proj(vision_patches))
                h = torch.where(modality_mask.unsqueeze(-1) == 1, v_emb, h)
            if audio_frames is not None:
                a_emb = self.norm(self.audio_proj(audio_frames))
                h = torch.where(modality_mask.unsqueeze(-1) == 2, a_emb, h)
        return h


class ArgonHybridAttention(nn.Module):
    """
    Hybride Attention-Schicht mit Latent-KV-Cache-Unterstützung:
    - Lokale Sliding-Window-Schichten: MQA, cached (k, v) begrenzt auf sliding_window.
    - Globale MLA-Schichten: cached ausschließlich den komprimierten 320-Dim Vektor (c_kv, k_rope_raw).
    """
    def __init__(self, config: ArgonConfig, layer_idx: int):
        super().__init__()
        self.config = config
        self.layer_idx = layer_idx
        self.hidden_size = config.hidden_size
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.num_kv_heads = config.num_key_value_heads
        self.head_dim = config.head_dim
        self.sliding_window = config.sliding_window
        self.softcapping = config.attn_logit_softcapping
        self.scaling = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)

        pattern = getattr(config, "sliding_window_pattern", 6)
        self.is_global = (layer_idx % pattern == (pattern - 1))
        self.use_mla = config.use_mla and self.is_global

        self.q_norm = ArgonRMSNorm(self.head_dim, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)
        self.k_norm = ArgonRMSNorm(self.head_dim, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)

        if self.use_mla:
            self.kv_lora_rank = config.kv_lora_rank
            self.qk_nope_dim = config.qk_nope_head_dim
            self.qk_rope_dim = config.qk_rope_head_dim
            self.v_head_dim = config.v_head_dim

            self.kv_down = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.kv_lora_rank + self.qk_rope_dim, bias=False)
            self.kv_up = nn.Linear(self.kv_lora_rank, self.num_heads * (self.qk_nope_dim + self.v_head_dim), bias=False)
            self.q_proj = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.num_heads * self.head_dim, bias=False)
            self.o_proj = nn.Linear(self.num_heads * self.v_head_dim, self.hidden_size, bias=False)
        else:
            self.q_proj = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.num_heads * self.head_dim, bias=False)
            self.k_proj = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim, bias=False)
            self.v_proj = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim, bias=False)
            self.o_proj = nn.Linear(self.num_heads * self.head_dim, self.hidden_size, bias=False)

    def forward(
        self,
        x: torch.Tensor,
        cos: torch.Tensor,
        sin: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
        past_key_value: Optional[Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]] = None,
        use_cache: bool = False,
    ) -> Tuple[torch.Tensor, Optional[Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]]]:
        bsz, q_len, _ = x.shape

        if self.use_mla:
            # 1. Query projizieren, normieren und auf die letzten 64 Kanäle RoPE für die aktuellen Positionen anwenden
            q = self.q_proj(x).view(bsz, q_len, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
            q = self.q_norm(q).transpose(1, 2)
            q_nope = q[..., :self.qk_nope_dim]
            q_rope = q[..., self.qk_nope_dim:]
            cos_q = cos[:, :, -q_len:, :]
            sin_q = sin[:, :, -q_len:, :]
            q_rope = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q_rope, cos_q, sin_q)
            q = torch.cat([q_nope, q_rope], dim=-1)

            # 2. Latente KV-Kompression berechnen: [bsz, q_len, 320]
            kv_comp = self.kv_down(x)
            c_kv_new = kv_comp[..., :self.kv_lora_rank]
            k_rope_new = kv_comp[..., self.kv_lora_rank:]

            # 3. Komprimierten Latent-KV-Cache aktualisieren (nur 320 Floats pro Token!)
            if past_key_value is not None:
                c_kv = torch.cat([past_key_value[0], c_kv_new], dim=1)
                k_rope_raw = torch.cat([past_key_value[1], k_rope_new], dim=1)
            else:
                c_kv, k_rope_raw = c_kv_new, k_rope_new

            present_key_value = (c_kv, k_rope_raw) if use_cache else None
            kv_len = c_kv.shape[1]

            # 4. KV aus dem Latent-Vektor entfalten, normieren und RoPE über die gesamte KV-Sequenz anwenden
            kv_up_out = self.kv_up(c_kv).view(bsz, kv_len, self.num_heads, self.qk_nope_dim + self.v_head_dim)
            k_nope = kv_up_out[..., :self.qk_nope_dim]
            v = kv_up_out[..., self.qk_nope_dim:]

            k_rope_expanded = k_rope_raw.unsqueeze(2).expand(-1, -1, self.num_heads, -1)
            k = torch.cat([k_nope, k_rope_expanded], dim=-1)
            k = self.k_norm(k).transpose(1, 2)
            v = v.transpose(1, 2)

            k_nope_norm = k[..., :self.qk_nope_dim]
            k_rope_norm = k[..., self.qk_nope_dim:]
            k_rope_rot = apply_rotary_pos_emb(k_rope_norm, cos[:, :, :kv_len, :], sin[:, :, :kv_len, :])
            k = torch.cat([k_nope_norm, k_rope_rot], dim=-1)

            scores = torch.matmul(q, k.transpose(-1, -2)) * self.scaling
        else:
            # Lokaler MQA Sliding-Window-Pfad
            q = self.q_proj(x).view(bsz, q_len, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
            k = self.k_proj(x).view(bsz, q_len, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            v = self.v_proj(x).view(bsz, q_len, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)

            q = self.q_norm(q).transpose(1, 2)
            k = self.k_norm(k).transpose(1, 2)
            v = v.transpose(1, 2)

            cos_q = cos[:, :, -q_len:, :]
            sin_q = sin[:, :, -q_len:, :]
            q = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, cos_q, sin_q)
            k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(k, cos_q, sin_q)

            if past_key_value is not None:
                k = torch.cat([past_key_value[0], k], dim=2)
                v = torch.cat([past_key_value[1], v], dim=2)

            # O(1)-Ringpuffer-Eviction: im Cache bleiben nur die letzten
            # sliding_window Token. Die Sliding-Maske blendet alles Ältere
            # ohnehin aus; die gecachten k sind bereits vorrotiert, die
            # relativen Phasen zwischen Query und Cache bleiben also korrekt.
            if use_cache and self.sliding_window is not None and k.shape[2] > self.sliding_window:
                present_key_value = (k[..., -self.sliding_window:, :], v[..., -self.sliding_window:, :])
            elif use_cache:
                present_key_value = (k, v)
            else:
                present_key_value = None

            if self.num_kv_heads < self.num_heads:
                k = k.repeat_interleave(self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads, dim=1)
                v = v.repeat_interleave(self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads, dim=1)

            scores = torch.matmul(q, k.transpose(-1, -2)) * self.scaling

        if self.softcapping is not None:
            scores = self.softcapping * torch.tanh(scores / self.softcapping)

        if attention_mask is not None:
            scores = scores + attention_mask

        weights = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1, dtype=torch.float32).to(x.dtype)
        attn_out = torch.matmul(weights, v)
        attn_out = attn_out.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(bsz, q_len, -1)
        return self.o_proj(attn_out), present_key_value


class ArgonDecoderLayer(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, config: ArgonConfig, layer_idx: int):
        super().__init__()
        self.layer_idx = layer_idx
        pattern = getattr(config, "sliding_window_pattern", 6)
        self.is_global = (layer_idx % pattern == (pattern - 1))

        self.input_layernorm = ArgonRMSNorm(config.hidden_size, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)
        self.self_attn = ArgonHybridAttention(config, layer_idx)
        self.post_attention_layernorm = ArgonRMSNorm(config.hidden_size, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)
        self.pre_feedforward_layernorm = ArgonRMSNorm(config.hidden_size, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)
        self.moe = ArgonMoELayer(config)
        self.post_feedforward_layernorm = ArgonRMSNorm(config.hidden_size, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        cos: torch.Tensor,
        sin: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
        past_key_value: Optional[Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]] = None,
        use_cache: bool = False,
    ) -> Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, Optional[Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]]]:
        residual = hidden_states
        norm_states = self.input_layernorm(hidden_states)
        attn_out, present_kv = self.self_attn(
            norm_states,
            cos=cos,
            sin=sin,
            attention_mask=attention_mask,
            past_key_value=past_key_value,
            use_cache=use_cache,
        )
        h = residual + self.post_attention_layernorm(attn_out)

        residual = h
        ffn_in = self.pre_feedforward_layernorm(h)
        moe_out, router_entropy = self.moe(ffn_in)
        out = residual + self.post_feedforward_layernorm(moe_out)
        return out, router_entropy, present_kv


class ArgonModel(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, config: ArgonConfig):
        super().__init__()
        self.config = config
        self.embed_tokens = ArgonMultimodalEmbedding(config)
        self.layers = nn.ModuleList([
            ArgonDecoderLayer(config, i) for i in range(config.num_hidden_layers)
        ])
        self.norm = ArgonRMSNorm(config.hidden_size, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)

    def _build_rotary_emb(self, seq_len: int, head_dim: int, theta: float, device: torch.device) -> Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        inv_freq = 1.0 / (theta ** (torch.arange(0, head_dim, 2, dtype=torch.float32, device=device) / head_dim))
        t = torch.arange(seq_len, device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
        freqs = torch.outer(t, inv_freq)
        emb = torch.cat((freqs, freqs), dim=-1)
        return emb.cos().unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0), emb.sin().unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)

    def _build_rotary_emb_2d(
        self,
        coords_x: torch.Tensor,
        coords_y: torch.Tensor,
        head_dim: int,
        theta: float,
        device: torch.device,
    ) -> Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        half = head_dim // 2
        inv_freq = 1.0 / (theta ** (torch.arange(0, head_dim, 2, dtype=torch.float32, device=device) / head_dim))
        split = half // 2
        freqs_x = coords_x.to(torch.float32).unsqueeze(-1) * inv_freq[:split].unsqueeze(0)
        freqs_y = coords_y.to(torch.float32).unsqueeze(-1) * inv_freq[split:].unsqueeze(0)
        angles = torch.cat([freqs_x, freqs_y], dim=-1)
        emb = torch.cat((angles, angles), dim=-1)
        return emb.cos().unsqueeze(1), emb.sin().unsqueeze(1)

    def _build_2d_coords(self, modality_mask: torch.Tensor, device: torch.device):
        bsz, seq_len = modality_mask.shape
        coords_x = torch.arange(seq_len, device=device, dtype=torch.float32).unsqueeze(0).expand(bsz, -1).clone()
        coords_y = coords_x.clone()
        has_vision = False
        mods_rows = modality_mask.tolist()
        for i in range(bsz):
            mods = mods_rows[i]
            p = 0
            while p < seq_len:
                if mods[p] == 1:
                    s = p
                    while p < seq_len and mods[p] == 1:
                        p += 1
                    run_len = p - s
                    G = max(1, int(round(math.sqrt(run_len))))
                    base = torch.arange(run_len, device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
                    coords_x[i, s:p] = s + (base % G)
                    coords_y[i, s:p] = s + torch.div(base, G, rounding_mode="floor")
                    has_vision = True
                else:
                    p += 1
        return coords_x, coords_y, has_vision

    def _prepare_masks(
        self,
        attention_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor],
        q_len: int,
        kv_len: int,
        device: torch.device,
        dtype: torch.dtype,
        modality_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
    ) -> Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        # Erzeuge vollständige [kv_len, kv_len] Kausalmaske und schneide die letzten q_len Zeilen für Decode/Prefill
        full_causal = torch.triu(torch.full((kv_len, kv_len), float("-inf"), device=device, dtype=dtype), diagonal=1)
        full_sliding = full_causal.clone()
        if self.config.sliding_window is not None:
            band = torch.tril(torch.full((kv_len, kv_len), float("-inf"), device=device, dtype=dtype), diagonal=-self.config.sliding_window)
            full_sliding = full_sliding + band

        causal_mask = full_causal[-q_len:, :].unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)
        sliding_mask = full_sliding[-q_len:, :].unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)

        if attention_mask is not None:
            if attention_mask.dim() == 2 and attention_mask.shape[-1] == kv_len:
                pad_mask = (1.0 - attention_mask.to(dtype=dtype)) * -1e9
                pad_mask = pad_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)
                global_mask = causal_mask + pad_mask
                sliding_mask = sliding_mask + pad_mask
            elif attention_mask.dim() == 4:
                global_mask = causal_mask + attention_mask
                sliding_mask = sliding_mask + attention_mask
            else:
                global_mask = causal_mask
        else:
            global_mask = causal_mask

        # Bidirektionale Aufmerksamkeit für zusammenhängende Vision-Patch-Blöcke im Prefill
        if q_len == kv_len and modality_mask is not None and getattr(self.config, "vision_bidirectional", True):
            n_rows = modality_mask.shape[0]
            runs_per_row = []
            for i in range(n_rows):
                mods = modality_mask[i].tolist()
                runs, p = [], 0
                while p < kv_len:
                    if mods[p] == 1:
                        s = p
                        while p < kv_len and mods[p] == 1:
                            p += 1
                        runs.append((s, p))
                    else:
                        p += 1
                runs_per_row.append(runs)

            if any(runs_per_row):
                if global_mask.shape[0] != n_rows:
                    expand_shape = (n_rows,) + global_mask.shape[1:]
                    global_mask = global_mask.expand(expand_shape).clone()
                    sliding_mask = sliding_mask.expand(expand_shape).clone()
                for i, runs in enumerate(runs_per_row):
                    for (s, e) in runs:
                        global_mask[i, ..., s:e, s:e] = 0.0
                        sliding_mask[i, ..., s:e, s:e] = 0.0

        return global_mask, sliding_mask

    def forward(
        self,
        input_ids: torch.Tensor,
        modality_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
        vision_patches: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
        audio_frames: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
        attention_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
        past_key_values: Optional[List[Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]]] = None,
        use_cache: bool = False,
        kv_modality_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
    ) -> Tuple[torch.Tensor, List[torch.Tensor], Optional[List[Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]]]]:
        """kv_modality_mask: Modalitätsmaske der GESAMTEN Sequenz im Cache
        (Prefill-Länge + Generate-Suffix) — wird im Decode-State benötigt, um
        die 2D-Vision-Koordinaten der gecachten Patch-RoPE-Kanäle zu rekonstruieren."""
        bsz, q_len = input_ids.shape

        # transformers 5.x Cache-Objekt (DynamicCache aus generate()) entpacken:
        # Die Latent-KV-Cache-Tensoren liegen unverändert in cache.layers[].keys/.values.
        # Nicht initialisierte Layer (generierter Leer-Cache im Prefill-Schritt) werden
        # über get_seq_length() == 0 herausgefiltert.
        if past_key_values is not None and not isinstance(past_key_values, (list, tuple)):
            past_key_values = [
                (layer.keys, layer.values)
                for layer in past_key_values.layers
                if layer.get_seq_length() > 0
            ]

        past_len = 0
        if past_key_values is not None and len(past_key_values) > 0 and past_key_values[0] is not None:
            # In Schicht 0 (lokales MQA) liegt k auf [bsz, kv_heads, past_len, head_dim]
            past_len = past_key_values[0][0].shape[2] if past_key_values[0][0].dim() == 4 else past_key_values[0][0].shape[1]
        kv_len_local = past_len + q_len

        # Absolute Länge: globale MLA-Schichten wachsen mit jedem Token unbegrenzt,
        # lokale MQA-Schichten sind ab sliding_window durch Ringpuffer-Eviction
        # konstant. Daraus folgen zwei Masken-/RoPE-Koordinatenräume.
        kv_len_abs = kv_len_local
        first_global = getattr(self.config, "sliding_window_pattern", 6) - 1
        if first_global > 0 and past_key_values is not None and first_global < len(past_key_values) \
                and past_key_values[first_global] is not None:
            kv_len_abs = past_key_values[first_global][0].shape[1] + q_len
        kv_len = kv_len_abs

        # Modalitätsmaske auseinandernehmen: fürs Embedding zählt nur der
        # Eingabe-Slice (letzte q_len — torch.where verlangt elementweise Deckung),
        # für die RoPE-Koordinaten die vollständige Cache-Sequenz. Ein
        # Vollmasken-Decode (modality_mask == Cache-Länge) ist damit auch ohne
        # separates kv_modality_mask korrekt, statt stillschweigend ignoriert.
        modality_mask_in = modality_mask
        modality_mask_kv = None
        if modality_mask is not None and modality_mask.dim() == 2:
            m = modality_mask
            if m.shape[1] > q_len:
                modality_mask_in = m[:, -q_len:]
                if m.shape[1] >= kv_len:
                    modality_mask_kv = m[:, -kv_len:]
                else:
                    # Zu kurze Maske deckt nur die letzten l Token (end-aligniert):
                    # fehlender Anfang = Text, hinten anwachsen lassen.
                    pad = m.new_zeros((m.shape[0], kv_len - m.shape[1]))
                    modality_mask_kv = torch.cat([pad, m], dim=1)

            elif q_len == kv_len:
                modality_mask_kv = m

        h = self.embed_tokens(
            input_ids=input_ids,
            modality_mask=modality_mask_in,
            vision_patches=vision_patches,
            audio_frames=audio_frames,
        )

        device = h.device
        dtype = h.dtype

        # 2D-Gitter-Koordinaten: im Prefill (q_len == kv_len) aus der Modalitätsmaske
        # der Eingabe; im Decode (q_len < kv_len) aus der vollständigen
        # kv_modality_mask, damit die im Cache roh liegenden k_rope-Vektoren der
        # Vision-Patches bei jeder Neuberechnung mit ihren Grid-Winkeln statt mit
        # einer 1D-Positionsnummer rotiert werden (Fix: RoPE-Winkel einfrieren).
        if modality_mask_kv is None and kv_modality_mask is not None and kv_modality_mask.dim() == 2:
            mask_kv = kv_modality_mask
            if mask_kv.shape[1] > kv_len:
                mask_kv = mask_kv[:, -kv_len:]
            elif mask_kv.shape[1] < kv_len:
                pad = mask_kv.new_zeros((mask_kv.shape[0], kv_len - mask_kv.shape[1]))
                mask_kv = torch.cat([mask_kv, pad], dim=1)
            modality_mask_kv = mask_kv

        coords_x = coords_y = None
        has_vision = False
        if modality_mask_kv is not None and getattr(self.config, "vision_2d_rope", True):
            coords_x, coords_y, has_vision = self._build_2d_coords(modality_mask_kv, device)
        has_vision = has_vision and coords_x is not None

        if has_vision:
            cos_local, sin_local = self._build_rotary_emb_2d(
                coords_x, coords_y, self.config.head_dim, self.config.rope_theta_local, device
            )
            rope_dim_global = self.config.qk_rope_head_dim if self.config.use_mla else self.config.head_dim
            cos_global, sin_global = self._build_rotary_emb_2d(
                coords_x, coords_y, rope_dim_global, self.config.rope_theta_global, device
            )
        else:
            cos_local, sin_local = self._build_rotary_emb(kv_len, self.config.head_dim, self.config.rope_theta_local, device)
            rope_dim_global = self.config.qk_rope_head_dim if self.config.use_mla else self.config.head_dim
            cos_global, sin_global = self._build_rotary_emb(kv_len, rope_dim_global, self.config.rope_theta_global, device)

        cos_local, sin_local = cos_local.to(dtype), sin_local.to(dtype)
        cos_global, sin_global = cos_global.to(dtype), sin_global.to(dtype)

        # Zwei Maskenräume: globale Schichten über die absolute Länge, lokale
        # Schichten über die evicted Cache-Länge (bei kurzen Sequenzen identisch).
        global_mask, sliding_mask_abs = self._prepare_masks(
            attention_mask, q_len, kv_len, device, dtype, modality_mask=modality_mask_kv
        )
        if kv_len_local == kv_len:
            sliding_mask = sliding_mask_abs
        else:
            local_attn_mask = attention_mask
            if attention_mask is not None and attention_mask.dim() == 2 and attention_mask.shape[-1] == kv_len:
                local_attn_mask = attention_mask[:, -kv_len_local:]
            _, sliding_mask = self._prepare_masks(local_attn_mask, q_len, kv_len_local, device, dtype, modality_mask=None)

        all_entropies = []
        next_decoder_cache = [] if use_cache else None

        for idx, layer in enumerate(self.layers):
            past_kv = past_key_values[idx] if (past_key_values is not None and idx < len(past_key_values)) else None
            if layer.is_global:
                h, layer_entropy, present_kv = layer(
                    h, cos=cos_global, sin=sin_global, attention_mask=global_mask, past_key_value=past_kv, use_cache=use_cache
                )
            else:
                h, layer_entropy, present_kv = layer(
                    h, cos=cos_local, sin=sin_local, attention_mask=sliding_mask, past_key_value=past_kv, use_cache=use_cache
                )
            all_entropies.append(layer_entropy)
            if use_cache:
                next_decoder_cache.append(present_kv)

        h = self.norm(h)
        if use_cache:
            # Rückgabe als echtes Cache-Objekt: die Generate-Schleife von transformers
            # 5.x ruft jede Iteration `cache.get_seq_length()` auf. DynamicCache ohne
            # config erzeugt reine DynamicLayer, die die 3-dim MLA-Latenztensoren
            # (c_kv, k_rope) unverändert speichern — kein Sliding-Crop der globalen
            # Latent-KV-Caches.
            next_decoder_cache = DynamicCache(ddp_cache_data=tuple(next_decoder_cache))
        return h, all_entropies, next_decoder_cache


class ArgonForConditionalGeneration(PreTrainedModel, GenerationMixin):
    config_class = ArgonConfig
    base_model_prefix = "model"
    _tied_weights_keys = {"lm_head.weight": "model.embed_tokens.text_embed.weight"}

    def __init__(self, config: ArgonConfig):
        super().__init__(config)
        self.model = ArgonModel(config)
        self.lm_head = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.vocab_size, bias=False)
        self.final_softcap = config.final_logit_softcapping

        if config.tie_word_embeddings:
            self.lm_head.weight = self.model.embed_tokens.text_embed.weight

        self.post_init()

    def get_input_embeddings(self):
        return self.model.embed_tokens.text_embed

    def set_input_embeddings(self, value):
        self.model.embed_tokens.text_embed = value

    def get_output_embeddings(self):
        return self.lm_head

    def set_output_embeddings(self, new_embeddings):
        self.lm_head = new_embeddings

    def prepare_inputs_for_generation(
        self,
        input_ids: torch.LongTensor,
        past_key_values: Optional[Any] = None,
        attention_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
        use_cache: bool = True,
        **kwargs,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        # Wenn der Latent-KV-Cache bereits gefüllt ist, übergebe nur das neueste Token.
        # transformers 5.x Cache-Objekte erlauben kein Indexieren — Länge via get_seq_length().
        if past_key_values is not None:
            cache_len = (
                past_key_values.get_seq_length()
                if isinstance(past_key_values, Cache)
                else len(past_key_values)
            )
            if cache_len > 0:
                input_ids = input_ids[:, -1:]
        model_inputs = {
            "input_ids": input_ids,
            "past_key_values": past_key_values,
            "attention_mask": attention_mask,
            "use_cache": use_cache,
        }
        # Multimodale Eingaben durchreichen: im Decode-State nur der letzte
        # Token mit Modalitätsmarkierung (dort im Regelfall rein Text →
        # Vision-/Audio-Tensoren weglassen); die volle kv_modality_mask bleibt
        # ungeschnitten, damit die 2D-Vision-RoPE-Koordinaten der gecachten
        # Patches rekonstruiert werden können.
        modality_mask = kwargs.get("modality_mask")
        if modality_mask is not None:
            if cache_len > 0:
                n = input_ids.shape[1]
                if modality_mask.shape[1] >= n:
                    step_mask = modality_mask[:, -n:]
                else:
                    step_mask = torch.zeros((input_ids.shape[0], 1), dtype=modality_mask.dtype, device=modality_mask.device)
                model_inputs["modality_mask"] = step_mask
                if bool(step_mask.eq(1).any()) and kwargs.get("vision_patches") is not None:
                    model_inputs["vision_patches"] = kwargs["vision_patches"][..., -n:, :]
                if bool(step_mask.eq(2).any()) and kwargs.get("audio_frames") is not None:
                    model_inputs["audio_frames"] = kwargs["audio_frames"][..., -n:, :]
            else:
                model_inputs["modality_mask"] = modality_mask
                if kwargs.get("vision_patches") is not None:
                    model_inputs["vision_patches"] = kwargs["vision_patches"]
                if kwargs.get("audio_frames") is not None:
                    model_inputs["audio_frames"] = kwargs["audio_frames"]
        if kwargs.get("kv_modality_mask") is not None:
            model_inputs["kv_modality_mask"] = kwargs["kv_modality_mask"]
        return model_inputs

    def forward(
        self,
        input_ids: torch.Tensor,
        modality_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
        vision_patches: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
        audio_frames: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
        attention_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
        past_key_values: Optional[List[Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]]] = None,
        use_cache: Optional[bool] = None,
        labels: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
        router_entropy_weight: float = 0.0,
        router_target_entropy: Optional[float] = None,
        kv_modality_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
        **kwargs,
    ) -> CausalLMOutputWithPast:
        use_cache = use_cache if use_cache is not None else False
        hidden_states, all_entropies, next_cache = self.model(
            input_ids=input_ids,
            modality_mask=modality_mask,
            vision_patches=vision_patches,
            audio_frames=audio_frames,
            attention_mask=attention_mask,
            past_key_values=past_key_values,
            use_cache=use_cache,
            kv_modality_mask=kv_modality_mask,
        )

        logits = self.lm_head(hidden_states)

        if self.final_softcap is not None:
            logits = self.final_softcap * torch.tanh(logits / self.final_softcap)

        loss = None
        if labels is not None:
            shift_logits = logits[..., :-1, :].contiguous()
            shift_labels = labels[..., 1:].contiguous()
            loss_fct = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()
            loss = loss_fct(shift_logits.view(-1, self.config.vocab_size), shift_labels.view(-1))

        if all_entropies and router_entropy_weight != 0.0:
            stacked_entropies = torch.stack(list(all_entropies), dim=0).float()
            n_layers = stacked_entropies.shape[0]
            layer_pos = torch.arange(n_layers, device=stacked_entropies.device, dtype=stacked_entropies.dtype)
            center = float(getattr(self.config, "router_depth_peak", 0.55)) * (n_layers - 1)
            sigma = max(float(getattr(self.config, "router_depth_sigma", 0.30)) * (n_layers - 1), 1e-6)
            layer_weights = torch.exp(-0.5 * ((layer_pos - center) / sigma) ** 2)
            layer_weights = (layer_weights / layer_weights.sum().clamp(min=1e-9)).view(n_layers, 1, 1)

            if labels is not None:
                bsz, seq_len = labels.shape
                valid_mask = torch.zeros((bsz, seq_len), device=stacked_entropies.device, dtype=stacked_entropies.dtype)
                valid_mask[:, :-1] = (labels[:, 1:] != -100).to(stacked_entropies.dtype)
                weighted_mask = valid_mask.unsqueeze(0).expand_as(stacked_entropies) * layer_weights
                mean_entropy = (stacked_entropies * weighted_mask).sum() / weighted_mask.sum().clamp(min=1e-9)
            else:
                mean_entropy = (stacked_entropies * layer_weights).mean()

            target = (
                router_target_entropy
                if router_target_entropy is not None
                else float(getattr(self.config, "router_entropy_target", 1.0))
            )
            reg_loss = router_entropy_weight * (mean_entropy - target) ** 2
            loss = reg_loss if loss is None else loss + reg_loss

        output = CausalLMOutputWithPast(
            loss=loss,
            logits=logits,
            past_key_values=next_cache,
            hidden_states=None,
            attentions=None,
        )
        output.router_entropies = all_entropies
        return output


ArgonForCausalLM = ArgonForConditionalGeneration

### FILE: moe_layers.py
### bytes: 6329   lines: 140

"""
moe_layers.py
=============
Sparsely-Gated Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) mit GeGLU-Experten und
Entropie-adaptivem Sigmoid-Routing für die Argon-Architektur.
"""

from typing import Tuple, Optional
import math

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F


class ArgonGeGLUExpert(nn.Module):
    """
    Feed-Forward-Experte mit GeGLU-Aktivierung (tanh-Approximation).
    """
    def __init__(self, hidden_size: int, intermediate_size: int):
        super().__init__()
        self.hidden_size = hidden_size
        self.intermediate_size = intermediate_size
        self.gate_proj = nn.Linear(hidden_size, intermediate_size, bias=False)
        self.up_proj = nn.Linear(hidden_size, intermediate_size, bias=False)
        self.down_proj = nn.Linear(intermediate_size, hidden_size, bias=False)

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        return self.down_proj(F.gelu(self.gate_proj(x), approximate="tanh") * self.up_proj(x))


class ArgonEntropyAdaptiveRouter(nn.Module):
    """
    Entropie-adaptives Sigmoid-Routing:
    1. Berechnet unabhängige Sigmoid-Affinitäten statt konkurrierendem Softmax.
    2. Aux-loss-freies Load-Balancing über additiven expert_bias.
    3. Misst die Shannon-Entropie H der Router-Verteilung pro Token:
       - Bei niedriger Entropie (Routine-Sequenzen) werden sekundäre Expertenränge gedämpft
         und die Gesamt-Routed-Amplitude auf 25% skaliert (Shared Expert dominiert).
       - Bei hoher Entropie (komplexe Mehrdeutigkeit) öffnen die Spezialisten bis 100%.
    """
    inline_balancing_enabled: bool = True

    def __init__(self, config):
        super().__init__()
        self.hidden_size = getattr(config, "hidden_size", 1152)
        self.n_routed_experts = getattr(config, "n_routed_experts", 8)
        self.base_k = min(getattr(config, "num_experts_per_tok", 2), self.n_routed_experts)
        self.entropy_threshold = getattr(config, "router_entropy_threshold", 1.8)

        self.weight = nn.Parameter(torch.empty((self.n_routed_experts, self.hidden_size)))
        nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.weight, a=5**0.5)

        self.register_buffer("expert_bias", torch.zeros(self.n_routed_experts))

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        logits = F.linear(x, self.weight)
        scores = torch.sigmoid(logits)

        # Shannon-Entropie der normalisierten Affinitäten
        probs = scores / (scores.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True) + 1e-9)
        router_entropy = -torch.sum(probs * torch.log(probs + 1e-9), dim=-1)

        # Top-K Auswahl über bias-korrigierte Scores
        biased_scores = scores + self.expert_bias.unsqueeze(0)
        _, top_indices = torch.topk(biased_scores, k=self.base_k, dim=-1, sorted=False)

        # Aux-loss-freies Load-Balancing im Training (Gradient-Checkpointing-sicher).
        # last_counts bleibt der Snapshot für die ausgelagerte Variante
        # (inline_balancing_enabled = False -> Update nach optimizer.step()).
        if self.training and torch.is_grad_enabled():
            with torch.no_grad():
                counts = torch.bincount(top_indices.reshape(-1), minlength=self.n_routed_experts).float()
                self.last_counts = counts.detach().clone()
                if self.inline_balancing_enabled:
                    avg_count = counts.mean()
                    self.expert_bias.add_(0.001 * torch.sign(avg_count - counts))

        top_weights = torch.gather(scores, dim=-1, index=top_indices)

        # Sekundäre Experten dämpfen, wenn Router-Entropie unter dem Schwellenwert liegt
        if self.base_k > 1:
            low_entropy_mask = (router_entropy < self.entropy_threshold).unsqueeze(-1)
            rank_mask = torch.arange(self.base_k, device=x.device).unsqueeze(0) >= 1
            top_weights = torch.where(low_entropy_mask & rank_mask, top_weights * 0.15, top_weights)

        top_weights = top_weights / (top_weights.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True) + 1e-9)

        # Kapazitäts-Skalierung der Routed-Amplitude relativ zum Shared Expert
        max_entropy = math.log(self.n_routed_experts)
        capacity_gate = (router_entropy / max_entropy).clamp(min=0.25, max=1.0).unsqueeze(-1)
        top_weights = top_weights * capacity_gate
        return top_indices, top_weights, router_entropy


class ArgonMoELayer(nn.Module):
    """
    MoE-Layer mit persistenten Shared Experts und spärlich aktivierten Routed Experts.
    """
    def __init__(self, config):
        super().__init__()
        self.hidden_size = getattr(config, "hidden_size", 1152)
        self.moe_inter_dim = getattr(config, "moe_inter_dim", 1728)
        self.shared_inter_dim = getattr(config, "shared_intermediate_size", getattr(config, "intermediate_size", 6912))
        self.n_shared_experts = getattr(config, "n_shared_experts", 1)
        self.n_routed_experts = getattr(config, "n_routed_experts", 8)

        self.shared_experts = nn.ModuleList([
            ArgonGeGLUExpert(self.hidden_size, self.shared_inter_dim)
            for _ in range(self.n_shared_experts)
        ])

        self.routed_experts = nn.ModuleList([
            ArgonGeGLUExpert(self.hidden_size, self.moe_inter_dim)
            for _ in range(self.n_routed_experts)
        ])

        self.gate = ArgonEntropyAdaptiveRouter(config)

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        orig_shape = x.shape
        x_flat = x.view(-1, orig_shape[-1])

        shared_out = torch.zeros_like(x_flat)
        for expert in self.shared_experts:
            shared_out = shared_out + expert(x_flat)

        top_indices, top_weights, router_entropy = self.gate(x_flat)
        routed_out = torch.zeros_like(x_flat)

        for i, expert in enumerate(self.routed_experts):
            token_mask, k_pos = torch.where(top_indices == i)
            if token_mask.numel() > 0:
                selected_x = x_flat[token_mask]
                expert_out = expert(selected_x)
                weights = top_weights[token_mask, k_pos].unsqueeze(-1)
                routed_out.index_add_(0, token_mask, expert_out * weights)

        total_out = (shared_out + routed_out).view(orig_shape)
        entropy_reshaped = router_entropy.view(orig_shape[0], orig_shape[1])
        return total_out, entropy_reshaped
